# RETRO_FIXES — settle the live findings of the 2026-09 retro audits

Source: the retroactive split audits (user rule 2026-09-12) of all 65 landed sub-steps of
`R4133_PROPS_PLAN` (26) and `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN` (39) — 1010 findings, archived under the
local-only `investigations/retro-audits-2026-09-13/`. A fable triage against `update`
`ab9f0f76` left **804 LIVE** findings (36 major, 301 minor, 467 note; 202 duplicates, 3 already
fixed, 1 invalid). This plan fixes all of them in **137 steps** (`RF-Dxx-nn`). It adds no new
product scope: the HOLD on GOLDEN_REBASE G1.11′ / WP-G3 stays until the user lifts it.

Working data (gitignored `tmp/retro_fix/`): `live_full.jsonl` (every finding: claim, evidence,
report path), `triage/*.json` (status, fix sketch), `wp_index.json` (the machine index of this
plan — steps, uids, files, waves), `rulings.md`, `notes/` (doc follow-ups left by steps).

## 0. Binding rules

CLAUDE.md is authoritative: source-integrity gate first (`.inputs/dss_capi`,
`.inputs/electricdss-tst`, `.inputs/electricdss-code-r4133-trunk` present and non-empty, else
STOP); `(Get-Command cargo).Source` under `.cargo\bin`; r4133 is the behavioural authority and
upstream bugs are never reproduced in any lane; every deliberate divergence is excluded
field-by-field and pinned by an expected-value test; tolerances are never loosened; no
`#[ignore]`, no filter that greens on zero matches; commit messages and records stay short.
Coordinator rulings R1–R12 (§5) are part of this plan.

A finding is closed only by evidence: the fix, or a recorded reason why it is not fixed
(never silently dropped). A finding the executor proves wrong at HEAD is recorded as
"invalid: <proof>". Line numbers in findings have drifted — always re-locate by symbol/phrase.

## 1. Step 0 — land G1.10c

Before wave 1: land the finished, audited `lane-e` (`0240372a`, GOLDEN_REBASE G1.10c) into
`update` (ff/merge, full gate on the merge result, push). Every RF step branches from the
`update` that contains it.

## 2. Per-step ritual (every RF step, in order — GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN §"Per-sub-step ritual")

0. **Tier check** — agents are spawned with the explicit model/effort of §3, never inherited.
1. **Implement** — one fresh executor per micro-part (`parts` in the step header; parts run
   sequentially in the same lane worktree, each hands over through
   `tmp/retro_fix/state/<step>.md`). Scope = exactly the step's uids and `files`. Shared
   documents (§4) are NOT edited by code/test steps: the executor appends the needed doc change
   to `tmp/retro_fix/notes/<doc-slug>.md` (`### <step> / <uid>` + the exact text to change).
2. **Gate green** — the seven commands of CLAUDE.md "## Gate" (fmt, clippy ×2,
   `cargo nextest run --workspace` in both lanes, `cargo test --workspace --doc` in both lanes:
   RF-I00-01 replaced the five-command set of c67782d2); plus
   `pwsh -File tools/lanes/lane_diff.ps1` when a compat kernel, a lane alias or the solver is
   touched. Steps whose diff is documentation only (`needs_gate: no`) run `cargo fmt --all
   --check` plus, in both lanes, `cargo test -p dss-core --test oracle_parity_cfg_gate` (the
   citation rails), `--test reliability_pins` (reads `TESTING.md` / `TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` /
   `golden-rebase.md`) and `--test props_r4133_replay` (reads the rp3 record and the props
   README) instead of the full gate; a diff that touches any `.rs`/`.json`/`.dss`/`.py` file
   runs the full gate regardless of the flag. Commands 4 and 5 (the two nextest runs) run with
   `DSS_ORACLE_TIMEOUT_SECS=600` (the CI value of `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; it lengthens only
   the per-request oracle deadline, never the comparison — user decision 2026-09-26, after the
   120 s default produced 1–4 infra-red re-runs per step while six lanes gated at once).
3. **Record + commit** — the step's 5–10 line record goes to
   `docs/phase-records/retro-fixes.md` (owned by this plan; appended by the settler at the end
   of the step, so parallel lanes never collide in it); `STATUS.md` is synced once per wave by
   the coordinator.
4. **audit-code + audit-tests** — fresh, independent, read-only agents on the step's commit
   range. **Split rule (user 2026-09-12): if the step diff exceeds 1500 changed lines, each
   role is split over `ceil(diff_lines / 2000)` agents, each scoped to a disjoint file slice
   of the diff**; every slice agent also sees the step brief. Findings are verified LIVE
   against the lane HEAD before they are reported.
5. **Settle** — a fresh settler settles every finding against evidence (r4133 source, live
   probe), fixes what is real, commits, writes the record, then re-runs **the gate whose kind
   matches the settlement diff** (user decision 2026-09-26): `git diff --stat <step commit>..HEAD`
   touching any `.rs`/`.toml`/`.json`/`.dss`/`.py`/`Cargo.lock` file → the full gate of stage 2;
   documentation only → the docs gate of stage 2; in doubt → full. `settle.md` names the kind and
   the diff --stat list. Audits clean → no empty commit, record "audits clean".
6. **Settlement audit** — a fresh agent audits the settlement commit(s) (same split rule). A
   confirmed finding goes back to a second settle round (max 2 rounds; leftovers are reported
   to the coordinator, never dropped). Round 2 gates by the same diff rule as stage 5, over its
   own commits (`<settle sha>..HEAD`).
7. **Land** — the coordinator merges the lane into `update` (ff where possible), runs the
   full gate on the merge result once per wave, pushes `origin/update` immediately.

Worktrees are removed only by the junction-safe PowerShell procedure of CLAUDE.md.

## 3. Agent tiers (written into every step header)

| role | model / effort |
|---|---|
| executor, `high` step | opus / high — S doc, citation, comment work |
| executor, `xhigh` step | opus / xhigh — M test/code work, anything moving ledger / pins / goldens |
| executor, `max` step | opus / max — L items, major-severity engine changes |
| audit-code, audit-tests | opus / xhigh for `high` steps; **opus / max for `xhigh` and `max` steps** (user rule 2026-09-25: fable replaced by opus max for cost - a fresh agent per role, never the executor) |
| settler | opus / max (a fresh agent - neither the executor nor an auditor) |
| settlement audit | opus / xhigh (a fresh agent, never the settler) |
| coordinator merge / gate | opus / high |

Budget per agent: ≤ 200–300k tokens of context. A step that cannot fit is split into `parts`;
an executor that sees it will not fit stops after a clean commit and writes the remainder to
the state file — the workflow spawns the next part. Agents never paste whole files or whole
reports into messages; long outputs are written to files in chunks.

## 4. Shared documents and scheduling

Shared documents — edited only by their doc-owner steps, which run after every step that left
a note for them: `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, `STATUS.md`, `TESTING.md`,
`docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md`, `…-rp2.md`,
`…-rp3.md`, `docs/plans-archive/R4133_PROPS_PLAN.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`,
`tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`, `tests/corpus/props_r4133/README.md`.

**Note-consumption rule (every doc-owner step; promoted here from RF-D05-01 after review round
c2):** a `notes/` entry is applied by the FIRST owner step of its document that runs after the
entry's author landed, and is then marked in the notes file, directly under its
`### <step> / <uid>` header, with `<!-- applied by RF-Dxx-nn <commit> -->`. An owner applies
only unmarked entries whose author has landed and leaves the rest untouched; the LAST owner of a
document asserts that its notes file holds no unmarked entry. The record lists the entries
applied, by `<step> / <uid>`.

**Line-citation counts (R10):** the exact per-document count of resolvable `file:LINE` citations
lives in the document itself as `<!-- line-citations: N -->` (RF-D07-01); an owner whose edit
moves the count updates the marker in the same commit and never keeps or drops a citation to
hold the number.

Steps run in **waves of up to 3 parallel lanes**. Within a wave the steps' `files` sets are
pairwise disjoint; a step starts only after every step in its `after` list has landed. The
schedule (§6) is computed from `wp_index.json` by `schedule.py`, never edited by hand.

**Usage guard (user 2026-09-18):** the execution workflow receives a token allowance; before
each wave it compares the ledger `tmp/retro_fix/usage_ledger.json` with the cap and, when
reached, starts no new wave, lets in-flight agents finish and stops resumably.

## 5. Coordinator rulings

Derived from binding project policy (CLAUDE.md); the user may overrule any of them.

- **R1 — gitignored `investigations/` files.** Executors run in a worktree that does not contain
  `investigations/`. Where a finding asks to correct or author such a file, the executor edits
  it in the MAIN checkout by absolute path (`E:/RustProject/dss-rs/investigations/...`). Nothing
  is committed; the step record names the file and says "local-only". Never delete or move
  anything there. New upstream reports take the next free number in `to_opendss/`.
- **R2 — CLAUDE.md.** Factual syncs only (a stale status sentence, a renamed test, a bug note
  that the fix made wrong). Minimal edits, no restructuring, listed explicitly in the record.
- **R3 — upstream bug vs "r4133 does it".** Policy 2026-08-02 decides: a proven r4133 bug is
  never reproduced. Fix in both lanes, exclude the divergent cells field-by-field, pin by an
  expected-value test, add the DIVERGENCES.md row with r4133 evidence. Applies to D00-06
  (swing-source sum), D02-12 (AutoTrans `sub=yes` substation losses), D01-10 (Show Isolated
  second `ReProcessBusDefs` that drops RelCalc accumulators: record + pin, do not adopt).
- **R4 — D01-01** keeps the `min(6, NPhases)` render clip; record it (DIVERGENCES row + pin).
- **R5 — D01-02** tmp/rp37 transcripts stay local-only; the record gets one sentence plus the
  epri-worker re-derivation recipe.
- **R6 — D08-10** keeps the port default 0.0 for `%pminnovars` / `%pminkvarmax`; behaviour is
  proven identical, so this is a DIVERGENCES.md entry plus a pin, not an engine change.
- **R7 — D05-08** reuses the RP3.11 evidence; re-probe the r4133 DLL only where a transcript is
  missing.
- **R8 — a fix that would need a tolerance loosened, a golden regenerated outside the documented
  procedure, or a ledger entry without an expected-value pin is NOT done**: the executor stops
  that finding, records why, and the settler reports it to the coordinator.
- **R9 — in-step edits to shared documents (2026-09-20, after review round c1).** A step whose
  code change *forces* a shared-document edit — a widened citation rail that reds on a digits-only
  file:LINE re-point (RF-D07-01, RF-D07-02, RF-D07-06) or a pin registry that asserts its own
  citation in a record (RF-D01-06 → `r4133-props-rp3.md`) — makes that minimal edit in-step. The
  documents are listed in the step's `files`, so the scheduler keeps it out of any wave that holds
  the doc-owner; it is NOT a doc-owner itself (index flag `inline_shared`) and still appends the
  corresponding `notes/` entry so the owner sees what moved. Everything beyond the forced lines
  stays with the owner steps of §4.
- **R10 — exact line-citation counts live in the document (2026-09-20, after review round c2).**
  RF-D07-01 SA-2 makes the `LINE_CITED_DOCS` counts exact via a `<!-- line-citations: N -->`
  marker parsed and asserted by the rail (option (b) of c2-3-2); every later owner of
  `TESTING.md` / `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` (and of any document RF-D07-02 adds to the rail)
  updates the marker in the same commit as the edit that moves the count. The cfg gate stays
  listed in those owners' `files` only as a scheduling guard (they may re-measure a unit test
  there); it is not a required edit. The note-consumption rule of RF-D05-01 is promoted to §4.
- **R11 — the D35(3) STOP-on-recurrence clause is reinstated (2026-09-20, after review round h1).**
  A corpus-gate red of the shape `"You must create a new circuit object first"` / compiled-to-no-
  circuit on either oracle transport (the G1.10a espvlcontrol one-off) is never retried into green
  and never filtered: the step STOPs with the evidence (deck, transport, `st_size`, the runner
  line) and the settler reports it to the coordinator. RF-D09-06 SA-0 ships the transport-side
  named error that makes the evidence diagnosable; after it lands a recurrence is triaged by a
  follow-up step under R12. The G1.10a record keeps its "retired without a ruling" sentence
  plus a pointer to this ruling (RF-D09-06's §4 notes).
- **R12 — follow-up steps created at execution time (2026-09-20).** Work that a step is told to
  measure and hand over rather than do — the SysCtx `tstart`/`tstop`/`intHour` carry-over of
  RF-D00-11 (5 production files + 19 test files), the unowned `.rs`-comment anchors listed by
  RF-D06-10, the port-side Save round-trip failures RF-D03-07 records as
  `off:save-roundtrip-port-bug`, the leftover property-level reds of RF-D01-05, the exclusion
  entries RF-D08-03 cannot narrow — is never parked to a later plan (port-gaps-immediately). The
  coordinator creates one owned step per hand-over, `RF-Dxx-nn` with the next free number of the
  origin's domain, with the origin's state file as its brief, added to `wp_index.json` and
  scheduled by the driver after the origin lands; the origin's record names it. Such steps join
  this plan's ledger and audit ritual unchanged.

## 7. Steps

One section per step, grouped by domain part; the `<!-- RF-STEP {...} -->` header of each section is the machine-readable contract (effort, parts, gate, oracle, after, n_uids) the execution workflow reads.

### RF-D00-01 — Recompute Newton Iterminal at the converged NodeV for every reader (Export/Show/Summary/monitors/meters)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-01", "effort": "max", "parts": 3, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 3} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -; plus `lane_diff.ps1` (§2.2).
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/compat.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/traits.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/newton.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/report/export/currents.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/report/show/bus_powers.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/report/show/currents.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/report/show/powers.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/solution/solution/power_flow.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/lane.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`, `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, `tests/corpus/modes/manifest.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `CLAUDE.md` (R2: factual sync of the Newton bug note only), `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`

Background (one defect, three faces): `do_newton_solution` (`solution/solution/power_flow.rs`) stamps every element's `Iterminal` at `NodeV_{n-1}`, then applies the final `NodeV -= dV` and never invalidates the stamp. `CktElement::compute_iterminal` (`elements/traits.rs`) is `SolutionCount`-gated (`iterminal_solved_for` / `mark_iterminal_solved` in `elements/ckt.rs`), so every cache-aware reader returns the one-step-stale current. G2.3 repaired exactly one reader (`exec/view.rs::snapshot_elements`, via `refresh_iterminal`). r4133 carries the same staleness on its cache-aware paths (`Common/CktElement.pas:524-533` `Losses`, `PDElem.Power[j]`) — an upstream bug, never reproduced; but r4133's `ExportCurrents` / `ShowCurrents` / `ShowPowers` call `GetCurrents` unconditionally, so on those three surfaces the port today matches neither oracle.

**Findings**
- `G|G2.3|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `solution/solution/power_flow.rs::do_newton_solution`, `elements/traits.rs::{compute_iterminal, terminal_power, losses}`: after `Set algorithm=Newton` every reader except `snapshot_elements` (Export Powers/Losses/Summary/P_ByPhase/Capacity/Overloads, Show Powers/BusPowers, `solution/meters/demand_interval.rs`, `elements/meter/monitor/sample.rs`) reports the stale current, so CLAUDE.md upstream bug 5 IS reproduced in both lanes. Fix at the root: at the end of `do_newton_solution`, after the final `NodeV -= dV`, invalidate every element's Iterminal stamp (or re-stamp once at the converged NodeV) so all cache-aware readers see the converged current; then drop the now-redundant special case in `snapshot_elements`. Correct the claims that say "not reproduced" on the basis of one reader (`compat.rs` Newton row, `harness/lane.rs` `LANE_SKIP_ELEM_POWERS` doc, the `newton.dss` note in `tests/corpus/modes/manifest.json`, the cfg-gate register row) and leave §4 notes for CLAUDE.md / TESTING.md / the G2.3 record.
- `G|G2.3|AT1|AT-1` (major) - `exec/tests/newton.rs`: the suite verifies the teardown on the snapshot path only, and `newton_dispatch_leaves_a_valid_but_stale_iterminal_cache` pins the stale cache (7.378e-2 A on `Line.l1`) as the Newton-dispatch signal. Rewrite that tripwire onto another Newton-only signal (e.g. the iteration count / the Newton dispatch itself) and turn the cache leg into a pin that the cache is NOT stale after a Newton solve; add report-layer pins: `Export Powers`, `Export Losses`, `Export Summary` and `Show Powers` under `algorithm=Newton` equal `algorithm=Normal` on the pin deck (`modes/newton/newton.dss` body), each asserting `S == V·conj(I)` within 1e-8 kVA, in both lanes.
- `G|G2.3|AC1|AC-2` (major) - `report/export/currents.rs` (module doc equating upstream's mutating `GetCurrents` with `compute_iterminal`), `report/show/currents.rs`, `report/show/powers.rs`, `report/show/bus_powers.rs`: these surfaces model r4133's unconditional scratch-buffer `GetCurrents` (r4133 `Common/ExportResults.pas:563-594`, `Common/ShowResults.pas:572,594,616` ShowCurrents and `:785,844,912` ShowPowers; dss_capi `ExportResults.pas:660`) as the cache-aware call — a pure port bug after Newton. The root fix makes them equal; in this item fix the `export/currents.rs` doc with those citations and add the pin that `Export Currents` and `Show Currents` under Newton equal Normal on the pin deck (the finding quotes `Vsource.SOURCE` I1 = 82.7513 A). If the executor finds any reader where invalidation alone is not enough, switch that reader to `refresh_iterminal` and say so in the record.

**Parts:**
1. Engine: the invalidation/re-stamp in `do_newton_solution` (+ `traits.rs` cache flag if needed), `view.rs::snapshot_elements` simplified, the dispatch tripwire rewritten. Commits; hands over the chosen mechanism and the new Newton-only signal in the state file.
2. Report readers: the expected-value pins in `exec/tests/newton.rs` for Export Powers/Losses/Summary/Currents and Show Powers/Currents (both lanes), the `export/currents.rs` doc fix with the r4133 citations. Commits; hands over the list of surfaces pinned.
3. Harness: `lane.rs` / `harness/mod.rs` exclusion docs, `oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs` census row, `tests/corpus/modes/manifest.json` note, `compat.rs` row, the §4 notes; full gate + `lane_diff`; triage of any corpus cell that moved - a ledger-scoped cell (monitor / meter / summary) gets its `tests/corpus/ledger.json` entry here (in Files for this part only), each with its expected-value pin in `newton.rs`; a lane-scoped one goes to `lane.rs`.

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 and the pinned dss-python on `tests/corpus/modes/newton/newton.dss` and `newton_feeder.dss`: read the monitor/meter/summary cells the gate compares after the Newton solve. Decides whether the fix moves any gated cell away from the (stale) oracle value: a cell that moves gets a field-level exclusion (ledger/lane) plus an expected-value pin naming both numbers (R8: no exclusion without a pin); a cell that does not move needs nothing. Iteration counts and discrete state must stay exact.

**Acceptance:**
- The new report-layer pins and the rewritten tripwire exist and pass in both lanes; `newton_powers_match_the_normal_algorithm` and `newton_total_powers_match_the_normal_algorithm` still pass.
- No golden byte moves; `corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines` green on both channels with every ledger entry hit; every new exclusion has a pin.
- `lane_diff` re-measured and its result recorded (expected `max |Δ| = 0`).
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D00-02 — Fix CIM grounded/SpacingSpecified export bugs and the CIM citation/test notes
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-02", "effort": "max", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D00-01"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-01.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/cim/export.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/compat.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_cim.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`

**Findings**
- `G|G2.1g|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `cim/export.rs`, the wye-load `EnergyConsumer.grounded` writer (`snap.neutral_node == 0`, with the "transformer writer's NodeRef[j2]=0 test" comment): it ports only one rung of the ladder it cites, ignoring the Load's own `Rneut`/`Xneut`, so a wye load with its neutral on a non-ground node and `rneut=0 xneut=0` (solidly grounded) exports `grounded=false`. Port the rest of the ladder (r4133 `Common/ExportCIMXML.pas:1241-1256`; Load `Rneut := -1.0` = open neutral, r4133 `PCElements/Load.pas:317-318,804`): snapshot `load.rneut` next to `neutral_node`, `grounded = neutral_node == 0 || rneut >= 0.0`. Pin in `golden_cim.rs` (`bus1=b.1.2.3.4` + `rneut=0 xneut=0` -> true; `rneut=-1` -> false; `.0` neutral -> true), fix the site comment, leave a §4 note for the G2.1g record. Both oracles write TRUE unconditionally and the G2.1g exclusion already covers the node, so no oracle compare moves. Per R1 add the same Rneut clause to `E:/RustProject/dss-rs/investigations/to_opendss/23-cim-grounded-always-true.md` (main checkout, local-only, named in the record).
- `RP|RP3.6|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `cim/export.rs`, ACLineSegment writer: `let spacing_specified = line.line_spacing_obj.is_some() && !line.line_wire_data.is_empty();` derives the flag from objects while the sibling `has_line_code` already reads `line.line_code_specified`; after `Edit linecode=` / `switch=` drops the flag but keeps the objects (Line.pas:663/:696) the export takes the spacing branch, losing `ACLineSegment.r/x/bch/gch/r0/x0/b0ch` and writing a scaled `Conductor.length` instead of r4133's 1.0. Read `line.spacing_specified` (r4133 `Common/ExportCIMXML.pas:3742` `else if SpacingSpecified`), keeping object lookups only for data access under the flag; in the same pass check the sibling `has_geometry = geometry_obj.is_some()` against r4133 `GeometrySpecified` and fix it the same way if it differs. Pin in `golden_cim.rs` with the audit deck (`spacing=sp1 wires=[w1 w1 w1] length=1 units=kft`, then `Edit linecode=lc1`, `Edit r1=0.5`, `Export cim100`): `Conductor.length` = 1, `ACLineSegment.r/x/r0/x0` present, no WireSpacingInfo reference; plus the converse (flag set -> spacing branch). §4 note for the RP3.6 record.
- `G|G2.2c|AC1|AC-1` (note) - five sites state the delta-vs-wye `ShuntCompensator.grounded` arms are "six lines" apart: `cim/export.rs`, `compat.rs` (CIM attribute-name row), `golden_cim.rs` (header doc), `oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs` (register row), and the G2.2c record (§4 note). True for dss_capi (`ExportCIMXML.pas:3700/:3706`) but r4133 is four lines (`:3183/:3187`). At each site qualify the distance ("six lines in capi :3700/:3706; four in r4133 :3183/:3187") or drop it and keep the two line pairs.
- `G|G2.1g|AC1|AC-3` (note) - `cim/export.rs`, the `nterms < 2` unreachability comment cites `elements/pd/capacitor/accessors.rs:210-211` as "conn=wye forces Nterms := 2"; at HEAD those lines are the delta arm (`set_nterms(1)`), the wye arm is the `_ => // Wye` arm below it. Cite by symbol (the wye arm of the capacitor `conn` setter), not by line; §4 note for the same citation in the record.
- `G|G2.1g|AC1|AC-4` (note) - record citations `golden_cim.rs:476` / `:526` no longer land (§4 note: replace with symbol anchors - the `only_grounded(&probe, "ShuntCompensator")` and `only_grounded(&mixed, "ShuntCompensator")` assertions); and the `cim/export.rs` wye-load comment cites `Load.pas:479-492` (capi only). In code: pair it with the r4133 twin `PCElements/Load.pas:489-503` (`0: NConds := Fnphases +1;`).
- `G|G2.1g|AT1|AT-3` (note) - `golden_cim.rs`, the three scratch dirs built as `std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dss_cim_probe_{tag}"))` (and the two older siblings of the same pattern) with `remove_dir_all` on entry: a fixed `%TEMP%` path shared by both lanes and every worktree, so concurrent gate runs can delete each other's export (spurious red only). Make each unique per process and lane (append `std::process::id()` and a `cfg!(feature = "oracle-parity")` tag) and remove it on exit.
- `RP|RP3.6|AT1|AT-3` (note) - `golden_cim.rs::cim_linecode_units_backfill_matches_the_condcode_string`: the `lckft` "invariance control" expects 0.00098753281, identical to the back-filled `lckill`/`lcsw`/`lcgeo` legs, so a rule that back-fills every code leaves it green; only `lcnoline` (0.301) discriminates. This step has no oracle budget: take the minimal fix - correct the doc to say only `lcnoline` discriminates - unless the executor can obtain the r4133 literal for a discriminating variant (linecode `units=mi` on the `units=kft` line) from one epri-worker `export cim100` run; never take the literal from the port.

**Acceptance:**
- New pins in `golden_cim.rs` for the wye-load `grounded` ladder (three cases) and for the `spacing_specified` flag (both directions) pass in both lanes.
- No golden byte moves; ledger unchanged; the CIM goldens and `oracle_parity_cfg_gate` citation rails stay green.
- The scratch dirs are per-process/per-lane and cleaned up.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason (investigations/23 edit named "local-only").

### RF-D00-03 — Pin the Newton exclusions (aggregates, phase_losses, newton_feeder) and repair stale citations around them
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-03", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D00-01", "RF-D00-02"], "n_uids": 15} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-01, RF-D00-02.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/compat.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/meter/monitor/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/derived_totals.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/newton.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/aggregates.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/lane.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-epri/src/dss.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `CLAUDE.md` (R2), `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`

Start from the `exec/tests/newton.rs` that RF-D00-01 left (its tripwire was rewritten there); the new pins go next to `newton_powers_match_the_normal_algorithm`. The exclusion list is `harness/lane.rs::LANE_SKIP_ELEM_POWERS` = `modes:newton/newton.dss`, `modes:newton/newton_feeder.dss`. Navigation: locate every site with `rg` (the strings and symbols named in each bullet) and open only the surrounding lines - never read `tests/harness/mod.rs` (19k lines), `oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs` (6k) or `exec/view.rs` (5k) in full. No oracle probe is run in this step (`oracle: false`).

**Findings** (pins first, then citations and docs)
- `G|G2.3|AT1|AT-4` (minor) - `harness/aggregates.rs::compare_aggregates`, value arms `if channels.losses` / `if channels.powers`: `lane::elem_channels_for` returns currents-only for the two newton decks, so Circuit.Losses, LineLosses, SubstationLosses, AllElementLosses and TotalPower are neither oracle-compared nor pinned there, and the survivor enumerations do not say so. Add `newton_circuit_aggregates_match_the_normal_algorithm` to `exec/tests/newton.rs` (the five aggregates after Newton equal the normal algorithm's to 1e-8 kVA); extend the `lane.rs` `LANE_SKIP_ELEM_POWERS` doc with its LANE-EXCLUSION marker and the `oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs` register row to name the five aggregates; §4 note for the CLAUDE.md Newton bullet ("Everything else about those decks ...").
- `G|G2.3|AT1|AT-6` (note) - `exec/tests/newton.rs::newton_powers_match_the_normal_algorithm`: the anti-vacuity guard `assert!(!worst_s_at.is_empty(), "no element was compared")` really tests "some power differed", so bit-exact agreement reds it with the wrong message; and `DECK` is a hand copy of the corpus deck. Replace the guard with a `compared` counter (the shape `newton_total_powers_match_the_normal_algorithm` already uses) and tie `DECK` to `tests/corpus/modes/newton/newton.dss` mechanically (read the corpus file via `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR` and compare the element lines, or load the file outright).
- `G|G1.3d(ii)|SA1|SA-3` (minor) - `harness/lane.rs`, the test asserting `!ch.powers && !ch.losses && !ch.phase_losses` for the newton list: the `phase_losses` channel is dropped on both decks in both lanes with no pin. Add `newton_phase_losses_match_the_normal_algorithm` (per-element PhaseLosses, Newton vs normal within 1e-8 kVA, plus sum-over-phases == `loss_w`) and cite it from the `lane.rs` "What still gates them" doc; run it on both models (next bullet).
- `G|G2.3|AC1|AC-3` (minor) - `harness/lane.rs::LANE_SKIP_ELEM_POWERS`: `newton_feeder.dss` is excluded on both channels in both lanes but every pin runs only the inlined 3-bus `DECK`. Parameterize the newton pins (powers, losses, total_powers, phase_losses, aggregates) over a second model loaded from `tests/corpus/modes/newton/newton_feeder.dss` (vendored corpus, never `.inputs`), same 1e-8 kVA band, quoting the stale-oracle figures 5.213217790400988e-3 / 2.1850404626011652 kVA in the message; cite the pin from the `lane.rs` doc.
- `G|G1.3c|AC3|AC3-7` (note) - `harness/lane.rs`: the citation `tools/oracle/oracle_server.py:612-632` ("the gate solves once more after the compile") now lands in the `Meters.Totals` docstring. Re-point it to the `clear` / `Set DefaultBaseFrequency=60` / `Compile` / `for ... range(n_steps): solve` block by function name, not by line. Triage found a further site with the same string in `harness/mod.rs` - fix it too.
- `G|G1.3c|AC2|AC2-2` (note) - `exec/tests/newton.rs` (doc of the total-powers pin): same stale `.py` citation; four sites in all (`newton.rs`, `lane.rs`, `harness/mod.rs`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`). Re-anchor the code sites by symbol. Do NOT extend the docs citation walker in `oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs` (its `ends_with(".rs")` skip) to `.py:LINE` targets here: that rail extension is owned by RF-D07-02 (`G|G1.6(i)|AT3|AT3-4`, later wave) - record "the `.py` rail is owned by RF-D07-02" against this uid.
- `G|G1.3c|AT2|AT2-3` (note) - `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` carries the same stale `.py` citation: shared doc, so leave a §4 note with the exact replacement text (the same symbol anchor as the code sites); the `newton.rs` half is closed by the bullet above.
- `G|G1.3c|AC2|AC2-1` (note) - r4133 `Common/CktElement.pas`: `GetPhasePower` spans `:1041-1073`, `:1071` is the `End` of the enabled branch and the `Else ... CZERO` arm is `:1072`. Fix the six sites: `exec/view.rs` (range -> 1041-1073; the `:1071` arm cite -> `:1072`), `exec/tests/newton.rs` (range), `exec/tests/derived_totals.rs` (range, and the arm cite), `crates/dss-epri/src/dss.rs` (arm cite). ### RF-D00-04 — Give the G1.9 aggregates surface a fail-on-stale witness register and a real Get_Losses band pin
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-04", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D00-03"], "n_uids": 3} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-03.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/aggregates.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`

**Findings**
- `G|G1.9|AT1|AT1-4` (minor) - the G1.9 aggregates surface (`harness/aggregates.rs`, driven from `corpus_gate/runner.rs`) is the only WP-G1 live surface without a fail-on-stale witness register: its census is dated prose in `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`, so Circuit.SubstationLosses and the two boolean solution scalars can decay into a corpus-wide 0-vs-0 compare with the gate green. Add a register in the pattern of the siblings (`FORCED_BUS_POPULATION`, `FORCED_TOPOLOGY_POPULATION` in `corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`; `SEQ_ARM_CENSUS_MEASURED`, `DISTANCE_POPULATION` in `harness/mod.rs`): the live cases with a non-empty SubstationLosses summand list, plus distinct-value census floors for the solution scalars, re-derived during the gate run and asserted both ways (a listed case that stops witnessing reds; an unlisted witness reds). Wire the both-ways assert into the epilogue of `corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines` in `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs` (in Files for that call only), beside the sibling calls `harness::assert_seq_arm_population()` / `scheduler::assert_topology_declines_are_the_pinned_population()`. Measure the population with one full gate run. §4 note: replace the prose census in TOLERANCE_NOTES with a pointer to the register.
- `G|G1.9|AC1|AC1-1` (minor) - `harness/aggregates.rs` module doc ("P1 and P1b never soften - they run on the raw oracle capture on every case"), repeated in TESTING.md and TOLERANCE_NOTES: P1b's fourth sub-arm, the port-vs-oracle AllElementLosses VALUE compare, sits inside `if channels.losses` and reads the ledger-accepted `cap_for(i)`, so it does soften. Correct the module doc (the value sub-arm is a value arm; identity/length/order stay on the raw capture) and hoist the `rust_ael.len() == elements.len()` length assert out of the `if channels.losses` block so the structural half really never softens; §4 notes with the corrected sentence for TESTING.md and TOLERANCE_NOTES.md.
- `G|G1.3d(ii)|AT1|AT1` (minor) - `harness/mod.rs::the_phase_bands_sum_to_at_most_the_get_losses_band`: `phases` and `all` are built from two textually identical comprehensions over `phase_loss_band`, so `assert_eq!(phases, all)` is a tautology and the real Get_Losses envelope is never referenced. Compare the phase-band sum against `aggregates::element_loss_allowance_kw` instead (equal when NConds == NPhases, strictly less with a neutral conductor - test both shapes). Preferably make `aggregates.rs` and `assert_power_close` call one shared `power_slot_band` so the doc's "no second copy" becomes true; any dedup must keep the band arithmetic byte-identical (R8) - otherwise correct the `phase_loss_band` doc instead.

**Acceptance:**
- The new register exists, is asserted both ways inside `corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines`, and the gate is green in both lanes; the measured population is in the record.
- `the_phase_bands_sum_to_at_most_the_get_losses_band` references `element_loss_allowance_kw` and covers a with-neutral case; it would red if `phase_loss_band` diverged from the envelope.
- No tolerance constant, golden byte or ledger entry changes.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D00-05 — Make NCIM re-initialise generator Q when generators are added/enabled after the first solve (index-OOB panic)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-05", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -; plus `lane_diff.ps1` (§2.2).
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/ncim.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/solution/solution/ncim.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/solution/solution/state.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, `STATUS.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `docs/plans-archive/R4133_PROPS_PLAN.md`

**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.13|AT1|AT-1` (major) - `solution/solution/ncim.rs::do_ncim_solution` and its one-shot flag `ckt.solution.ncim_init_gen_q` (`solution/solution/state.rs`: set `true` only at construction, cleared by `do_ncim_solution` after the first solve, never re-armed): both the PQ-generator sizing (`ncim_init_pq_gen`) and the model-3 sizing in `ncim_get_num_generators(.., init_q)` are gated on it, so a generator that is created - or merely enabled - AFTER the first NCIM solve indexes zero-length arrays and aborts the process with "index out of bounds: the len is 0 but the index is 0" (the model-3 and the model-4 generator arms of the NCIM injection code). RP3.13's acceptance criterion "the panic is impossible from any deck" is false and none of the three existing panic pins (`ncim_pq2pv_promotion_does_not_panic_and_closes_kcl`, `ncim_missing_voltage_bases_does_not_panic`, `ncim_below_three_nodes_does_not_panic`) reaches it. r4133 has the same one-shot init (`Solution.pas:648`, `:1157`; `InitPQGen` `:1662-1681`) - a latent upstream defect, never reproduced. Fix: make the generator arrays self-sizing - `do_ncim_solution` re-runs the sizing / `InitPQGen` port whenever the enabled 3-phase generator census differs from the stored array lengths (or the circuit structure changed since the last NCIM solve); index with checked access so no deck can panic. Decide, from `InitPQGen`, what Q a late generator starts from and whether existing generators keep their converged Q (keep it unless r4133's init would reset it on a re-init - state the choice and its citation in the function doc). Pins in `exec/tests/ncim.rs`: `ncim_generator_added_after_first_solve_does_not_panic` (model=3 and model=4) and `ncim_generator_enabled_between_solves_does_not_panic`, each also asserting convergence and KCL closure at the generator bus; plus disable/remove-then-resolve sequences. §4 notes: correct the "panic class closed" claim in the RP3.13 record and the archived plan's acceptance line; ORPHANED_GAPS/STATUS only if an item there names this panic.

**Parts:**
1. Reproduce first: add the failing pins (New Generator / `enabled=yes` after the first NCIM solve) and confirm the OOB panic at HEAD; then the self-sizing fix in `ncim.rs` / `state.rs`. Commits with the pins green; hands over the chosen Q-state rule and the r4133 lines read.
2. The remaining sequences (add, enable, disable, remove; model 3 and 4) in both lanes, the r4133 citations in the docs of the touched functions, the §4 notes, full gate + `lane_diff`.

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 on the part-1 deck (generator added / enabled after a first `Set algorithm=NCIM` solve). Measure whether r4133 answers at all (it may overrun its dynamic array as on the P1 deck of RP3.13) and, if it does, the converged node voltages and iteration count. Decides: if r4133 answers sanely, the new pins carry its node voltages as literals at the calibrated floor; if it crashes/hangs/returns garbage, the pins are KCL + port-self-consistency only and the record says "r4133 cannot answer: <symptom>". Never adopt a garbage value.

**Acceptance:**
- The three new pins (both generator models) fail at the pre-fix commit and pass after it, in both lanes; the three existing NCIM panic pins still pass.
- No unchecked index into the NCIM generator arrays remains (`rg` evidence in the record).
- The gated NCIM corpus cases stay green on both channels with exact iteration counts; no golden byte moves; ledger unchanged; `lane_diff` result recorded.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D00-06 — Correct the NCIM swing-source sum: element set, multi-terminal stride, enabled filter, clip, bystander cache writes
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-06", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D00-01", "RF-D00-05"], "n_uids": 6} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-01, RF-D00-05; plus `lane_diff.ps1` (§2.2).
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/elements/traits.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/ncim.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/solution/solution/ncim.rs`, `investigations/to_opendss/` (R1: main checkout, local-only), `tests/corpus/ledger.json` (only for a gated NCIM cell proven to move, each entry with its pin)
**Doc notes (§4):** `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`

All six findings sit on one function, `solution/solution/ncim.rs::ncim_stamp_swing_source_currents` (the port of r4133 `VSource.pas` `CalcInjCurrAtBus`), and on its tripwire `exec/tests/ncim.rs::ncim_swing_bus_carries_no_pc_element_on_the_gated_decks`. Design them together; the physically correct value is I_swing = -(sum of the terminal currents of every other enabled element bonded to the swing bus).

**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.13|AC1|AC-1` (major) - the PD loop (over `pd_elements` + faults) and the PC loop (over `pc_elements` + sources) sum a different element set than r4133: r4133 `getPCEatBus` (`Circuit.pas:1540-1580`) includes the Capacitor and Reactor classes (`:1558`) and ADDS them PCE-style, while `getPDEatBus` (`:1493-1536`) drops every element whose two buses strip to the same name (`:1522`) and only tests GetBus(1)/GetBus(2) (`:1520-1521`). Audit probe (deck: 3-phase source, `Line.l1`, `Load.ld1`, shunt `Capacitor.cs` at `sourcebus`, `Set algorithm=NCIM`): both engines agree on every node voltage, the port's Vsource row closes KCL, r4133's does not - an r4133 bug. Keep the port's KCL-correct set. Delete the false "zero exposure" derivation in the function doc of `ncim_stamp_swing_source_currents` and the "only for a PC element" premise in the tripwire's doc; add `ncim_swing_source_current_closes_kcl_with_a_shunt_capacitor` (Vsource.source = -(I_line + I_cap), residual ~0, r4133's measured row quoted as the second number); extend the tripwire to scan `pd_elements` + faults for Capacitor/Reactor/Fault at the swing bus so a gated r4133 deck that grows one reds; §4 note with the DIVERGENCES.md row (r4133 lines + probe numbers).
- `RP|RP3.13|AC1|AC-2` (major) - PD-loop stride `((cd.yorder as f64) / 2.0).round()` reproduces r4133's 2-terminal assumption (`VSource.pas:1135` `Round(ActivePDE.Yorder / 2)`), so for a PD element with more than two terminals the sum reads the wrong conductors and prints a KCL-violating current in both lanes. Use the true per-terminal stride `cd.nconds` and index the terminal actually bonded to the swing bus. Pin `ncim_swing_source_current_closes_kcl_through_a_three_winding_transformer` (audit deck: 3-winding wye transformer with winding 2 on `sourcebus`, loads at `b1`/`b3`; expected row = negated transformer terminal-2 currents, Iresid ~0; r4133's row 9.2280-58.8813j, ~0, 55.5562-21.2260j quoted as the divergent number); DIVERGENCES.md row via §4 note.
- `RP|RP3.13|AC1|AC-3` (minor) - the `clip` closure (`|v: Option<&Complex64>| v.copied().unwrap_or(ZERO)`) silently substitutes zero for an out-of-range conductor read; with the old stride that overrun is ordinary (4-winding transformer or AutoTrans tertiary at the swing bus). After the AC-2 fix the overrun is structurally impossible: replace `clip` with direct terminal-slice access plus a `debug_assert!` on the length and correct its comment; if a defensive `.get()` stays it must push a diagnostic, never contribute a silent zero. Cover with a 4-winding / tertiary-at-swing variant of the AC-2 pin.
- `RP|RP3.13|AC1|AC-4` (minor) - the swing-source selection (first `ckt.sources` entry that is a `VSource` with `node_ref.first() == Some(&1)`) does not test `cd.enabled`, although both loops in the same function do. Add the enabled test (check r4133's own selection in `VSource.pas` / `Solution.pas` and cite it; an enabled test is correct physics regardless). Pin: a disabled VSource declared ahead of the live one on the slack node - the live source's current must be the KCL value.
- `RP|RP3.13|AT1|AT-4` (minor) - the tripwire re-derives "the stamped swing source" with a different predicate (`cd.enabled && node_ref.first() == Some(&1)`, no VSource type test) under a comment calling it the product's own rule; `ckt.sources` also holds Isource and GICsource. Expose the product's selection as one `pub(crate)` helper in `ncim.rs` and call it from the tripwire; do not fork the rule.
- `RP|RP3.13|AC1|AC-5` (note) - the stamp calls `refresh_iterminal` (`elements/traits.rs`) on every element at the swing bus, which overwrites that element's `cd.iterminal` and calls `mark_iterminal_solved`; r4133 reads into a local `ElmCurrents` buffer (`VSource.pas:1123`, `:1158`) and never touches the bystanders' `ITerminal`. Compute the bystander currents into a local buffer (via `get_currents`) instead. If its `&mut` side effects make that impractical, document the cache write in the function doc and pin that Powers/Losses of a swing-bus element after an NCIM solve equal V·conj(I) at the converged NodeV. Keep this consistent with the cache semantics RF-D00-01 established.

**Parts:**
1. Probes first (the two audit decks on epri-worker r4133 - cheap, and their numbers go straight into the pin docs), then the engine: `ncim_stamp_swing_source_currents` element set (`AC-1`), true per-terminal stride (`AC-2`), `clip` removal (`AC-3`), enabled test (`AC-4`), bystander currents into a local buffer or the documented fallback (`AC-5`), the shared `pub(crate)` selection helper + tripwire extension (`AT-4`, `AC-1`), and the two KCL pins (shunt capacitor, 3-winding transformer) quoting both numbers. Read `ncim.rs` and the relevant fns of `traits.rs` by symbol only; gate green in both lanes; commit; hand over through `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D00-06.md`: the probe transcripts (deck, command, r4133 row, node voltages), the helper name, the pin names, the swing-current numbers.
2. The 4-winding / tertiary-at-swing variant (`AC-3`) and the disabled-first-VSource pin (`AC-4`); the re-run of the five gated NCIM decks (a moved cell = field-level ledger exclusion + expected-value pin, R8 otherwise); the upstream report(s) in the main checkout (R1, house format = read one neighbouring report only); the three §4 notes (incl. the `DIVERGENCES.md` row); full gate + `lane_diff`; commit.

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133, one run each on the two audit decks (shunt capacitor at the swing bus; 3-winding transformer with winding 2 at the swing bus): re-measure the Vsource terminal currents and node voltages at full f64 precision. Decides the literals quoted as "r4133's number" in the two pins and in DIVERGENCES.md, and confirms that node voltages agree (so the divergence is confined to the Vsource current row). Also re-run the five gated NCIM decks: a gated cell that moves gets a field-level exclusion plus pin (R8), otherwise nothing.

**Ruling:** R3 - a proven r4133 bug is never reproduced: fix in both lanes, exclude divergent cells field-by-field, pin by expected-value test, add the DIVERGENCES.md row with r4133 evidence (R3 names D00-06 explicitly). R1 - the upstream report(s) for the swing-source sum are written in the MAIN checkout under `E:/RustProject/dss-rs/investigations/to_opendss/` with the next free number, local-only, named in the record.

**Acceptance:**
- `ncim_swing_source_current_closes_kcl_with_a_shunt_capacitor`, `ncim_swing_source_current_closes_kcl_through_a_three_winding_transformer` (+ the 4-winding variant) and the disabled-first-VSource pin exist and pass in both lanes, each naming both numbers.
- The tripwire and the product share one selection helper; the tripwire scans PD elements and faults too and is green on the five gated decks.
- No `unwrap_or(ZERO)` on a conductor read remains in the stamp; no golden byte moves; ledger gains entries only for cells proven to move, each with a pin; `lane_diff` result recorded.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D00-07 — Harden the NCIM tripwire population and replace the vacuous P8/P9 legs with real r4133 pins
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-07", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D00-06"], "n_uids": 4} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-06.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/ncim.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `docs/plans-archive/R4133_PROPS_PLAN.md`

Test-only step on the file RF-D00-06 just changed: re-read the final swing-source semantics and the shared selection helper there before editing. "P1", "P8", "P9" are the numbered pins of the RP3.13 block in `exec/tests/ncim.rs` (see its module header).

**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.13|AT1|AT-3` (minor) - `exec/tests/ncim.rs::ncim_swing_bus_carries_no_pc_element_on_the_gated_decks` and its helper `pc_bindings_to_bus`: the tripwire that carries the whole "zero oracle exposure" argument hardcodes its five-case population (three deck names plus two literal (directory, swing bus) pairs, `b1` and `89_clinchrv`) with no completeness check, and the source-scan helper matches only `bus1=` / `bus=` in `*.dss` files of one directory. Add a completeness rail: re-derive the NCIM-gating population by scanning `tests/corpus` for `algorithm=nc*` (case-insensitive) and assert it equals the hardcoded five; extend `pc_bindings_to_bus` to `bus2=` and to redirect/compile targets outside the deck directory - or use compiled terminals for all five decks and drop the source scan.
- `RP|RP3.13|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - module-header bullet of the RP3.13 block still says r4133 "writes past the end of the dynamic array and **deadlocks**" - the symptom the settlement refuted. Rewrite: r4133 overruns the dynamic array, the solve still answers (converged, 5 iterations, node voltages - pinned in P1) and only the first element access afterwards blocks.
- `RP|RP3.13|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - P9's leg `let r4133 = -i_line[k] + i_load[k]; let residual = r4133 + i_line[k] + i_load[k]; assert!((residual - 2.0*i_load[k]).norm() < 1e-9)` is an algebraic identity and can never red. Build r4133's row from the measured literals (`R4133_LINE_T1` / `R4133_LDSWING`) and/or assert the engine's `i_line` / `i_load` equal those literals at the calibrated floor, then assert the residual of THOSE numbers is 2·I(load). §4 note: correct the record's settlement AC-2 sentence ("names both engines' rows ...").
- `RP|RP3.13|SA1|SA-3` (minor) - P8's doc claims "**r4133 cannot be the oracle here**", but on P8's deck the DLL answers the solve (converged, 3 iterations, YNodeVarray, `Line.l1` terminal-1 currents); only the first VSource element read dies (DSS error #327, stack overflow). Narrow the claim to the VSource element reads and add the r4133 legs the DLL does answer as literals re-probed at full precision, compared at the calibrated floor. §4 notes: the same claim in the RP3.13 record and in the archived plan.

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 on P8's exact deck (copy it from the test): `converged`, iteration count, `YNodeVarray`, `Line.l1` terminal-1 currents at full f64 precision - these become the P8 literals (audit probe gave converged=True, 3 iterations, `Line.l1` t1 = 92.93435244633514-38.001194022084746j; re-measure, do not copy). Do NOT read any VSource element on that deck (it kills the worker). If a re-probed value disagrees with the port above the floor, stop that finding (R8) and report - it is a port bug or a new divergence, not a tolerance question.

**Acceptance:**
- The population rail exists and would red on a sixth NCIM corpus deck; the tripwire covers `bus2=` / out-of-directory bindings (or uses compiled terminals throughout).
- P9 asserts on measured r4133 literals (mutating a literal reds it); P8 carries the r4133 converged / iterations / node-voltage / `Line.l1` legs.
- No tolerance constant changes; no golden byte moves; ledger unchanged.

### RF-D00-08 — Stop reproducing the two r4133 WindGen kvarBase/varBase bugs; exclude and pin the divergences
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-08", "effort": "max", "parts": 3, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D00-03"], "n_uids": 2} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-03; plus `lane_diff.ps1` (§2.2).
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/elements/pc/windgen/nominal.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/pc/windgen/tests.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/lane.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs`, `investigations/to_opendss/55-windgen-qmode0-zero-var-dispatch.md`, `investigations/to_opendss/77-windgen-qmode2-negative-kvarbase-double-negation.md`, `investigations/to_opendss/78-windgen-varbase-before-kvarbase-renormalisation.md`, `tests/corpus/ledger.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `STATUS.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`

The two uids describe the same two defects from the settlement side and the code side; both are closed by one fix each. Defect (c): in the QMode=2 (volt-var) arm of `nominal.rs`, `kvar_calc = self.kvar_base * vmag_tmp; if kvar_calc.abs() > self.kvar_base { kvar_calc = self.kvar_base; if vmag_tmp < 0.0 { lead_lag = -1.0 } }` is r4133 `PCElements/WindGen.pas:1313-1318` line for line - a magnitude tested against the SIGNED base. With `kvar_base < 0` (`pf=-0.9`, or `kvar=-400` with kVA unset) the test is always true: the curve is defeated and a negative curve point flips the sign twice. Defect (b): in the recalc function of `nominal.rs`, `var_base = 1000.0 * kvar_base / nphases` is computed BEFORE the `if !self.kva_not_set` kVA renormalisation of `kvar_base` (`WindGen.pas:1360-1361` before `:1374-1379`), so models 4/5 (`windgen/solve.rs`) and `yq_fixed` use the previous recalc's base.

**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.10|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `elements/pc/windgen/nominal.rs`, both sites above: two upstream defects reproduced verbatim in both lanes, unpinned, unexcluded, against the 2026-08-02 policy. Fix (c): clamp on `kvar_base.abs()` and carry the base's sign exactly once (no double negation; the curve is followed inside the clamp). Fix (b): compute `var_base` after the renormalisation, with one explicit sign convention shared with arm 1 / QMode 0 - write that convention into the function doc. Both-numbers expected-value pins: unit pins in `windgen/tests.rs` (negative-pf deck: volt-var follows the curve and an absorbing machine never injects; `kW=1000 kVA=1200 pf=-0.9`: models 4/5 and `yq_fixed` use the fresh base on the FIRST recalc) and live-deck pins in `tests/props_r4133_pins.rs` quoting r4133's measured number beside the correct one.
- `RP|RP3.10|SA1|SA-2` (major) - `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, RP3.10 "Open, recorded not chased" items (b) and (c) under the umbrella claim "none reproduced": false at HEAD. After the fix, §4 note to correct that claim (both defects were reproduced until RF-D00-08; name the pins and any ledger rows), plus the same sentence in report 55 (R1: main checkout). Measure, do not assume, the oracle footprint: triage expects zero (gated windgen decks have pf >= 0); touch `lane.rs` / `tests/corpus/ledger.json` only if a gated cell actually moves, field-by-field, each entry with a pin (R8).

**Parts:**
1. `nominal.rs`: both fixes + the unit pins in `windgen/tests.rs`. Commits; hands over the sign convention chosen and the pin decks.
2. Live r4133 probe for both numbers; `props_r4133_pins.rs` expected-value pins; gate run to see which gated windgen cells move; ledger/lane exclusions only for cells proven to move. Commits; hands over the probe transcript path and the moved-cell list (possibly empty).
3. Upstream reports (R1): correct `55-...md`, author the two new reports; §4 notes; full gate, stale-ledger triage, `lane_diff`.

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 on (i) a QMode=2 WindGen with negative `kvar_base` (pf=-0.9) and a volt-var curve with a negative point, (ii) `kW=1000 kVA=1200 pf=-0.9` with models 4 and 5: read the WindGen kvar / terminal powers after the first solve. Decides the "r4133 prints X" literal of each pin and proves the divergence exists upstream; then the five `modes:windgen/*` gated decks decide whether any ledger row is needed.

**Ruling:** R1 - gitignored `investigations/` files are edited in the MAIN checkout by absolute path, nothing committed, the record says "local-only", nothing deleted or moved; new upstream reports take the next free number in `to_opendss/`. The code fix itself needs no ruling (R3 policy). Note: at plan time the highest existing report is 74, so the file names 77/78 in the Files list are provisional - take the next free numbers at execution time and name them in the record.

**Acceptance:**
- Unit pins (windgen/tests.rs) and live pins (props_r4133_pins.rs) for both defects exist, name both numbers, and pass in both lanes; they red when either fix is reverted.
- No golden byte moves; `ledger.json` gains entries only for cells proven to move, each with a pin, none stale; existing four RP3.10 windgen ledger entries still hit.
- Reports 55 + two new ones exist in the main checkout (local-only, numbers recorded); `lane_diff` result recorded.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D00-13 — Make cloned Generator/PVSystem/Storage/CapControl user models live (ensure_live reachable)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-13", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D00-11"], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-11.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/elements/control/cap_control/control_loop.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/control/cap_control/user_model.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/pc/generator/user_model.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/pc/pvsystem/user_model.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/pc/storage/registers.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/pc/storage/user_model.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/user_model_clone.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** -
**Findings**
- `RP|RP1.3|SA1|SA-6` (minor) - each sibling slot's `ensure_live` (`generator/user_model.rs`, `pvsystem/user_model.rs`, `storage/user_model.rs`, `cap_control/user_model.rs`) is reached only from inside the slot's own calc/init/integrate/sample/do_pending, while every engine-side caller first guards on `user_model_exists()` = `live.is_some_and(|l| l.exists())` (Generator callers incl. the ShaftModel; `pvsystem/user_model.rs`; `storage/registers.rs`; `cap_control/control_loop.rs`), so a cloned element (live = None) silently falls back to its built-in model. Fix: port the WindGen settlement shape - `windgen/user_model.rs::take_live_user_model` (engine-side `ensure_live` revive ahead of the `exists()` guard), as it stands after RF-D00-11 - to the four classes, Generator's ShaftModel slot included; load errors are surfaced into the ErrorLog, never swallowed. Add one clone-then-solve pin per class in the new `exec/tests/user_model_clone.rs` (declared in `exec/tests/mod.rs`), mirroring the clone pins of `exec/tests/windgen_usermodel.rs`: clone the circuit (`ClassArena::clone_ckt`), solve, and assert the user model - not the built-in - produced the result (a guest-distinctive value), plus a failing-load case that yields a diagnostic.
**Parts:**
1. P1 - Generator (UserModel + ShaftModel) and PVSystem: make the engine-side sites revive a cloned slot; create `exec/tests/user_model_clone.rs` with the Generator and PVSystem pins; full gate (§2.2); commit. Hands over in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D00-13.md`: the revive helper shape used, the fixture guests/decks used by the pins, anything found in Storage/CapControl while reading.
2. P2 - Storage (+ `storage/registers.rs`) and CapControl (+ `cap_control/control_loop.rs`): same shape, same pins per class; full gate; commit. Run `tools/lanes/lane_diff.ps1` only if a solver path or compat kernel was touched (not expected).
**Acceptance:**
- `exec/tests/user_model_clone.rs` holds a clone-then-solve pin for each of Generator, PVSystem, Storage, CapControl, each red when the revive is removed (prove locally), green in both lanes.
- No engine-side `user_model_exists()` guard can short-circuit a cloned slot in the four classes; load failures produce a diagnostic.
- Un-cloned behaviour unchanged: no golden byte moves, no ledger change, corpus gate green.
- The uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D00-16 — Close the props_r4133 evidence-lock holes (digest, max_rel/example columns, population drift, supplement header)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D00-16", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": [], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/props_norm.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_evidence_lock.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`, `tests/corpus/props_r4133/examples_supplement.txt`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md`, `tests/corpus/props_r4133/README.md`
Order inside the step: do the two `examples_supplement.txt` edits (AT2-3, AT2-4 of RP2.1) FIRST, then compute the new digests, so the supplement is hashed in its final form. The frozen data rows of every evidence file stay byte-identical; only comment/header lines of the supplement change.
**Findings**
- `RP|RP0.1|AC1|AC1-1` (minor) - `props_r4133_evidence_lock.rs`: the `VERBATIM` SHA-256 table covers five files only; `examples_full.txt` (3378 rows) is guarded structurally (row count, unique triple, sums vs `bins.tsv`, `classify()` bin), so an in-place rewrite of a value in 2203 numeric-only rows passes, while the module doc claims the lock stops a quiet edit. Fix: add SHA-256 + length rows for `examples_full.txt`, `examples_supplement.txt`, `bins.tsv`, `shape_in_scope.txt` (same raw-byte handling as `verbatim_copies_keep_their_bytes`; `the_directory_is_declared_binary_safe` already guarantees no EOL conversion - confirm before hashing). Update the module doc; leave TESTING.md and README notes saying the digest, not the structural checks, stops a quiet edit.
- `RP|RP0.1|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `bins()` / `struct Bin` store `f[0..=5]` and `f[7]` but never `f[6]` (`max_rel`), and the pair-file loop of `bins_tsv_agrees_with_the_pair_files` keeps only the trailing rows field, so the §1.1 rule `max_rel >= 1e-4` is never checked. Fix: capture `f[6]`, parse column 4 of `numeric_pairs*.txt`, and assert: `bins.max_rel == numeric_pairs.max_rel` (94/94), `numeric_pairs_in_scope.max_rel == bins.max_rel_in_scope` (53/53), numeric bin is 6/7 iff `max_rel >= 1e-4` (take the exact bin direction from the plan §1.1 / README, do not guess), `max_rel_in_scope <= max_rel`. Negative probes M1 (`line.rmatrix` max_rel `1.50e+00` -> `1.50e-09` in `bins.tsv`) and M2 (`capacitor.cuf` `4.00e-06` -> `4.00e+06` in `numeric_pairs_in_scope.txt`) must red - run on a scratch copy, never commit a mutated evidence file.

### RF-D01-01 — Fix Relay/SwtControl per-phase state parse off-by-one and pin the >6-phase render clip
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D01-01", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/elements/control/relay/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/control/relay/tests.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/control/swt_control/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/elements/control/swt_control/tests.rs`, `investigations/to_opendss/` (R1: main checkout, local-only)
**Doc notes (§4):** `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `docs/plans-archive/R4133_PROPS_PLAN.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`
**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.7|AC3|AC3-1` (major) - `relay/mod.rs` `interpret_relay_state` (`while !token.is_empty() && i < RCMAX`) and `swt_control/mod.rs` `interpret_switch_state` (`... && i < SW_MAX`): both lanes reproduce the r4133 per-phase parse off-by-one (Relay.pas:1286 `i<RELAYCONTROLMAXDIM`, SwtControl.pas:461 `i<SWTCONTROLMAXDIM` - twin confirmed at source; the ganged arms loop `1 to MAXDIM`, the array is `Array[1..6]`), so the 6th per-phase token is silently dropped and two pins assert the bug. Fix: loop `i <= RCMAX` / `i <= SW_MAX` in both lanes; flip the two relay pins (the "6th token must be dropped" pin and row 2 of the ordinal/interpreter differential, currently `[open, open, open, open, open, closed, ]`) and any SwtControl twin pin into expected-value pins that the 6th token lands; fix the "silently dropped" site comments. Zero corpus exposure, so no ledger row: leave a record note and a `DIVERGENCES.md` note citing both Pascal lines. Author the upstream-ready report for the off-by-one (R1: `E:/RustProject/dss-rs/investigations/to_opendss/`, main checkout, local-only, the next free number listed at execution time, house format of the neighbouring reports, both Pascal lines `Relay.pas:1286` / `SwtControl.pas:461` quoted) and name its recorded id in the record and in the `DIVERGENCES.md` note. Triage evidence slip: it says the r4133 render bound is `Min(MAXDIM, NPhases)` at Relay.pas:1409/:1420 - those getters are uncapped (see next bullets); do not copy that sentence.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `swt_control/mod.rs` `state_size()` clips at `min(6, phases)` while r4133 SwtControl.pas:591/:602 loop `1 to ControlledElement.NPhases` uncapped; the token-count divergence on a >=7-phase element is recorded nowhere. Keep the clip (R4): r4133 token 7 is `FPresentState^[7]` on `StateArray = Array[1..6]` (SwtControl.pas:19), an OOB read that is never reproduced. Add an expected-value pin (7-phase switched line renders six tokens; r4133 measured seven plus warning 384) beside `render_is_one_token_per_controlled_element_phase`, fix that test's doc that credits `Min(6,NPhases)` to r4133's getter, and leave the `DIVERGENCES.md` + `ORPHANED_GAPS.md` notes.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT3|AT3-1` (minor) - plan `R4133_PROPS_PLAN.md` RP3.7 "As executed" says r4133 getters "render up to min(6, NPhases)"; that cap is the port's. Precondition settled at source: both state arrays are 6 slots (Relay.pas:62, SwtControl.pas:19), so the cap is a non-reproduced OOB and needs pin + registration only, no code change. State the reason in both `state_size` docs (relay and swt_control), add the Relay twin of the 7-phase pin in `relay/tests.rs`, and leave notes: plan text correction (getters uncapped: SwtControl.pas:591/:602, Relay.pas:1409/:1420; `MAXDIM` bounds only the parse loops :461/:1286) and an `ORPHANED_GAPS.md` 1.15 width sentence next to the missing warning. `exec/tests/swt_control.rs` does not exist at HEAD; the triage path is indicative - put pins in the two `tests.rs` files and do not create it unless needed (a new file also needs a `mod` line in `exec/tests/mod.rs`, outside this step's files: report instead).
- `RP|RP3.7|AC2|AC2-5` (note) - `swt_control/mod.rs` `ganged_view` + `sample` / `do_pending_action`: the retained 0.14.5 glue reads slot 1 and ganged-writes all slots (`set_all`), discarding a heterogeneous per-phase state (repro `state=(open, closed, closed)` then `normal=closed`); r4133 comments both bodies out (SwtControl.pas:396-408, :484-507). Registration only: leave an `ORPHANED_GAPS.md` 1.16 note with this second symptom and add a one-line cross-reference to 1.16 in the `ganged_view` doc. Retiring the glue stays with 1.16.
- `RP|RP3.7|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - `swt_control/mod.rs` `state_size()` doc claims `recalc` refreshes `ctrl_snap` at every `EndEdit`; `recalc` only reads it. Rewrite: `ctrl_snap` is written only by `set_object_ref` (`accessors.rs`) and `make_pos_sequence`; point at `ORPHANED_GAPS.md` 1.13 for the staleness gap.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT2|AT2-2` (note) - `swt_control/mod.rs` `normal_state` field doc links a non-existent `SwtControl::normal_view` and claims a `normal_state_set` render gate; `get_enum_array` renders unconditionally. Rewrite the sentence to what `normal_state_set` actually gates (the Edit supplemental latch), or drop it.
- `G|G2.2d|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - `relay/tests.rs` `non_debug_lines` filters on `"DEBUG SAMPLE"`, which never matches (event-log elements are stored verbatim as `Element=Debug Sample: ...`), so it counts every line and its doc is false. Filter on `Element=Debug Sample:` (the spelling `sample_state_trace_follows_debugtrace` and `tests/harness/lane.rs` use), correct the doc, and add a self-check test: with `DebugTrace=yes` after a `sample()` the helper's count excludes the trace line.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT3|AT3-4` (note) - `relay/tests.rs`, the 6-phase per-phase-cap pin: its doc says it "asserts nothing about a fresh 6-phase render" while the body asserts `closed6` before the `edit relay.r6 state=closed` baseline. Correct the doc: the assert pins the port's six in-bounds closed slots (the non-reproduced r4133 init OOB) and is load-bearing. Same test as AC3-1 - one edit.
**Parts:** (four ~1.4-2k-line files: navigate by `rg` on the fn/test names named above, never read a file in full)
1. Probes (a) and (b) first (two small decks each on epri-worker r4133, numbers into the state file), then the code: the parse fix in both classes (`RP|RP3.7|AC3|AC3-1`) with the two flipped relay pins + a SwtControl 6th-token pin, the two 7-phase pins (`RP|RP3.7|AT2|AT2-1`, `RP|RP3.7|AT3|AT3-1` - the `state_size` doc reasons included), the `non_debug_lines` fix + self-check (`G|G2.2d|AC1|AC-2`) and the `AT3-4` doc line of the same test. Gate green in both lanes; commit; hand over through `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D01-01.md`: the probe transcripts, the pin names, the two Pascal lines quoted.

### RF-D01-04 — Generalise the Save sizing-property hoist beyond the six hardcoded classes
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D01-04", "effort": "max", "parts": 3, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 3} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/report.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/report/save/save.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/save_roundtrip.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, `STATUS.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`
**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.11|AT1|AT-1` (major) - `exec/tests/report.rs` `save_puts_the_sizing_property_first_for_every_curve_class` (+ `save_write_puts_npts_first_for_loadshape`, `xycurve_save_write_puts_npts_first`) pin exactly the six classes `sizing_property` lists, so nothing reds for any other class although the property tables declare the same sizer->array relation via `PropDef::*_array(name, size_prop)` for a dozen more. Add a table-driven pin over ALL classes that declare a sized array (derive the list from the property tables, not by hand): Capacitor NumSteps, WireData/CNData/TSData/LineCode/LineGeometry/XfmrCode Seasons->Ratings, LineSpacing, Line, Transformer/AutoTrans, GenDispatcher, StorageController, ESPVLControl - assert save -> recompile -> re-save is a byte fixed point and every sizer precedes its arrays. Winding-indexed classes (`wdg=` cursor) need care: the hoist must not reorder cursor-dependent tokens.
- `RP|RP3.11|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `report/save/save.rs` `sizing_property()` is a six-arm `match class_name` with `_ => None`; `save_write` walks properties in set order, so `kvar=[100 200 300] NumSteps=3` is saved `kvar` first and reloads as 3x33.33 - a silently converging wrong circuit on a corpus-used spelling. Both upstreams share it (r4133 SaveWrite guards only LoadShape `Npts`); R3: fix, never reproduce. Replace the match with a declarative hoist: for every array property the deck set, write its `size_prop` ahead of it when the sizer was also set; LoadShape keeps the r4133 "always write Npts" rule as the one special case. Pin with Save->reload round-trips: `new capacitor.cstep kvar=600 kv=12.47 numsteps=4 states=[1 0 1 0]` (reloaded total kvar == 600, states kept) plus one LineSpacing (`NConds` -> X/H) and one LineCode case; update the `save.rs` module doc (the P7 paragraph) and leave a record note correcting "defect class closed".
- `RP|RP3.11|AT1|AT-5` (note) - `exec/tests/report.rs`, the deciding windgen divergence pin cites `tests/props_r4133_replay.rs:1197-1199` for "r4133's own SaveWrite emits kvar=0 for a windgen ... flattens PFNominal to 1.0"; the text has moved (those lines are now the `windgen-qmode0-no-arm` sentence). Re-cite by symbol: name the ledger cause / pin in `props_r4133_replay.rs` that carries the sentence, no line number; leave a record note for the twin citation in `r4133-props-rp3.md`.
**Parts:**
1. Census + design, no engine change. The census is NOT read by hand (the `_array(` spelling has ~140 hits over ~97 files under `crates/dss-core/src`): write a throwaway script (scratchpad, not committed) that walks the class property tables for every `PropDef` constructor carrying a `size_prop` (`prop_def.rs`: `double_array`, `int_array`, and the other constructors that set `size_prop:` - list them from that file first) and emits one row per (class, array property, sizer property, constructor kind); write that table plus the design (rule for cursor classes, r4133 `SaveWrite` order per class family) to `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D01-04.md`. Commits the AT-5 citation fix.
2. Implement the table-driven hoist in `save.rs` plus the unit pins in `exec/tests/report.rs`: one hand-written reload pin per class family named in AC-1 (capacitor / LineSpacing / LineCode) and ONE table-driven all-classes fixed-point pin that iterates the part-1 census (carried into the test as a const table, or re-derived from the property tables at runtime), building one minimal deck per (class, sizer) from the property table (sizer = 2 or 3, array = as many tokens) rather than hand-authoring a deck per class; the two cursor classes (Transformer, AutoTrans) get one explicit case each. Gate green; commit. Hands over the list of any Save golden / run-file comparison that moved.
3. `save_roundtrip.rs` deck coverage for the newly hoisted classes, triage of anything part 2 moved, the four §4 notes (record, DIVERGENCES row with r4133 SaveWrite evidence, ORPHANED_GAPS, STATUS); gate green; commit.
**Probes:** epri-worker r4133: `Save circuit` on the capacitor `numsteps` deck (and one Seasons/Ratings deck), recompile the saved deck on r4133 and read back `kvar` / `Ratings` - proves the shared upstream bug for the DIVERGENCES row. This step authors NO `to_opendss` report: the upstream report for the sizing-property-last defect is written by RF-D05-08 (wave 44, `RP|RP3.11|SA1|SA-3`, slug `savewrite-sizing-property-last`), which covers the whole class (curve classes + Capacitor NumSteps / Seasons->Ratings / LineSpacing ...) - hand it the epri-worker Save/recompile transcript (decks, commands, r4133 read-backs) through `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D01-04.md` (transcript kept in the main checkout under `tmp/`, local-only) and name that hand-over in the record. If a Save byte golden or G1.10b run-file cell moves, no golden is regenerated ad hoc (R8) and the exclusion is NOT written in this step: the golden-token exclusions live in the golden harness (`golden_reports.rs` `GateSpec`, `harness/lane.rs`, `harness/mod.rs`) and run-file cells in `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, none of which is in this step's Files (the wave partners RF-D00-01 and RF-D08-06 own them). Stop that half: record every moved cell (file, token, port value, oracle value) in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D01-04.md` and the step record, and report to the coordinator, who assigns the field-level exclusion plus expected-value pin to a step that owns the harness/ledger.
**Acceptance:**
- The six-arm match in `sizing_property()` is gone; the all-classes fixed-point pin and the capacitor / LineSpacing / LineCode reload pins pass in both lanes.
- The three existing curve-class pins still pass (LoadShape always-write rule kept).
- No golden byte, ledger entry or harness exclusion moves in this step; every moved Save golden token / run-file cell is recorded (file, token, both values) in the state file and the record and reported to the coordinator for an owned exclusion + pin.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D01-05 — Make save_roundtrip compare emitted-deck properties, not only solve observables
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D01-05", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D01-04"], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D01-04.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/report/save/save.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/save_roundtrip.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** -
**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.11|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `tests/save_roundtrip.rs` `round_trip_full` compares only solve-observable state (node voltages, iteration count, discrete control state, checkpoint Y, file set, per-bus `kVBase` via `bus_kv_bases`), so a property lost in the emitted deck (`Ratings`, capacitor step `kvar`, `%Mag`, `BHCurrent`) moves nothing it reads. Generalise the comparator `save_roundtrip_protection` already has (the `element_properties` snapshot + `assert_prop_token_eq`) into `round_trip_full`: snapshot every element's full property list before Save, recompile the saved deck, compare on all round-trip decks. Every property it reds on is either fixed in `save.rs` or listed in one explicit named exclusion table (class.property -> reason), each entry with an expected-value pin - never silently skipped, and the token comparison is not loosened beyond what `assert_prop_token_eq` does today.
**Parts:**
1. Add the property-level comparison helpers and run them over every round-trip deck with an empty exclusion table; record every red (deck, class.property, pre/post token) in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D01-05.md`. Commit only a green tree: if there are reds, commit the helpers un-wired and hand the red list over.
2. Triage each red: fix the Save writer in `save.rs`, or add the named exclusion + expected-value pin; wire the comparison into `round_trip_full`; gate green; commit. Bound (stop rule): if the part-1 red list exceeds ~8 distinct `class.property` pairs, or any red needs a fix in a file outside Files (a class renderer / accessor rather than `save.rs`), do NOT triage the tail here - land what is green (the `save.rs` fixes made, the exclusion table holding entries ONLY for pairs that already carry a pin - never a wildcard or class-wide skip, R8 shape) and leave the comparison un-wired from `round_trip_full` (as part 1 committed it) rather than wiring it over an unresolved red; record the remaining pairs (deck, class.property, pre/post token, where the fix lives) in the state file and the record, and report them to the coordinator for an owned follow-up step.
**Acceptance:**
- `round_trip_full` compares full per-element property lists on every round-trip deck in both lanes.
- The exclusion table is explicit: each entry names class.property, a reason and its pin; no class-wide or wildcard skip.
- Mutation check recorded: temporarily un-hoisting Capacitor `NumSteps` reds the harness.
- The uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.
