### RF-D02-10 — Close regen.rs rail gaps: knob/lane guard on public writers, produced_by pin, announcement asserts
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D02-10", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D08-07"], "n_uids": 6} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D08-07.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_lock.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/regen.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`

**Findings**
- `G|G0.2|AT1|AT-2` (note) - `harness/regen.rs` module doc ("G3.3a snapshots 144 of reports/export*'s 146 files"). The count never matched the corpus: `git ls-files tests/golden/reports | grep -c /export` = 152 (76 `.txt` + 76 `.meta.json`; 150 anchored `capi_v0145`, 2 `capi015` sidecars `export_capacity_seasonal.meta.json` / `export_overloads_seasonal.meta.json` refused). Fix: re-measure and write "150 of 152" in the module doc; re-measure the sibling G3.3b `show*` figure (144) while there. Leave doc notes for the same wrong figure in `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md` (G3.3a heading/body) and the G0.2 record. RF-D08-06 (already landed) left a `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md` note re-counting the same G3.3a sweep size: read it in `tmp/retro_fix/notes/` first and write ONE final number - your note must say it confirms or supersedes the RF-D08-06 figure, so the plan owner lands a single count.
- `G|G0.2|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - `harness/regen.rs` `ProducedBy::admits` (`LaneInvariant` admits every lane) + `golden_lock.rs` (regen carries `produced_by` over verbatim: `prev.and_then(|a| a.produced_by).unwrap_or(Parity)`; the only assertion is the shape check `is_self == produced_by.is_some()`). Flipping one JSON word to `lane-invariant` on a `self` row disarms the lane guard and the whole gate stays green; only `json/schema_full_port.json` is value-pinned. This defect is ALREADY fixed by RF-D08-07 (landed before this step): its `G|G0.1|AC2|AC2-2` added a `LANE_INVARIANT` register next to `DEANCHORED` in `golden_lock.rs`, the assert arm `self` row `produced_by` == register value, the regen derivation and a hand-flipped-row unit pin. Do NOT add a second register or a second design. Fix here: read RF-D08-07's landed check; extend it only where it does not already cover the point - i.e. if `regen.rs::the_committed_lock_parses_into_this_mirror` still carries its own `produced_by` reading, make that mirror read the `LANE_INVARIANT` register (the exact `self` row set - four rows, all `parity` at `ab9f0f76`: `json/schema_full_port.json`, `adiakoptics/midi_torn_tree.txt`, `props/recloser.json`, `props/relay.json` - is re-derived from `tests/golden/golden.lock.json`, never hard-coded twice). If RF-D08-07's pin already reds on a flipped row and the mirror needs nothing, close this uid as "closed by RF-D08-07" with the pin name.
- `G|G0.2|AC1|AC-5` (note) - `harness/regen.rs`, `Refusal::explain`, the `WrongLane` remedy arm `ProducedBy::LaneInvariant => Lane::Default.build_flag()`. The arm is unconstructible (`LaneInvariant.admits(_)` is always true) and, if ever reached, gives a wrong instruction. Fix: replace it with `unreachable!("lane-invariant admits every lane")`, or restructure `Refusal::WrongLane` to carry the producing `Lane` so no `LaneInvariant` arm exists.
- `G|G0.2|AT1|AT-4` (note) - `harness/regen.rs` `Refusal::explain`, `Rails::announce` and the `reminded` one-shot. No test observes the REFUSED/SNAPSHOT announcements: all 12 tests assert only the returned `Outcome` and disk bytes, so emptying `explain()` stays green. Fix: make the announce path testable (return the line, or route through an injectable sink on `Rails`) and add `refusals_announce_their_remedy_once_per_path` asserting the text names the artifact and the remedy (build flag / anchor), the per-path dedup, and the once-per-run stale-lock reminder.
- `G|G0.2|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `harness/regen.rs`, public `snapshot_text` / `snapshot_bytes` (one-line wrappers over private `snapshot_text_with` / `snapshot_bytes_with`). `the_armed_public_helpers_delegate_through_the_guards` drives only the `_with` twins; the public forms are called only in the `!armed` branch of `the_helpers_are_inert_without_the_knob`, so rewriting `snapshot_text` to `snapshot_bytes_with(regen(), rel, text.as_bytes())` (bypassing `write_text`'s binary-path assert) stays green. Fix: collapse the twin behind one private fn with a test-injectable rails source, or add an armed test that drives the PUBLIC wrappers against an injected `Rails` (cfg(test) thread-local/OnceLock override) and asserts a binary path through `snapshot_text` still trips the binary-path assert. That mutation must red.
- `G|G0.2|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - `harness/regen.rs`: `pub struct Rails`, `pub fn load`, `pub fn from_lock_text`, `pub fn write_bytes`, `pub fn write_text`. The §1.2 invariants of GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN (inert without `DSS_UPDATE_GOLDENS`; refuse the non-producing lane) hold only for `snapshot_*`: `Rails::load(repo_root(), Lane::Parity).write_text(..)` from a default build with no knob rewrites a parity golden (`load` never reads `UPDATE_ENV`, the lane is caller-declared). Fix: make those five items private/`pub(super)` (the unit tests are a child module), leave `snapshot_text`/`snapshot_bytes` as the only public writers, have `load` take the lane from `Lane::current()`; add a source-scan pin (e.g. `rails_are_constructed_only_inside_regen`) that reds on `Rails::` outside `regen.rs`. Do this one first - SA-1 and AT-4 build on the narrowed surface.

**Acceptance:**
- New tests exist and pass in both lanes: the `produced_by` value check (RF-D08-07's `LANE_INVARIANT` pin, extended or referenced - never duplicated), `refusals_announce_their_remedy_once_per_path`, the armed public-wrapper test, the `Rails::` source-scan pin; each named mutation above was tried and reds.
- `Rails` and its writers are no longer `pub`; no `LaneInvariant` remedy arm returns a build flag.
- No golden byte and no `golden.lock.json` byte moves; doc notes left for `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md` and the record (150 of 152).
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D02-11 — Give the G1.9 aggregate pins witnesses and fix their Pascal citations

Check first which source classes `total_power` actually walks and pick one of them.
- `G|G1.9|AC2|AC2-3` (note) - `exec/view.rs` doc of `sum_list_losses` and `exec/tests/aggregates.rs` (the `line_losses` pin comment) cite `Common/CktElement.pas:707-712` for `Get_Losses`' `FEnabled` guard; `:707` is the FUNCTION header, the guard `If FEnabled Then Begin` is `:742`. Fix both citations to `:742` (header at `:707`).
- `G|G1.9|AT2|AT2-4` (note) - the `IsShunt` filter citations end one line short: `exec/view.rs` twice (`Common/Circuit.pas:2436-2440`, in the `sum_list_losses` doc and at the "enabled AND not shunt" comment of the circuit-losses view) and `aggregates.rs` once (`Common/Circuit.pas:2438-2440`). r4133 `Circuit.pas:2440` is the `{Ignore Shunt Elements}` comment, the test `If Not pdElem.IsShunt` is `:2441`. Fix: `2436-2441`, `2436-2441`, `2438-2441`.
- `G|G1.9|AC2|AC2-4` (note) - `exec/tests/aggregates.rs`, rationale comment of `total_iterations_is_an_alias_of_iterations`. It says the value is "the LAST step's inner iteration count, never an accumulation" and that the accumulator is "exposed by NEITHER oracle surface"; r4133 `SolveSnap` ends with `Iteration := TotalIterations` (`Common/Solution.pas:2724`; local declared `:2678`, accumulated `:2703`), so SolutionI(40) (`DDLL/DSolution.pas:218-220`) and (7) (`:54`) DO return the per-solve accumulator, and the port mirrors it (`solution/solution/power_flow.rs`, `ckt.solution.iteration = total_iterations`). Fix: rewrite the comment accordingly; the alias assertion stays. If the G1.9 record repeats the sentence, leave a doc note.

**Acceptance:**

### RF-D02-12 — Rule on and fix substation_losses omitting AutoTrans sub=yes
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D02-12", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D02-01", "RF-D02-11"], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D02-01, RF-D02-11.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/elements/pd/auto_trans/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/tests/aggregates.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`

**Findings**
- `G|G1.9|AC2|AC2-2` (minor) - `exec/view.rs`, `Dss::substation_losses` (filters `ckt.transformers` on `is_substation()`) and its twin walk further down the same file (the second `.is_substation()` site, the loss-terms breakdown); pinned as correct by the AutoTrans block of the substation-losses pin in `exec/tests/aggregates.rs` (`New AutoTrans.a1 ... sub=yes`, asserts `terms.substation_losses == ["transformer.sub1"]`). r4133 declares AutoTrans property 30 `sub` (`PDElements/AutoTrans.pas:319`), documents "Designates whether this AutoTrans is to be considered a substation" (`:400-401`), stores `IsSubstation` (`:511`, field `:160`) and never reads it; `DDLL/DCircuit.pas:335-341` walks Transformers only. Fix (per the ruling below): include enabled AutoTrans elements with `is_substation` in both walks, flip the pin to expect the AutoTrans term (and assert the total grows by exactly the AutoTrans' own losses), keep the r4133 citations in the doc comment as the evidence of the upstream omission. At `ab9f0f76` `is_substation` is a PRIVATE field of `AutoTransObj` (`elements/pd/auto_trans/mod.rs`, read only by the `SUB` arms in `auto_trans/accessors.rs`) with no getter, while `view.rs` calls `.is_substation()` on Transformer only. Add the one-line read-only getter `pub fn is_substation(&self) -> bool` in `auto_trans/mod.rs` next to the Transformer twin (`transformer/mod.rs::is_substation`) - that file is in this step's files (RF-D02-01, its other editor, has landed); nothing else in `auto_trans/` changes. Doc notes: a `DIVERGENCES.md` row with the r4133 lines above, the record, the `ORPHANED_GAPS.md` sync. Per R1 write the upstream report `investigations/to_opendss/<next free number>-...md` in the MAIN checkout by absolute path (local-only, named in the record).

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133: a small deck with one `Transformer sub=yes` and one `AutoTrans sub=yes`; read `Circuit.SubstationLosses` and the AutoTrans' own `Losses`. Decides: confirms live (not only by source) that r4133 omits the AutoTrans term, and gives the both-numbers evidence for the DIVERGENCES row (port total = r4133 total + AutoTrans losses). Then the corpus gate: expected 0 moved cells (no corpus deck sets `sub=yes` on an AutoTrans); any moved cell is excluded field-by-field with a pin.

**Ruling:** R3 - a proven r4133 bug is never reproduced: fix in both lanes, exclude divergent cells field-by-field, pin by an expected-value test, add the DIVERGENCES row with r4133 evidence (R3 names D02-12 explicitly).

**Acceptance:**
- The aggregates pin asserts the AutoTrans `sub=yes` term is part of `substation_losses` (names and watts) in both lanes and reds on the pre-fix filter.
- Ledger gains 0 entries (or exactly one per measured moved cell, each with a pin); no golden byte moves.
- The `to_opendss` note exists locally (R1) and is named in the record; doc notes left for `DIVERGENCES.md`, `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, the record.

### RF-D02-13 — Pin the Show Voltages LL report-side convention and the dropped stale rows
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D02-13", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D02-12", "RF-D09-03"], "n_uids": 2} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D02-12, RF-D09-03.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/report/export/seq_voltages.rs`, `crates/dss-core/src/report/show/voltages.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/reports_show.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`

Note on files: `crates/dss-core/tests/reports_show.rs` does not exist at `ab9f0f76` (the report integration drivers are `golden_reports.rs`, `inc_matrix_reports.rs`, `ncim_reports.rs`). Either create it as a new thin expected-value driver for the two pins below, or put the pins into inline `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` of `report/show/voltages.rs` / `report/export/seq_voltages.rs` (both have none today); say which in the record. Do not add byte goldens for them.

**Findings**
- `G|G1.4c|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `exec/view.rs`, the G1.4c doc block near the VLL view claiming `report/show/voltages.rs` "reproduces no defect at all" and that the report paths "still carry upstream's conventions"; the behaviour lives in `report/show/voltages.rs` (the L-L loop, `kk` / `volts_ll` / `node_name_ll` declared per iteration, write guarded by `if kk.is_some()`). Upstream sets `kk := 1` BEFORE the `IF jj <= 4` block (r4133 `Common/ShowResults.pas:188-190`, write guard `If kk > 0` `:214-215`; capi 0.14.5 identical, `src/Common/ShowResults.pas:233-247`), so for the 4th and later node it prints a stale duplicate L-L row; on a bus like NEV `13kvbus` (nodes `[1,2,3,4,10]`) the oracles emit 5 rows, the port 3. This is a correct non-reproduction of an upstream bug that is unpinned and mis-documented. Fix: no behaviour change; correct the two false sentences in `view.rs`, add an expected-value pin (`Show Voltages LL Node` on a synthetic 5-node bus with nodes `[1,2,3,4,10]`: exact row count, no stale duplicate row, citing `ShowResults.pas:188-190/:214-215`). Doc notes: the same sentences in `DIVERGENCES.md` (the G1.4c VLL entry) and `TESTING.md`, plus a new DIVERGENCES row for the dropped stale rows; per R1 write the upstream report into `investigations/to_opendss/` (next free number) in the MAIN checkout, local-only.
- `G|G1.4c|AC2|AC2-5` (note) - `exec/view.rs` ("the two are pinned separately") and the `DIVERGENCES.md` sentence "The split between the report convention and the API semantics is pinned on both sides": the report half has no pin. `report/export/seq_voltages.rs` and `report/show/voltages.rs` carry no tests, and every voltage-report golden runs IEEE13 (nodes 1-3 only), so the ground-substituting branch in `seq_voltages.rs` (`bus.find(k)` = 0 -> ground for a missing phase) is unreachable in the suite (ORPHANED_GAPS 1.19: "zero golden cells, zero live cells"). Fix (preferred): add the report-side pin - a small synthetic deck with a >=3-node bus missing a phase, asserting what `Export SeqVoltages` and `Show Voltages` print today (literal expected cells) - so the `view.rs` sentence becomes true; leave a doc note syncing `DIVERGENCES.md` and ORPHANED_GAPS 1.19. RF-D09-03 (landed before this step, `G|G1.4c|SA1|SA-1`) already rewrote the S-VLL doc in `view.rs` and left `DIVERGENCES.md`/`TESTING.md` notes stating the two facts the pin must witness: `Show Voltages` never prints a ground-paired row (the `kk > 0` guard) and the real report/API difference on a `[1,3]` bus is pair order (3,1) vs (1,3). The pin asserts both; your `DIVERGENCES.md` note EXTENDS RF-D09-03's replacement text (read it in `tmp/retro_fix/notes/` first) and never restates the convention differently. Only if the pin proves infeasible, withdraw the "pinned" sentences instead and point at the 1.19 measurement.

**Acceptance:**
- A pin for the 5-node-bus `Show Voltages LL Node` row set and a pin for the missing-phase report convention exist, pass in both lanes, and red when (a) `kk` is hoisted out of the loop, (b) the ground-substitution branch is changed.
- `view.rs` no longer claims "reproduces no defect at all"; its "pinned separately" sentence names the new pins.
- No product behaviour change; no golden byte, no ledger entry moves; doc notes left for `DIVERGENCES.md`, `TESTING.md`, `ORPHANED_GAPS.md`; the `to_opendss` note is named in the record (R1).
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D02-14 — Add per-channel census and liveness asserts to the run-file comparison; fix run_files_pins citations
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D02-14", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D02-05", "RF-D02-08"], "n_uids": 5} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D02-05, RF-D02-08.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/run_files.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/run_files_pins.rs`, `tests/corpus/ledger.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`

**Findings**
- `G|G1.10a|AC3|AC3-2` (minor) - `corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`, `assert_scratch_declines_are_the_pinned_population`; `harness/run_files.rs` `SCRATCH_DECLINES` (keyed `(case label, name)`, no channel) and `ScratchCensus` (`visits`/`compared` only, printed, never asserted). The run-file SET census has no per-channel term, so a per-channel re-mask of `compare_run_files` is invisible, contradicting the call-site comment ("a re-masked `compare_run_files` request reds here ..."). G1.10b mitigates only the 7 file-exporting cases via `RUN_FILE_CONTENTS_COMPARED`. Fix: count (case, channel) set compares at the gating call site (`runner.rs`) - or reuse `RUN_FILE_CONTENTS_ROWS`, which already records one row per gated (case, channel) including `files=0` - and assert in the epilogue that rows-per-channel equals the requested population re-derived from the manifests minus ledger channel skips (the `scheduler::inc_matrix_requested_channels` precedent); fix the overclaiming comment. Doc notes for the record and `TESTING.md`.
- `G|G1.10a|AT2|AT2-2` (minor) - `harness/run_files.rs`, the `COMPARED` counter documented as "the non-vacuity half" of the census; no assertion reads it and there is no per-channel liveness rail, unlike `harness/inc_matrix.rs` (`compared_capi`/`compared_r4133`) and the topology rail in `scheduler.rs`. Fix: split `COMPARED` into per-channel counters on `ScratchCensus`, incremented by channel where the set compare happens; in `assert_scratch_declines_are_the_pinned_population` assert `compared > 0` and per-channel `> 0` whenever live cases request `compare_run_files` on that channel; add a unit test of the rail modelled on the inc_matrix one. Implement together with AC3-2 as one design (one per-channel census, two assertions: population equality and liveness).
- `G|G1.10a|AC3|AC3-3` (note) - `run_files_pins.rs` module doc (the Windows-only rationale "dss-epri is `#[cfg(windows)]`") and `scheduler.rs` `assert_scratch_declines_are_the_pinned_population`, the `#[cfg(not(windows))]` arm (`assert!(!requested, "... the classification lives in the #[cfg(windows)] crate dss-epri ...")`). Both are contradicted by D33(3): `crates/dss-epri/src/lib.rs` has a bare `pub mod guard;`, and `force_run_files` sets `compare_run_files` on every live non-large case, so the off-Windows arm is an unconditional panic. Fix: reword both to the D33(3) wording already in `harness/run_files.rs` (guard ungated; the `r4133` transport is the Win64 DLL) and replace the never-true assert with an honest cfg-skip stating the real reason.
- `G|G1.10a|AC3|AC3-5` (note) - `run_files_pins.rs`, the Visualize pin doc ("Rewrites that `.DSV` (`:114-115`)"), and the `tests/corpus/ledger.json` cause `visualize-dssview-file-pair`. r4133 `Plot/DSSGraph.pas`: `:114` is `TRY`, `:115` `AssignFile(ActiveDSSGraphFile, FileName)`, `:116` `Rewrite(ActiveDSSGraphFile)`. Fix: cite `:115-116` in the pin doc and the ledger cause; doc note for the same range in `DIVERGENCES.md` (the phase-record mention is historical - leave it).
- `G|G1.10a|AT3|AT3-3` (note) - `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, cause `visualize-dssview-file-pair`: ":3730 _V.DSV and :3739 _I.DSV" are the `vizVOLTAGE:` / `vizCURRENT:` case labels; r4133 `Plot/DSSPlot.pas` has `_V.DSV` at `:3732`, `_I.DSV` at `:3741` (`_PQ.DSV` `:3746`). Fix: `:3730` -> `:3732`, `:3739` -> `:3741` in the cause, together with the `:114-115` -> `:115-116` edit of AC3-5 (same cause string, `run_files_pins.rs`, `DIVERGENCES.md` via doc note). Cause text is outside the per-entry lock digest - verify the ledger lock/stale rails stay green after the edit; re-grep the r4133 tree before writing any number.

**Acceptance:**
- The run-file census carries a per-channel term; the epilogue asserts per-channel population equality and `compared > 0` per requested channel; the rail's teeth are proven by its OFFLINE unit test (a forged census with one channel at zero, or a population short by one (case, channel), must red - name the test in the record). A live mutation run (masking `compare_run_files` on one channel in `runner.rs` and re-running the corpus gate) is optional: if done, record it in one line; it is never a required second gate.
- No `#[cfg(not(windows))]` assert that can only panic remains; both Windows-only rationales use the D33(3) wording.
- `ledger.json`: only the cause text `visualize-dssview-file-pair` changes (0 entries added or removed, fail-on-stale green); all corrected citations verified against `.inputs/electricdss-code-r4133-trunk`.
- No golden byte moves; doc notes left for `TESTING.md`, the record and `DIVERGENCES.md`.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

- `RP|RP1.3|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - `generator/accessors.rs`, `pvsystem/accessors.rs`, `storage/accessors.rs`: `num_variables` / `variable_name` / `get_all_variables` / `set_variable` (and the `get_variable` path). On the DynamicExp path (`self.dyneq.has_dynamic_eq()`) `get_all_variables` returns early and `num_variables` returns the bare dyneq count, so a class with both `DynamicEq=` and a loadable `UserModel=` drops the model's variables; the RP1.3 settlement fixed this for WindGen only. Fix: port the WindGen shape (`windgen/dynamics.rs::variable_base` + the four accessors in `windgen/accessors.rs`) to Generator (UserModel AND ShaftModel tails), PVSystem and Storage: the tail is appended after BOTH arms and counted/addressed on both. Cite r4133 `generator.pas:2929-2959`, `PVsystem.pas:2793-2800`, `Storage.pas:3858-3867` (tail block is outside the `if DynamiceqObj = nil ... else` chain). Do NOT reproduce r4133's overlap (it writes the tail at `@States[NumGenVariables+1]` on the dyneq arm, clobbering the dump, and its NumVariables omits the dyneq count): place the tail after the dyneq block as WindGen does and leave a DIVERGENCES note. The overlap is a proven r4133 defect in three classes, so per R1 also write ONE upstream report for the Generator/PVSystem/Storage overlap (`investigations/to_opendss/<next free number>-der-usermodel-tail-overlaps-dynamicexp.md`, MAIN checkout by absolute path, local-only, named in the record and in the DIVERGENCES note) with the three Pascal ranges above as evidence - or record explicitly why none is owed; the WindGen twin is reported separately by RF-D00-10 (reports 75/76, later wave), which will cross-reference yours. Pin per class (wasm-backed or stub slot model, the same fixture style the WindGen pin uses): `DynamicEq` + user model -> `NumVariables == dyneq + model vars`, tail names and values appear, `set_variable` reaches the tail. The three listed `tests.rs` exist at `ab9f0f76`: the pins go there (the WindGen pin's home, `exec/tests/windgen_usermodel.rs`, is RF-D00-10's file and not in this step's files - put nothing there).
- `RP|RP1.3|SA1|SA-4` (minor) - `generator/dynamics.rs` (the `_ => "ERROR"` arm of the variable-name match and its "Pascal seeds `Result := 'ERROR'`" comment), `storage/dynamics.rs` (`0 => "ERROR"` arm), `ind_mach012/dynamics.rs::variable_name_impl` (`_ => "ERROR"` + doc). The `'ERROR'` out-of-range seed exists only in dss_capi (`.inputs/dss_capi/src/PCElements/Generator.pas:2736`); r4133 `generator.pas:2961-2972` has the implicit `Result := ''` and `If i<1 Then Exit`. Fix: read r4133 `VariableName` of all three classes (Generator, Storage, IndMach012); where r4133 yields `''` switch the out-of-range arm to `""`, add a unit pin per class (index 0 and index > NumVariables), and correct the comments that credit the capi-only seed to "Pascal". If a `capi_v0145` observable carries `'ERROR'`, exclude that field only and pin it. Leave the DIVERGENCES/record note per §4.

**Parts:**
1. Generator: tail on both arms incl. ShaftModel, its pin; the out-of-range `VariableName` fix + pins in generator / storage / ind_mach012 `dynamics.rs`. Commit; hand over the helper shape chosen (name, signature) and the probe result.
2. PVSystem and Storage accessors + pins in the same shape; both §4 notes. Commit.

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 (and dss-python for the capi side): `VariableName(0)` and `VariableName(NumVariables+1)` on a Generator, a Storage and an IndMach012 - decides per class whether the arm becomes `""` and whether a capi-side exclusion is needed. No probe of the wasm tail (r4133 cannot load the port's models); the source citation is the evidence there.
**Acceptance:**
- Three new tail pins (Generator, PVSystem, Storage) and the out-of-range name pins pass in both lanes.
- No golden byte moves; no ledger entry is added unless the probe shows a gated `'ERROR'` observable (then exactly one exclusion with its pin).
- `tmp/retro_fix/notes/` carries the DIVERGENCES text (tail placement vs r4133 overlap; `''` vs capi `'ERROR'`) and the RP1.3 record correction; the `to_opendss` report exists locally (R1) and is named in the record, or the record says why none is owed.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

Fix: make it census-driven, with this BOUND (no general deck walker, no redirect resolution): grep every `tests/corpus/**/*.dss` for lines matching `(?i)memorymapping\s*=\s*(yes|true)` (13 decks at `ab9f0f76`); parse ONLY those LoadShape definition lines for the `mult=(file=...)` / `csvfile=` / `file=` argument; resolve the path relative to the deck's own directory (a redirected deck is found by the same grep, so no redirect chain is followed); skip `.sng`/`.dbl`; assert the discovered set equals the literal expected list (kept as the expected value) and that exactly one file carries exponent bytes. `tests/corpus_manifest.rs::census()` is a deck-walking helper of the same shape you may COPY (not import - that file belongs to RF-D03-03).

It never states that r4133's command-string round trip quantizes `Cs-Cm` to 5 significant digits while the port writes the full f64. No behaviour change.
- `G|G2.5|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `capacitor/tests.rs`: the makeposseq pins use single-step banks only, so the multi-step `Cuf` tail (`Some(0.0)` for steps 2..N in `solve.rs`, `InterpretDblArray` semantics) is asserted nowhere and a `None` tail would silently keep the old values. Fix: add a pin on a `numsteps=3 cmatrix=...` capacitor asserting the emitted `Cuf` edit is `[value, 0, 0]` and that after applying it steps 2..3 read 0.0.
- `G|G2.5|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - `tests/corpus/modes/inputformat/shape_mmf_io/` has no `.gitattributes`, so `mmpq8_plain.csv` and `shape_mmf_io.dss` check out CRLF against LF blobs (`git ls-files --eol`: `i/lf w/crlf`). The MMF reader indexes by line width, so the corpus gate is re-run in both lanes.

Fix: after the `.gitattributes` lands, refresh the two text files to the committed LF bytes (delete + `git checkout --` those two paths only; the blobs are already LF, so no blob may change - verify with `git status` and 10-byte records), then run `DSS_GATE_ONLY=shape_mmf_io` green. Touch only this directory (corpus re-vendor EOL trap).

**Acceptance:**
- The census-driven `mmf_accept_set_fix_is_gated_by_exactly_one_deck`, the new three-step `Cuf` pin and the new `.gitattributes` hygiene test pass in both lanes.

### RF-D03-01 — Correct the G2.1c zero-footprint claim: exclude and pin the negative-rating SeqCurrents divergence
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D03-01", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/report/export/seq_currents.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_reports.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`, `tests/corpus/ledger.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `STATUS.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G2.1c|AC1|AC-1` (major) - the doc comment of `golden_reports.rs::export_seqcurrents_prints_zero_for_an_undefined_rating` ("no committed golden and no gated corpus case moved"), the G2.1c TORN_DOWN/teardown-register prose in `oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs` (search "negative" near the G2.1c row) and the G2.1c record in `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` all claim no gated corpus deck has a negative `normamps`/`emergamps`. That is false: the gated deck `tests/corpus/modes/makeposseq/makeposseq_xfmr.dss` (population.lock row: micro, `engines=capi_v0145`, runf=0) has `AutoTrans.at` (`wdg=1 conn=s kv=4.16`, `wdg=2 conn=w kv=12.47`) whose DERIVED ratings are negative (series vbase = (kV1-kV2)/sqrt3 < 0 in `elements/pd/auto_trans/yterminal.rs` -> normamps -152.848446716868, emergamps -208.429700068457). On it the port's `seq_currents.rs` closure `pct_of_rating` prints 0 in `%Normal`/`%Emergency` while both oracles print the raw rating (r4133 `Version8/Source/Common/ExportResults.pas:355-358`, capi `ExportResults.pas:409-414`); no byte moved only because the corpus gate never compares Export SeqCurrents text for that case. Fix: (1) correct the sentence in the two in-tree code sites to name the deck, the derived values and the real reason; leave the record correction as a §4 note for `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` (and notes for `STATUS.md`/`TESTING.md` only if they repeat the claim - grep first). (2) Add an expected-value pin in `golden_reports.rs`, e.g. `export_seqcurrents_derived_negative_autotrans_rating_prints_zero_pct`: compile+solve the vendored corpus deck, `Export SeqCurrents`, assert the `AutoTrans.at` terminal-1 `%Normal` and `%Emergency` cells are exactly `0` and (sanity leg) that the element's `normamps`/`emergamps` are the negative values above. (3) A `ledger.json` entry / field exclusion is added ONLY if a gated comparison actually sees the cells today (it does not while runf=0); if none is needed, leave `ledger.json` untouched and say so in the record. (4) Record as an OPEN item (state file + record note) whether the negative derived AutoTrans rating is itself an upstream bug (should be `abs`): read r4133 `Version8/Source/PDElements/AutoTrans.pas` rating derivation, cite unit:lines, and if it is a bug write the upstream report locally per R1 and report to the coordinator - do NOT change `yterminal.rs` in this step (not in `files`). Also sync the local-only `investigations/issue-12*` sentence per R1 (main checkout, not committed). `seq_currents.rs` is touched only if its comment repeats the false claim.
**Probes:** dss-python (pinned 0.15.7, `tools/golden/PIN.txt`) and epri-worker r4133 on the deck verbatim: read `AutoTrans.at` `normamps`/`emergamps` and the `Export SeqCurrents` row. Expected on both: raw `-152.848...`/`-208.4297...` in the percent cells. The result decides the literal oracle values quoted in the pin's doc comment and confirms the divergence is shared by both channels (so no channel re-gating helps; exclusion+pin is the only route).
**Acceptance:**
- The new pin exists and passes in both lanes; it fails if `pct_of_rating` is reverted to print the raw rating.
- No occurrence of the false "no gated corpus case/deck" sentence remains in `crates/` (rg proves it); notes left for the three shared docs.
- No golden byte moves; `ledger.json` unchanged, or gains exactly one entry with the pin above named in it.
- The abs-or-not question is recorded with r4133 `AutoTrans.pas` line evidence.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D03-05 — Count per-cell skips in CensusBlindSpots (or narrow its doc) and pin the counting offline
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D03-05", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": [], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/props_census.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `tests/corpus/props_r4133/README.md`
**Findings**
- `RP|RP0.2|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `struct CensusBlindSpots` and `collect_element_divergences`: the struct doc states the invariant "an uncounted skip would make a partial census read like a complete one", but only the whole-element Recloser/Relay drop is counted; the arm `if skip_prop(class, ename, policy.channel()) || skip_transformer_cursor(class, ename, cursors_disagree) { continue; }` never touches `blind`, so the `SKIP_PROPS`/`LANE_SKIP_PROPS` cells and the 13 `TRANSFORMER_CURSOR_PROPS` cells (fires on `asymmetric:transformer/transformer_asym.dss` `Transformer.t3w` and `solvable_now:Test/YgD-Test.dss` `Transformer.tr1`) are dropped silently and appear in no artifact. Fix (preferred): add `skipped_prop_cells` and `skipped_cursor_cells` counters to `CensusBlindSpots`, split the combined `if` so each arm bumps its own counter before `continue`, surface both in the `channels` block of `props_census.json` / `run.json` (at `ab9f0f76` the writer is `corpus_gate/props_census.rs` - the `blind: &BTreeMap<&'static str, CensusBlindSpots>` parameter and the `assert_eq!(blind, CensusBlindSpots::default())` in its tests - and the per-case collection is `corpus_gate/scheduler.rs::census_one` plus the `collected` map above it; both files are in **Files**, and the counters themselves are bumped in `harness/mod.rs::collect_element_divergences`), and add an OFFLINE pin (no oracle, synthetic element/prop input) asserting that a skipped prop cell and a skipped cursor cell each bump exactly their counter and produce no divergence row. Fallback (only if counting is infeasible without a wide refactor): narrow the struct doc to exactly what is counted and record why. Do not regenerate the vendored census under `tests/corpus/props_r4133/` unless the schema change forces it; if it does, say so in the record and leave §4 notes describing the two new fields for `tests/corpus/props_r4133/README.md` and `TESTING.md`.
**Acceptance:**
- The offline counting pin exists and passes in both lanes (or the doc is narrowed with a recorded reason).
- Existing census consumers still parse the artifact (additive fields only); no ledger entry, golden byte or tolerance moves.
- §4 notes left for both shared docs when the artifact schema gained fields.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D03-06 — Golden tooling: repoint the EPRI-bridge citations (lock regen) and guard gen_props.py against frozen-anchor overwrite
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D03-06", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D08-07"], "n_uids": 2} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D08-07.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_lock.rs`, `tests/golden/golden.lock.json`, `tools/golden/README.md`, `tools/golden/gen_flicker.py`, `tools/golden/gen_props.py`, `tools/golden/gen_protection.py`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G0.1|AC3|AC3-2` (note) - the provenance citation `STATUS "EPRI bridge parity round"` (the byte-proof that the r4133 regen reproduces the r3723 payload) no longer resolves: the section moved to `docs/phase-records/epri-bridge.md` in the STATUS archiving round. Four sites: the `flicker/` reason string among the `R4133_FAMILIES` reasons in `tests/golden_lock.rs`, the module docstrings of `tools/golden/gen_flicker.py` and `gen_protection.py`, and `tools/golden/README.md`. The `golden_lock.rs` site is ALREADY repointed by RF-D08-07 (`G|G0.1|AC1|AC1-4`, landed before this step, with its lock regen). Fix here: repoint the three `tools/golden` sites to `docs/phase-records/epri-bridge.md` (section "EPRI bridge parity round"), using the same wording RF-D08-07 wrote into the lock reason; then VERIFY (do not re-edit) that the `golden_lock.rs` flicker reason and the `flicker/pst_demo.json` row of `tests/golden/golden.lock.json` already point at `epri-bridge.md`. Only if that verification fails (RF-D08-07 missed the site) make the `golden_lock.rs` edit and regenerate the lock with the documented `DSS_UPDATE_GOLDEN_LOCK=1` procedure, verifying with `git diff` that ONLY reason strings changed - no digest, no row added or removed (a moved digest means something else is wrong: stop, R8). Expected outcome: no lock byte moves in this step.
- `G|G0.1|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `tools/golden/gen_props.py`: the tool captures every scenario from the pinned capi oracle and then sweeps `for p in OUT_DIR.glob("*.json"): if p.stem not in by_class: p.unlink()`, so a plain run OVERWRITES the re-anchored `props/{relay,recloser,fuse,swtcontrol}.json` and DELETES the four capi015-only files (`autotrans_bh`, `linemedium`, `linespacing_eqspacing`, `transformer_bh` - see `CAPI015_ARTIFACTS` / `CAPI015_REASON` in `golden_lock.rs`, "unreproducible"), with no warning anywhere. Fix: make the tool read `tests/golden/golden.lock.json` and never write or unlink a `props/*.json` whose lock row is anchored `capi015` or `self` (use the lock's actual anchor field/values - check the schema in `golden_lock.rs`; also cover the r4133-cross-validated rows the settlement re-anchored): skip with a loud stderr notice, and only proceed under an explicit `--force-frozen` flag; add the warning to the module docstring and one line to `tools/golden/README.md`. Validate without the oracle if possible (a dry-run/`--list` path or a small pure function + a python self-check); do NOT run a real regen. Leave a §4 note for `TESTING.md` §"Regenerate a golden" naming all eight affected files (today it names only relay/recloser).
**Acceptance:**
- `rg "EPRI bridge parity round"` shows every hit pointing at `docs/phase-records/epri-bridge.md`; `golden_lock` tests green in both lanes; the lock is untouched (expected, RF-D08-07 repointed it) or, in the fallback only, its diff is reason-text only.
- `gen_props.py` refuses to overwrite/unlink frozen-anchor files without `--force-frozen` (demonstrated by a dry run or self-check recorded in the step record); no file under `tests/golden/props/` changes.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D03-07 — Re-probe the 59 A-Diakoptics off:save-roundtrip skips, flip the ones that pass, add a stale-skip probe
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D03-07", "effort": "max", "parts": 3, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/manifest.rs`, `docs/phase-records/part2-adiakoptics.md`, `tests/corpus/manifests/ad_sweep.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `STATUS.md`, `TESTING.md`
**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.11|AT1|AT-3` (minor) - `tests/corpus_gate.rs::corpus_ad_matches_normal_mode`: an `off:` disposition is only checked against `manifest.rs::AD_OFF_REASONS` and then counted (`_ => off += 1`), never run. 59 cases in `tests/corpus/manifests/ad_sweep.json` carry `off:save-roundtrip-*` (relpath 24, regxfmr 17, relay 8, geometry 4, autotrans 3, userdll 2, control 1) whose documented causes (`docs/phase-records/part2-adiakoptics.md`, "regulator/transformer state + LineGeometry/WireData ordering + relative data-file paths") are exactly what RP3.11 P3/P4/P7 fixed in Save; at least `Test/CapacitorConfigs.dss` no longer reproduces. Fix: re-probe all 59 at HEAD, flip every case whose save round-trip and AD comparison pass to the strongest disposition that passes (`full`, else `pf`), re-justify the rest with the measured cause, and add an opt-in stale-skip probe (env-gated like `DSS_AD_CLASSIFY`, e.g. `DSS_AD_STALE_SKIPS=1`; it must RUN every `off:save-roundtrip-*` case and red when one passes; when the env var is unset it does nothing - it is a probe, not a filtered gate). No tolerance change (R8); an `AD_OFF_REASONS` entry left with zero users is removed.
**Parts:**
1. P1 - build the env-gated stale-skip probe in `corpus_gate.rs` (reuse `ad_run_case_abs` / the `AD DECOMPOSE` leg-1 save-round-trip measurement, catching per-case failure instead of aborting the sweep), run it over the 59, commit the probe, and hand over the per-case outcome list (case, reason, leg1/leg2 gaps, pass/fail, first error) in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D03-07.md`.
2. P2 - flip every passing case in `ad_sweep.json` (`Test/CapacitorConfigs.dss` first), run `corpus_ad_matches_normal_mode` and the corpus gate on them, triage any new divergence: a ledger entry only with measured evidence and a pin. Deterministic rule for a case whose round trip fails for a port-Save reason (engine files are outside **Files**, so no engine edit happens here and nothing halts): keep it `off:` under the new measured reason `off:save-roundtrip-port-bug` (register it in `AD_OFF_REASONS`), write the deck, the first error and - where the r4133 probe below was run - its verdict into the state file; the coordinator turns that list into a follow-up RF engine step in the next wave (a found gap is fixed in this programme, never parked to a later plan). Commit; hand over the flipped list and the port-bug list.
3. P3 - re-justify each remaining `off:` reason per measured cause (rename to a more exact existing reason where the measurement says so), prune unused `AD_OFF_REASONS`, update the `docs/phase-records/part2-adiakoptics.md` bucket text and counts, leave §4 notes for `STATUS.md` / `TESTING.md` (new env knob, new full/pf/off counts).
**Probes:** the measurement is in-engine (normal mode vs save round-trip vs A-Diakoptics; the reference is the port's own normal-mode solve, itself oracle-gated by the corpus gate). Use epri-worker r4133 only when a flipped case shows a Save divergence whose correct behaviour is unclear: run r4133 `Save Circuit` + recompile on that deck and compare the re-solved voltages - that decides "port Save bug" (fix) vs "deck genuinely not round-trippable" (keep `off:` with the measured reason).
**Acceptance:**
- The stale-skip probe exists, is documented in the record, and with its env var set is green at the end of P3 (no passing case still marked `off:save-roundtrip-*`).
- `ad_sweep.json` census before/after is recorded (59 -> N, by reason); every remaining `off:` has a measured justification in `part2-adiakoptics.md`; every `off:save-roundtrip-port-bug` case is listed in the state file (deck, first error, r4133 verdict where probed) for the coordinator's follow-up engine step - none is silently left `off:` under the old reason.
- No tolerance loosened; no golden byte moves; any ledger entry added has a named pin.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D03-08 — Re-justify the two WindGenerator hold-outs on the r4133 channel (promote or re-tag)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D03-08", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D03-01", "RF-D08-03"], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D03-01, RF-D08-03.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`, `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, `tests/corpus/manifests/skipped_oracle_issue.json`, `tests/corpus/manifests/solvable_now.json`, `tests/corpus/manifests/population.lock.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `TESTING.md` (population counts, promote branch only)
**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.2|AC1|AC-3` (minor) - `tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`: `const RP32_SKIPPED_TAG = "capi015_multistep_limitation"`, the census assertion that pins it (message "the hold-out reason must still be the oracle's multi-step limitation") and the doc block that references `[RP32_SKIPPED_TAG]` justify RP3.2's "exactly four entries" closure by a capi015-only argument. The two held-out decks (`Version8/Distrib/Examples/WindGenerator/WindGen_GFL_Dynamics/Run_IEEE123Bus_GFLDaily.DSS` and `.../WindGen_QSTS/Run_IEEE123Bus_GFLDaily.DSS` in `tests/corpus/manifests/skipped_oracle_issue.json`, notes dated 2026-07-17) have no capi channel at all (dss_capi 0.14.5 has no WindGen class) and would be gated on r4133, where multi-step cases are gated routinely. Fix: measure on r4133 and then either (a) PROMOTE both to r4133-gated multi-step cases and re-derive the RP3.2 cells, ledger entries and pins (`props_r4133_pins.rs`), or (b) if r4133 gating is genuinely blocked, RE-TAG: a non-capi tag carrying the measured r4133-side reason in `skipped_oracle_issue.json`, `RP32_SKIPPED_TAG`, its assertion message and the doc block updated to match. The notes also name a WindGen daily `Losses`-staleness quirk: if it shows on r4133 it is an upstream bug - not reproduced, cells excluded field-by-field and pinned (R3), never a reason to hold the whole deck out.
**Parts:**
1. P1 - probe both decks multi-step through epri-worker r4133 (compile, snapshot, then the deck's own `set mode=dynamics ...` / `set mode=daily number=24` sequence) and on the port; record convergence, step count, per-step capture fidelity (does the bridge re-nominalize time-varying elements like the capi015 server did?) and the first divergence, in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D03-08.md`; decide promote-vs-retag from that evidence. No repo edit is required in P1 beyond the state file.
2. P2 - execute the decision. Promote: move the two rows from `skipped_oracle_issue.json` into `tests/corpus/manifests/solvable_now.json` with `"engines": "r4133"` and the deck's own multi-step depth (the manifest's existing r4133-only multi-step rows are the model), then regenerate `tests/corpus/manifests/population.lock.json` with the documented `DSS_UPDATE_POPULATION_LOCK=1` procedure (`population_lock.rs`; TESTING.md §corpus hygiene) - both files are in **Files**; verify with `git diff` that only the two new rows and their lock tokens moved (anything else moving = stop under R8 and record). Population counts quoted in docs go to the coordinator as §4 notes (`TESTING.md`), not as edits. Every new ledger entry gets an expected-value pin, carries `measured` (+`source`) provenance taken from the gate run and - the existing windgen entries are element sub-channel exclusions, so the new ones carry element `channels` too - moves `DEAD_CHANNEL_EXEMPT_CENSUS` in `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs` in the same commit (the RF-D08-03 rule, landed before this step; that file is in this step's files for that constant only). Re-tag: edit the four files in scope only (~60 lines). Leave the §4 note for `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md` (RP3.2 closure wording).
**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 on the two decks above, multi-step. Decides: (1) can the r4133 channel gate them (bridge captures true per-step state, run time acceptable for the unconditional corpus gate) -> promote; (2) blocked for a measured reason (name it: e.g. bridge capture limitation, run time, deck aborts on r4133) -> re-tag with that reason; (3) whether the `Losses` staleness exists on r4133 -> exclusion + pin + upstream report per R1/R3.
**Acceptance:**
- No hold-out in `skipped_oracle_issue.json` for these two decks is justified by a capi015-only argument; `RP32_SKIPPED_TAG` (if it survives) names a measured r4133-side reason and its census test passes in both lanes.
- Promote branch: both decks run in `corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines` on `r4133`; ledger gains only entries that are hit, pinned and carry `measured` provenance, with the census constant moved in the same commit (fail-on-stale stays green); population counts in docs are noted for the coordinator.
- No tolerance loosened (R8); the probe transcript location is named in the record.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

Wrong sum under item 1 (960 129 + 888 = 961 017; 961 031 folds item 2's +14). Item 1 prints 210 pairs / 961 017 cells; move 961 031 after item 2, matching `docs/plans-archive/R4133_PROPS_PLAN.md` (the "Corrected totals" passage).
- `RP|RP2.3|AT2|AT2-3` (note) - README RP2.3 section, boxed "CORRECTION (RP2.3 audit settlement, 2026-08-23)". It still presents per-cell narrowing as a pending RP4.1 precondition. Register the real gap instead: `fuse.pas:577` `i<FUSEMAXDIM` honours 5 tokens while `fuse/accessors.rs` writes up to 6; fix the "How to do it" premise and mark the 5-token cap an upstream off-by-one candidate (policy: not reproduced unless proven intended - no engine change in this step). Write `NN-savewrite-sizing-property-last.md` to cover the whole class from that transcript plus RP3.11's: one mechanism (`TDSSObject.SaveWrite` walking properties in set order, so the sizing property lands after its array), the `TCC_Curve` / `GrowthShape` / `Spectrum` curve classes from RP3.11's evidence plus Capacitor `NumSteps`, Seasons->Ratings, LineSpacing `NConds` and whatever else D01-04's table lists, one repro per class family. Write one note each: (1) r4133 has no `TXfmrCodeObj.SaveWrite`, so a saved multi-winding XfmrCode keeps only the active winding and silently re-compiles into a different converging circuit (port side: module doc of `crates/dss-core/src/elements/general/xfmr_code/save.rs`); (2) `TDSSObject.SaveWrite` prints `npts` twice for a LoadShape that parses no array property, `NptsRdy` being tested only on the non-restart advance (r4133 `Version8/Source/General/DSSObject.pas:158-172`); (3) `TCC_Curve` / `GrowthShape` / `Spectrum` emit their sizing property last, so those lines reload as zeros; (4) r4133 has no `UserDynInit`, so `Save` drops every DynamicEq state-variable initializer. Evidence sources: `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D01-04.md` and its transcript (for (3)), `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md` §RP3.11 (+ its settlement), the doc comments under `crates/dss-core/src/report/save/`, the RP3.11 pins in `exec/tests/report.rs` / `props_r4133_pins.rs`, and main-checkout `tmp/rp311*` transcripts if present; open the cited Pascal to quote exact lines for (1), (3), (4).
