### RF-D06-01 — Give the reliability compare real liveness rails and drive its settlement asserts
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-01", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D08-02"], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D08-02.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/reliability_pins.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G1.6(i)|AT2|AT2-2` (minor) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `check_reliability_compare_ran` + `compare_reliability`: the fail-on-nothing-ran rail counts only payloads/meters, so a surface that compares `0 == 0` everywhere (RelCalc drive lost on all engines) stays green. Fix: add a value-level liveness counter to `compare_reliability` (meters carrying a non-zero reliability scalar / non-empty sections), print it in the `corpus_gate.rs` epilogue, assert `> 0` per channel in `check_reliability_compare_ran`, and pin both directions over injected counters like the sibling distance / short-circuit rails. TESTING.md wording goes to a §4 note.
- `G|G1.6(i)|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `compare_reliability`: the multi-line `RelCalc` error check and the `band.is_some()` denominator gate are exercised by no test (one caller, no offline drive). Fix: extract the per-cell decision into a pure fn that runs without a live oracle payload and drive it from `tests/reliability_pins.rs` (the binary without the gate epilogue): one `should_panic` for a two-message RelCalc outcome, one for the `band == None` gate, plus the passing equal-cell arm of SA-1 below.
- `G|G1.6(ii)|AT2|AT2-2` (minor) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `reliability_tests::every_bus_reliability_column_is_compared_per_bus`: it drives `compare_bus_reliability` from the `#[cfg(test)]` module that compiles into the corpus_gate binary whose epilogue asserts the same process-global counters, against the absolute rule in the `bus_reliability_walk_counters` doc; its safety rests on unpinned counter placement. Fix: move the test into `tests/reliability_pins.rs`; if something forces it to stay, qualify the rule and pin that a failed compare leaves the `BUS_RELIABILITY_*` statics unmoved (read before/after the `should_panic` drives).
- `G|G1.6(ii)|AC2|AC2-4` (note) - same site, the rule text "Never drive compare_bus_reliability from a #[cfg(test)] test in this module": violated in letter by the settlement's own drive and never amended. Fix: closed by the move above (rule then holds verbatim - confirm no other same-module drive remains); only if the test stays, amend the rule to the exact invariant (a same-module drive is allowed only when it panics before the counters are bumped) and make that structural.
- `G|G1.6(i)|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `compare_reliability` denominator gate: `assert!(band.is_some(), ..)` runs even when the cell already compared bit-identical, so an equal cell with `|I| < i_abs` false-fails. Fix: assert `band.is_some()` only when `!rel_num_eq(x, y)`; pin "equal cell with |I| < i_abs does not panic" with the SA-2 drive.
- `G|G1.6(ii)|AT2|AT2-7` (note) - `tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `assert_structural`: the per-bus and per-meter halves share the one `reliability` ledger field, so a bare `{"field":"reliability"}` exclusion written for a meter cause would silently drop all eight bus columns too. Fix: structural rule - a `reliability` scope must carry a `name_re`. RF-D08-02 (in **After**) introduces exactly this refusal shape for bare `distance` scopes in `LedgerRuntime::load()` (`G|G1.4b|AC1|AC-1`, "consider the other `PER_VALUE_EXCLUSION_FIELDS` too") and moves the scope-channel checks into the loader (`G|G1.0|AT2|AT2-2`): add `reliability` to D08-02's bare-scope refusal list on that same loader path and add the negative drive in its pattern - do not write a second check. State the both-direction isolation in the `compare_reliability` doc and leave the TESTING.md sentence as a §4 note. (If the rule breaks an existing ledger entry, fall back to the doc-only warning and record why.)
- `G|G1.6(i)|AT2|AT2-3` (note) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, zone-list ORDER panic text + `compare_reliability` doc: claims all three lists come from `sequence_list()` (`solution/meters/zones/build.rs`), but only `branches` does; `ends` comes from `zone_end_elements()` and `pce` from `zone_pce()` (`exec/view.rs`). Fix: cite the producer per list in both the message and the doc.
- `G|G1.6(i)|AC2|AC2-2` (note) - `tests/harness/mod.rs` (two sites) and `tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`: stale `solution/meters/reliability.rs:293` (the unguarded AverageRepairTime division, now ~:327) and `:53-57` (per-meter error collection, now ~:88-92). Fix: re-point by naming the statement / loop rather than a line number.

**Acceptance:**
- `check_reliability_compare_ran` fails on zero live-value meters per channel, pinned in both directions over injected counters; the epilogue prints the new count.
- `tests/reliability_pins.rs` holds the two `should_panic` drives, the equal-cell-below-`i_abs` pass, and `every_bus_reliability_column_is_compared_per_bus`; no `compare_*reliability` drive remains in `harness/mod.rs`'s test module (or the rule is amended and pinned).
- A bare `reliability` ledger scope is refused structurally (negative drive), or the doc-only fallback is recorded.
- No ledger entry, golden byte or tolerance moves; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D06-02 — Fix the alloc_factors MeteredTerminal offset, the zero-peakcurrent skip and the reliability measurement docs
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-02", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D06-01"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-01.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/reliability_pins.rs`, `tools/oracle/oracle_server.py`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Findings** (the first three are one defect - fix once, close all three)
- `G|G1.6(i)|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `reliability_array_band`: the `alloc_factors` band takes `i_ref` from `calc_current[k]`, but upstream divides by `Cabs(CalculatedCurrent[i + (MeteredTerminal-1)*NConds])`; for `MeteredTerminal > 1` the band is the image of a current that was never the divisor and can go silently loose. Fix: carry `metered_terminal`/`nconds` (or the offset current itself) in `MeterReliabilityView` (`exec/view.rs`) and index `calc_current[k + iOffset]`; if the oracle payload cannot supply the offset cell, make `compare_reliability` panic loudly on any meter with `MeteredTerminal != 1` instead. Fix the derivation text above `reliability_array_band` and the "both took ELSE" comment; add an offline pin for the terminal > 1 arm in `tests/reliability_pins.rs`.
- `G|G1.6(i)|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - same defect, code-audit view: latent today (all six gating decks use terminal=1) and nothing asserts it. Fix: as above; cite r4133 `MeterElement.pas:63-66` (`iOffset`) in the doc; pin with a terminal=2 fixture; the `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` formula correction goes to a §4 note.
- `G|G1.6(i)|AT3|AT3-2` (minor) - same defect, doc view (`tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` derivation + the `MeterReliabilityView` doc in `exec/view.rs`): the Pascal quote omits `i+iOffset` and the view doc's "writes at that offset" mechanism statement is wrong; nothing refuses a `terminal != 1` deck. Fix: correct the view doc in `exec/view.rs` here; leave the TOLERANCE_NOTES quote fix (r4133 `MeterElement.pas:60-67`) as a §4 note.
- `G|G1.6(i)|AC2|AC2-4` (note) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `reliability_skip_applies`: documented as an iff for "CalcAllocationFactors never ran" but also true after a real AllocateLoads with `SensorCurrent == 0` (`peakcurrent=0`), silently dropping `calc_current` and `alloc_factors` on both channels. Fix: see AC3-1 (one change); additionally amend the `RELIABILITY_SKIP_FIELDS` table doc to name the `peakcurrent=0` case, and add the offline pin "all-zero alloc_factors with non-zero calc_current is NOT read as never-ran".
- `G|G1.6(i)|AC3|AC3-1` (minor) - same predicate vs `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` ("both arrays still exactly zero"): the shipped predicate reads only `alloc_factors` and drops `calc_current` with it. Fix: make the predicate match the documented regime - require BOTH port arrays all-zero, or make it per-field so `calc_current` is skipped only when itself all-zero; unit pin `alloc_factors=[0,0,0]` + non-zero `calc_current` -> `calc_current` IS compared; sync the "Honest limit" doc; TOLERANCE_NOTES sentence as a §4 note.
- `G|G1.6(ii)|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - `compare_bus_reliability` doc in `tests/harness/mod.rs` + three shared docs: the "400/400 cells equal under ==" measurement is attributed three mutually exclusive ways. Fix: state it once, and state BOTH facts so the later doc-owner reading (RF-D04-03 `G|G1.6(ii)|AT2|AT2-6`: "400/400 is capi's own count, 50 buses x 8; the channels share 44 buses = 352 cells") cannot contradict it: 400 = capi's own count on the four capi-gated decks (midi_energymeter 35, midi_relcalc 5, makeposseq_ctrl 6, midi_duty_ctrl 4 = 50 buses x 8 columns), each run on both engines for the measurement; the cross-channel intersection is 44 buses = 352 cells; the two r4133-only combo decks (40 buses / 320 cells) were never cross-oracle compared. Confirm both counts against the F4 measurement notes under `tmp/g1` (main checkout) before wording; if either is unconfirmable, say "population unconfirmed" for that number in the record rather than guess (this step has no oracle budget). Harness doc here; TESTING / GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN / TOLERANCE_NOTES / golden-rebase record as §4 notes carrying that exact sentence, so D04-03 AT2-6 becomes "verify".
- `G|G1.6(ii)|SA1|SA-2` (note) - NaN mechanism docs + `tools/oracle/oracle_server.py`: on the capi transport a bare `NaN` token breaks the outer `Resp` line parse (skipped as a stray line -> 120 s timeout, one retry, "oracle worker failed twice"), not the caps' field decode as documented. Fix: make it loud and fast - `allow_nan=False` on the server's `json.dumps` emit, returning an error `Resp` on `ValueError`; extend `a_non_finite_reliability_cell_fails_the_decode_on_both_transports` to the Resp-level path; corrected mechanism text for TOLERANCE_NOTES / TESTING as §4 notes. If `allow_nan=False` trips a legitimately non-finite payload elsewhere in the gate, revert it, keep the doc correction, and record why.

**Acceptance:**
- A `MeteredTerminal > 1` meter is either banded from `calc_current[k + iOffset]` or refused loudly; the chosen arm is pinned offline in `tests/reliability_pins.rs`.
- The zero-`alloc_factors` / non-zero-`calc_current` pin exists and passes; `reliability_skip_applies` and its doc agree.
- The NaN pin covers the Resp-level path; the corpus gate stays green in both lanes with the server change (no ledger entry, tolerance or golden moves).
- §4 notes left for all four shared docs; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D06-03 — Add fail-on-stale liveness to LANE_SKIP_PROPS and harden the monitor-pad rail
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-03", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D06-02", "RF-D09-07"], "n_uids": 4} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-02, RF-D09-07.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/lane.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`
**Findings** (both pairs install the same pattern: hit counter + pure check core + gate-epilogue assert)
- `G|G2.2b|AC1|AC-1` (note) - `tests/harness/mod.rs`, `LANE_SKIP_PROPS` / `skip_prop`'s lane arm: the exclusion G2.2b made unconditional has no fail-on-stale guard, so a mask that stops being hit (LVTestCase retired or re-classified `kind: large`, `corpus_gate/scheduler.rs::force_properties` narrowed) survives silently. Fix: per-row visit/hit `AtomicUsize` counters bumped where the lane arm masks (hit = the two values actually differed), an `assert_lane_skip_props_are_live()` modelled on `assert_pd_skip_rows_are_live` (guarded by surface-ran, silent under `DSS_GATE_ONLY`) called from the `corpus_gate.rs` epilogue after the full walk, and both directions pinned over injected counters.
- `G|G2.2b|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, G2.2b record "REFUTED" disposition: the refutation covers only direction A (mask removed => gate reds), not direction B (mask goes inert). Fix: the rail above closes direction B; leave a §4 note amending the G2.2b disposition to "direction A was loud; direction B railed by RF-D06-03" and a TESTING.md note naming the new assert.
- `G|G2.4|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - `tests/harness/lane.rs`, `assert_monitor_pad_is_live`: it asserts only `misses == 0`, so the transform can become dead code unnoticed, and its counters are charged inside the shared transform, so the two in-binary unit tests pollute the hit counter. Fix: build on RF-D09-07 (in **After**, `lane.rs`), which already extracts a pure `check_monitor_pad(hits, misses) -> Result` tested in both verdicts and routes the transform through `expected_monitor_channel_with(&hits, &misses, ..)` so unit tests never touch the global statics - do NOT re-extract or change its signature shape: extend that core with the `carriers_visited` floor (`hits > 0` whenever a `modes:time/generaltime*` carrier was visited; silent only when none was), and, if the counting still sits inside the shared transform after D09-07, move it to the gate call site in `harness/mod.rs` (the `expected_monitor_channel` caller; decision D24: count at the call site); pin the new direction offline through D09-07's injectable core. Read D09-07's record in `docs/phase-records/retro-fixes.md` first.
- `G|G2.4|AT1|AT-3` (note) - `tests/harness/lane.rs`, the monitor-pad transform's shape test (doc: "insists the capture really is the placeholder"): it cannot tell the client pad from a genuine one-sample zero channel and is applied corpus-wide, so a port flush regression (`flushed_records` wrongly 0) against a genuine single-0.0 capture is masked in both lanes. Fix: scope the normalization to the three measured `modes:time/generaltime*` case labels, passed into `expected_monitor_channel` like the per-case event-log narrowing earlier in `lane.rs`; add a unit test for a non-carrier label. RF-D09-07 `G|G2.4|SA1|SA-4` already rewrote the `flushed_records` doc with the residual silent-pass window: verify that rewrite covers the genuine one-sample-zero case, add one sentence only if it does not (no second doc rewrite). Only if scoping proves impossible, soften the doc claim and record why.

**Acceptance:**
- `assert_lane_skip_props_are_live` runs in the gate epilogue and is pinned fire/silent over injected counters; the `Monitor.BaseFreq` row is measured live (hit > 0) on the full gate.
- The monitor-pad rail is two-directional via RF-D09-07's pure core (extended, not re-extracted) pinned offline; unit tests no longer move the gate counters; a non-carrier label is not normalized (unit test).
- No ledger entry, golden byte or tolerance moves; `pwsh -File tools/lanes/lane_diff.ps1` is run because `lane.rs` changes (expect the measured Δ unchanged).
- §4 notes left for `TESTING.md` and the G2.2b record; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D06-04 — Pin the D11(2) suppression set, the SC Voc/Isc liveness and make `variables` exclusions hit-on-mask
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-04", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D06-03", "RF-D08-01"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-03, RF-D08-01.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`, `tests/corpus/ledger.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G1.4a|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `tests/corpus_gate.rs` epilogue, the D11(2) bus-array suppression print: the "8 (case, channel) pairs" written into the record and TESTING.md are only printed, never enforced, so the normalization can widen silently. Closed by RF-D08-01 `G|G1.4a|AT1|AT1` (in **After**): it lands `BUS_ARRAY_SUPPRESSIONS` (sorted (case, channel) list, `TOPOLOGY_STALE_DECLINES` precedent) asserted against `run.ledger.bus_array_suppressions()` on an unfiltered run. Here: verify that constant exists, is asserted in BOTH directions and self-silences under `DSS_GATE_ONLY`; do not add a second constant or re-freeze the population. Extend only what D08-01 left open (offline fire/silent drives if absent; entry-level granularity `(case, channel, entry)` if the list is per (case, channel) only) and say in the record which of these were already there; TESTING.md names the constant via a §4 note only if D08-01's note did not.
- `G|G1.5|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - `compare_bus_short_circuit` (`harness/mod.rs`) + epilogue: fail-on-stale counts only buses with a full n x n matrix, so the Voc/Isc half (least headroom, the only arm D11(2) suppresses) could compare `0 == 0` unnoticed. Fix: count buses compared with a NON-ZERO Voc (and, separately, Isc), expose the counters, assert the measured population fail-on-stale in the epilogue (DistFromMeter style, silent under `DSS_GATE_ONLY`), with offline fire/silent drives. One full gate run measures the floor.
- `RP|RP3.10|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - `harness/mod.rs`, `compare_variables`: `skip` is consulted BEFORE the tolerance check, so `Scope::hit` means "matched a name", not "masked something", and a dead `variables` exclusion is never reported stale. Fix: evaluate the tolerance first and consult `skip` only after the compare failed (the `distance` field's pattern), keeping the name-vs-oracle-index assert on the masked path; update the `variables` field doc in `corpus_gate/ledger.rs` (TESTING.md via note); unit pin "an in-floor variable named by an exclusion leaves its scope un-hit -> stale"; then run the corpus gate and prune from `tests/corpus/ledger.json` any WTG3 `variables` exclusion that proves dead (R8: prune only, never add an unpinned entry).
- `RP|RP3.10|AC1|AC-5` (note) - same comparator + RP3.10 record: the record credits the name-safety assert with proving both engines spell the 22 variables identically, but it runs only on the 3-4 excluded indices. Fix: hoist the `eq_ignore_ascii_case` port-vs-oracle name assert out of the `if skip(..)` branch so every index is name-checked; if a compared name legitimately differs, record it explicitly and leave a §4 note rewording the record sentence.
- `G|G1.5|AC2|AC2-2` (minor) - `src/exec/view.rs`, doc block of the Voc pin ("a live Voc (and an Isc derived from it)"): Isc is never refreshed outside a fault study - `ComputeIsc` has one call site, `SolveFaultStudy` (r4133 `SolutionAlgs.pas:904`) - so on a harmonics deck Isc stays the allocation zero. Fix: correct the doc; qualify the `Isc = Ysc*Voc` identity used as the D11(2) rationale in the `corpus_gate.rs` comment and the `compare_bus_short_circuit` doc as "true at study time only; a later PreserveNodeVoltages rebuild moves Voc, not Isc" (TOLERANCE_NOTES via note); optionally extend the Voc pin to assert `isc == 0` on the harmonics deck.
- `G|G1.5|AC1|AC3` (note) - `compare_bus_short_circuit`, the circuit-level "study ran" bit: `port_ran` is a per-circuit `views.iter().any(..)` while `oracle_ran` reads ONE witness bus, so a legal partial allocation (ZscRefresh-style, unported today) would false-red. Fix: derive `port_ran` from the same witness bus (first bus with >= 2 nodes); add a fixture pin with a partial allocation.
- `G|G1.5|AT1|AT1-1` (minor) - `bus_short_circuit_tests`: `Zsc0` is the only value arm with no non-vacuity drive. Fix: add `a_corrupted_zsc0_reds_the_short_circuit_comparator` beside the zsc1 drive - bump `e.zsc0` past the band -> `should_panic`, keep a 90 %-of-band leg green.

**Acceptance:**
- RF-D08-01's D11(2) constant is verified two-directional and `DSS_GATE_ONLY`-silent (gaps closed here are named in the record); the Voc/Isc census is asserted in the epilogue and pinned fire/silent offline with the value measured on this step's full gate run.
- `compare_variables` is hit-on-mask with its unit pin; every variable index is name-checked; `ledger.json` changes only by removal of proven-dead `variables` exclusions (each named in the record), fail-on-stale stays green.
- `a_corrupted_zsc0_reds_the_short_circuit_comparator` and the partial-allocation pin exist and pass.
- No tolerance or golden moves; §4 notes left; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D06-05 — Drive the untested element derived/seq/cplx-seq arms and bound the ledger-rewritten seq band
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-05", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D06-04"], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-04.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Parts:**
1. The six comparator drives in `harness/mod.rs` - `G|G1.3a|AT2|AT2-1`, `G|G1.3a|AT2|AT2-2`, `G|G1.3b|AC1|AC1-3`, `G|G1.3b|AT1|AT1-3`, `G|G1.3c|AC1|AC4`, `G|G1.3c|AT1|AT1-4` (the `PropsChannel` threading and the `ElementCap`-shaped fixtures); unit tests green, full gate green (the sentinel rejects need the live corpus gate once); commit. Hands over the fixture helpers it built and any r4133 case that published a sentinel (stop condition of AT1-3).
2. `G|G1.3b|AT2|AT2-1` + `G|G1.3b|SA1|SA-2`: the ledger-side bound pin in `corpus_gate/ledger.rs` and the band-base decision (recorded; R8 if either choice needs an unpinned ledger entry), the CapControl fixture, the in-code "ULP-scale" correction, the §4 note; gate green; commit.
**Findings**
- `G|G1.3a|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `harness/mod.rs`, `compare_element_derived`: the three polar branches (`currents_mag_ang`, `voltages_mag_ang`, `residuals`) are executed by no committed test. Fix: in `derived_polar_floors` add a non-zero-terminal fixture (yorder > 0) reaching the three `if channels.*` blocks - one `should_panic` per channel, one green in-band leg, one cleared-selector leg, modelled on the G1.4a drives.
- `G|G1.3a|AT2|AT2-2` (minor) - `derived_polar_floors`, `const REL: f64 = 1e-7; const ABS: f64 = 1e-5;`: bare literals nothing ties to `tol_for("feeder")`. Fix: read `i_rel`/`i_abs` from `tol_for("feeder")`, or add a tier-lock assert equating them, so a tier change reds the pins. Never change the tier itself.
- `G|G1.3b|AC1|AC1-3` (note) - `compare_element_seq`, the `match (channel, arm)` that applies `SEQ_C012`: the only combination where it enters, (R4133, ThreePhase), is never driven end-to-end. Fix: add to `seq_floors` an acceptance/rejection pair - an error sized between the capi band and the capi band + `SEQ_C012 * max|Xph|` passes on R4133 and `should_panic`s on CapiV0145.
- `G|G1.3b|AT1|AT1-3` (minor) - `no_seq_payload`: accepts capi's one-element `DefaultResult` `[0.0]` sentinel on BOTH channels although r4133's measured shape is all-empty. Fix: give it a `PropsChannel` argument (sentinel on CapiV0145 only, all-empty on R4133); extend `the_capi_default_result_sentinel_is_the_only_extra_zero_terminal_shape` with an R4133 leg that reds on the sentinel; the live gate confirms no r4133 case publishes it (if one does, stop and record - do not re-loosen silently).
- `G|G1.3c|AC1|AC4` (minor) - `no_cplx_seq_payload`: looser than its documented measurement and channel-blind (accepts `cseq_i = ([0.0], [])`, and capi's `cseq_v` sentinel on r4133). Fix: current half must be empty on both channels; voltage half may be `([0.0],[])` only on capi, empty on r4133 (thread `PropsChannel` from `compare_element_cplx_seq`, as `na_seq_power` does); extend `the_zero_terminal_cplx_seq_shapes_are_the_measured_ones` with the `([0.0],[],[],[])` reject and the r4133-with-capi-sentinel reject. This tightens, never loosens.
- `G|G1.3c|AT1|AT1-4` (note) - `compare_element_total_powers`: the conductor-layout ties documented as "asserted under every channel policy" sit after the `if !channels.total_powers { return; }` early return, so they never run on the two `newton*` decks. Fix: move the ties (nconds > 0, `NTerms*NConds == p_kw.len`, and the `p_kw`/`p_kvar`/`i_re`/`i_im` length equalities) above the early return and add a `should_panic` fixture with the bit cleared that truncates `exp.p_kvar`. If the move reds on the newton decks, correct the doc instead and say why.
- `G|G1.3b|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` §G1.3b derivation 1 + `corpus_gate/ledger.rs`: the "ULP-scale" premise for taking the seq band base from a ledger-rewritten polar capture is false for the EXCLUSION `capi-capcontrol-time-bus-is-the-capacitors` (0.90 % base shift), and the rule is reusable unbounded. Fix: ledger-side pin - every entry rewriting a polar sub-channel while leaving `seq_*` compared either has envelope `max_rel <= 1e-8` or sits in a named, exactly-asserted register; §4 note for TOLERANCE_NOTES naming that exclusion and stating the bound.
- `G|G1.3b|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `compare_element_seq` band-base doc ("ULP-scale ... last digits") in `harness/mod.rs`: the settlement answered with documentation only and the claim is falsified by the same entry. Fix (pick one, record which): compute the `seq_powers` band from the un-rewritten oracle capture, or make ledger validation require `seq_powers` be scoped whenever `voltages_mag_ang`/`seq_voltages` are rewritten on an element with non-zero terminal current; pin it with a CapControl fixture with zero SeqPowers; correct the in-code "ULP-scale" claim. Keep it consistent with the AT2-1 register pin; R8 applies if either choice would need a new unpinned ledger entry.

**Acceptance:**
- New drives exist and pass: three polar `should_panic`s + green + cleared-selector legs, the (R4133, ThreePhase) pair, the R4133 sentinel rejects for `no_seq_payload` and `no_cplx_seq_payload`, the total-powers tie fixture.
- The ledger-side bound pin exists and the current `ledger.json` satisfies it unchanged (this step's `files` do not include the ledger); the full corpus gate stays green in both lanes.
- No tolerance is loosened (`tol_for("feeder")` untouched); no golden byte moves.
- §4 note left for `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D06-06 — Witness the bus sequence/at-bus arms where the oracle declines and lock PdElementCap's field census
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-06", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D06-05"], "n_uids": 10} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-05.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/manifest.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-epri/src/capture.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G1.4c|AC1|AC1-1` (minor) - `harness/mod.rs`, `compare_bus_seq_and_vll`: the sequence arm's port-side availability rule is still gated on `!declines`, so where an oracle channel declines the sequence pair nothing is asserted about whether the PORT answers (the L-L twin was hoisted by settlement 6fc63848, the sequence twin was not). Fix: hoist it, mirroring the L-L arm - assert `v.cplx_seq_voltages.is_some() == (phase_nodes_present(&nodes) == 3)` (and `seq_voltages` likewise) on EVERY bus; add a `should_panic` drive with a 2-node bus whose port view answers and an n > 3 r4133 drive. Re-measure `SEQ_GROUND_SUBSTITUTION_POPULATION` only if its counting moves.
- `G|G1.4c|AC1|AC1-3` (note) - same fn, the `match walk` classification: the two `assert_eq!(channel, ..)` rails in the `SecondLoopHang` and `CapiDefault` arms are tautologies over a two-variant enum (they restate the `bounded` argument). Fix: remove or reword them and correct the closing comment's count of asserting branches; alternatively derive the class from the payload so the rail can red.
- `G|G1.4c|AT1|AT1-1` (minor) - `a_perturbed_sequence_or_line_to_line_value_reds_the_comparator`: no negative drive reds the SeqVoltages / CplxSeqVoltages VALUE arms on r4133, the only channel whose band carries the `SEQ_C012` term. Fix: run both perturbation legs on `PropsChannel::R4133` too (3-node bus so r4133 answers), with a step sized past the band INCLUDING `SEQ_C012 * max_vph`, plus an in-band leg between the capi and r4133 bands.
- `G|G1.4c|SA1|SA-4` (note) - the `FirstLoopHang` comparator arm: converted from an unconditional panic into a conditional assert with no drive. Fix: build the class with a hand-made view/capture pair (node list all < 1, bypassing the deck fixture); assert green on `vll_declined` + empty payload and `should_panic` on a non-empty payload. If the class is provably unconstructible, restore the unconditional panic and record why.
- `G|G1.4d|AT1|AT1-4` (note) - offline at-bus fixtures `R4133_REPLIES` and `NODE_LESS_R4133`: hand-written from the same Pascal reading that `r4133_at_bus_walk` / `normalized_at_bus` encode, while the capi twins were measured live. Fix: measure both live (see Probes), confirm or replace the constants with the measured replies, and reword the "written out by hand" doc to "measured on r4133, <date>" citing the probe.
- `G|G1.4d|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `assert_port_at_bus_is_s4` / `compare_bus_at_bus`: the only drive calls the assert directly, so deleting its single call line leaves everything green. Fix: a `should_panic` drive THROUGH `compare_bus_at_bus` with a port list that drops an S4 element (expects the "S4 applied to this bus" message), or a did-it-run counter on the `assert_sc_study_compare_ran` precedent. Do NOT register the new drive in `G1_4D_PINS` here: `the_g1_4d_pins_the_docs_cite_exist_exactly_once` requires every registered name to be spelled in one of the `G1_4D_PIN_DOCS` (TESTING.md, TOLERANCE_NOTES, the plan, the record - all §4 documents this step cannot edit), so the registration cannot be green in-step. Instead write the pin's file + name into the TESTING.md note (at-bus section, "cite this drive") and into the state file; RF-D07-06 (`G|G1.4d|AT2|AT2-2`, runs after this step) registers it in the existence-only sub-table it introduces, and the TESTING.md doc-owner cites it.
- `G|G1.6b|AT1|AT1` (minor) - `PdElementCap` vs `PD_FIELD_NAMES` / `pd_fields!`: nothing locks the struct's field count to the compared list, so a new PDElements column can be captured on both channels and never compared. Fix: an exhaustive destructure with no `..` inside the `pd_fields!` pin (a 15th field then stops compiling) and/or `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]`; extend `the_field_extraction_is_the_fourteen_frozen_keys_in_read_order` accordingly and fix the module doc's drift claim (it covers renames only).
- `G|G1.4b|AC1|AC-3` (note) - `compare_bus_distances` doc: the oracle-internal BusList-order identity and the AllNodeDistances run-length rail are stated unconditionally but carry no information when all distances are equal (the ~373 forced meterless cases). Fix: qualify the doc (they bite only on the metered population counted by `record_distance_compare`; on meterless cases only the lengths bite); optionally add the free exact cross-side assert `exp_all_bus[i] == all_bus[i]`.
- `G|G1.4c|AC1|AC1-2` (note) - r4133 `DDLL/DBus.pas` citations, one line off: in `harness/mod.rs` `:296-297` == `:530-531` -> `:297-298` == `:529`/`:531` (two sites); the six -1.0 fill `:531` -> `:532`; `corpus_gate/manifest.rs` `:284-317` -> `:285-318`; `manifest.rs` and `harness/mod.rs` `:520-547` -> `:520-548`. Fix: open `.inputs/electricdss-code-r4133-trunk` `DDLL/DBus.pas`, verify each target line, correct; if the source disagrees with the triage's numbers, the source wins (record it).
- `G|G1.4a|AC2|AC2-2` (note) - r4133 `DBus.pas` citations propagated to ten places: BaseFactor `:413-414` -> `:414-415` (`exec/view.rs` two sites, `harness/mod.rs`, `corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-epri/src/capture.rs`) and the ascending-node walk `:415-421` -> `:418-422` (`exec/view.rs` four sites; the `TESTING.md` copy via a §4 note). Fix: grep the old ranges, verify against the source, correct every copy. `tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs` is in `files` only in case a citation rail there quotes one of these strings - touch it only if it reds.

**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 only. Run the at-bus fixture deck and the node-less deck that back `R4133_REPLIES` / `NODE_LESS_R4133` (the decks are named in the fixtures' doc) through the same DDLL at-bus calls `r4133_at_bus_walk` models, and capture the raw replies. Result decides: identical -> keep the constants and re-label them as measured; different -> replace them with the measured replies and, if `r4133_at_bus_walk` / the r4133 arm of `normalized_at_bus` then disagree with the live gate, stop and report (that is a comparator misreading, not a fixture edit). Keep the probe transcript under `tmp/retro_fix/state/` and cite it in the record.

**Acceptance:**
- The sequence availability rule runs on every bus; new drives exist: 2-node port-answers `should_panic`, n > 3 r4133, the R4133 seq / cplx-seq value legs, the FirstLoopHang pair (or the restored panic with its reason), the through-`compare_bus_at_bus` S4 drive (unregistered; its name is in the TESTING.md note and the state file for RF-D07-06).
- A 15th `PdElementCap` field cannot compile or decode uncompared; the fourteen-keys pin is extended.

- No ledger entry, tolerance or golden moves; measured populations change only if re-measured and recorded; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D06-07 — Pin both renders of the both-channel SKIP_PROPS rows and correct the SKIP_PROPS rationale comments
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-07", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D06-06"], "n_uids": 9} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-06.

- `RP|RP2.1|AT3|AT3-1` (major) - `harness/mod.rs`, `SKIP_PROPS_BOTH_CHANNELS`: five rows (Capacitor.FaultRate, Reactor.FaultRate, Capacitor.CMatrix, Reactor.RMatrix, Reactor.XMatrix) mask ~4 128 cells on the r4133 channel with no expected-value pin naming both numbers. Fix: add pins in the shape of `fault_gmatrix_renders_a_materialised_zero_matrix_when_unset`: FaultRate renders `'0.0005'` on the port (r4133 freezes `Str_Real(x,0)` -> `'0'`, `Capacitor.pas:777` / `Reactor.pas:1135`); unset CMatrix / RMatrix / XMatrix render the materialised zero triangle (r4133 `''`, `DSSObject.pas:112-115`). ### RF-D06-09 — Make the property display floor honest: inf cells, ArrayForm folding, FLOOR_HITS and the narrowed-row stale arm
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D06-09", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D06-08", "RF-D08-05", "RF-D09-01"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-08, RF-D08-05, RF-D09-01.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/props_norm.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Findings**
- `RP|RP2.4|AT1|AT-1` (major) - `props_norm.rs`, `number_rel` / `is_display_render` / `under_display_floor`: two DIFFERENT strings whose numeric tokens both overflow to `inf` are claimed as one value and the r4133 compare is dropped, so a wrong bus name on the r4133-gated `epri_dpv/M1` feeder cannot red (the hole `harness::value_verdict` was written to close). Fix: `number_rel`'s non-finite arm returns `None` and `is_display_render` must not short-circuit on non-finite equality, so only byte-identical strings pass. Flip the existing `inf` vs `inf` pin to `false`; add pins that `under_display_floor("0x008E1248.1", "0x008E1400.1")` and `("1e400", "9e999")` are false, plus a `compare_prop_lists`-level test that a wrong epri_dpv bus name fails under the r4133 policy. Re-run the replay count locks: `CLAIMED_DISPLAY_FLOOR` must not move (if it does, stop - a real cell was riding the hole; record it, R8).
- `RP|RP2.4|AC1|AC-3` (minor) - `props_norm.rs`, `NormRule::ArrayForm` / `numbers_match`: RP2.4 wired the 2e-4 floor into it, so the rule is no longer value-preserving although the seam's contract doc says rules never change WHICH value a cell is; the folded cell is credited to the normalization link. Fix, in two halves: (a) read `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D09-01.md` first (predicate name / FORM definition / pin, or the whole-revert + stale rows); the `NORM_HITS` crediting half is closed by RF-D09-01 `RP|RP2.1|AC2|AC2-1` (in **After**: a NORM hit only when the two sides differ in FORM, never on in-band numeric noise, with a pin that an ArrayForm row fed same-skeleton in-band numbers records a visit and no hit) - verify that rule and its pin at the lane HEAD and build on it, never a second crediting rule; (b) what remains here: make ArrayForm's token compare exact again and let in-floor gaps fall through to the display-floor link (credited to the floor link under D09-01's form-based rule); if the replay count locks forbid that, keep `numbers_match` and record why. Correct the "value-preserving" contract docs (normalization-seam comment in `harness/mod.rs`, the ArrayForm doc in `props_norm.rs`); pin the disposition of the `'[ 600 700.00003]'` vs `'[600,700,]'` cell. (Triage's "land with RP2.1 AC2-1" is that D09-01 uid.)
- `RP|RP2.4|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `props_norm.rs`, `FLOOR_HITS` / `display_floor_counters`: it counts every already-agreeing numeric cell, so it is not the "live floor population" the docs claim, and no epilogue reads it - the only chain link without a liveness guard. Fix: bump `FLOOR_HITS` (and the per-thread touch) only when `rust != oracle`, like the echo link; fix the in-code docs that call the counters "the live floor population" (TESTING / TOLERANCE_NOTES copies via notes); tighten the `harness/mod.rs` display-floor counter test to an exact delta on a genuinely divergent cell; add a live epilogue reader in `corpus_gate.rs` (hits > 0 whenever the r4133 props walk ran, silent under `DSS_GATE_ONLY`).
- `RP|RP4.1|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `props_norm.rs`, `check_echo_rows_are_live` second (narrowed-row) arm: inert for FOUR of the twenty narrowed rows in the gate binary (`load.yearly`, `reactor.bus2`, `regcontrol.idle`, `relay.reset` - sibling tests feed the shipped ECHO statics) while the doc says exactly one; the doc also cites `harness::tests::` for a driver that lives in `harness::props_policy_tests`. Fix (preferred): stop sibling tests feeding the shipped statics - drive those fixtures through the counter-parameterised / offline entry RF-D08-05 `RP|RP4.1|AT2|AT2-1` (in **After**) introduced in `props_norm.rs` for the r4133 props-walk statics; reuse that mechanism for the ECHO statics, do not add a second isolation mechanism in the same file - so the arm is live for all twenty. Minimal alternative: replace the "exactly one row" doc with a pinned const list of the sibling-fed rows (+ the exempt `fault.bus2`), asserted by a test. Fix the module path either way.
- `RP|RP2.4|SA1|SA-3` (note) - `harness/mod.rs`, the assert message of the test whose doc the RP2.4 settlement rewrote: still "must compare ... exactly". Fix: reword to "must compare ... at the case tier floors (no display floor)".
- `RP|RP2.4|AT1|AT-4` (note) - `harness/mod.rs`, `UNDER_FLOOR` fixture comment: attributes the `%-.5g` family to `PCElements/Vsource.pas:1326-1341`. Fix: the verified r4133 ranges are `%-.5g` = `Vsource.pas:1327-1335` (+ `:1343` basemva) and `%-.8g` = `:1337-1342` (+ `:1344`), matching `props_norm.rs` `R4133_DISPLAY_FLOOR`; verify against the source before writing.
- `RP|RP2.1|AT3|AT3-4` (note) - the normalization seam's contract doc anchors `lane::expected_rerounded` on `lane.rs:546-592` (now the monitor-pad transform). Fix: replace the line anchor with the symbol reference `harness/lane.rs::expected_rerounded` in both `harness/mod.rs` and the `props_norm.rs` module doc.

**Acceptance:**
- Pins exist and pass: `inf` vs `inf` is NOT under the floor, the two overflow-string pairs are false, a wrong epri_dpv bus name reds under the r4133 policy; the replay count locks (`CLAIMED_DISPLAY_FLOOR` and siblings) are unchanged, or the moved cell is reported.
- The ArrayForm cell's disposition is pinned and the seam contract doc matches the code.
- `FLOOR_HITS` counts only divergent cells (exact-delta test) and the gate epilogue asserts it live; the narrowed echo arm is live for all twenty rows or the sibling-fed list is pinned.
- No tolerance loosened (the 2e-4 floor constant is untouched), no ledger or golden change; §4 notes left; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-01 — Harden the file:LINE citation walker (anchoring, comma form, floors)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-01", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": [], "n_uids": 5} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `TESTING.md`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`

All five findings live in one region of the cfg gate: `LINE_CITED_DOCS`, `file_citations_in`,
`range_end`, `backticked_idents`, `operational_docs_line_citations_point_at_the_line_they_name`
and `rust_comments_citing_a_record_line_point_at_the_passage_they_name`. The number of
citations the tightened anchor (AC-1 / SA-1) will red is UNMEASURED: at `ab9f0f76` the two
documents hold ~173 `file.rs:LINE` citations, ~95 of them with no backticked ident before the
citation on the citing line (they fall back to the previous line), and every red is a manual
`grep -n` re-point into `harness/mod.rs` (19k lines) or `corpus_gate/runner.rs`. So the work is
ordered measurement-first, in two parts:

**Parts:**
1. `G|G1.10a|AC4|AC4-4` (comma form + unit test + the one known re-point), `RP|RP5.1|SA1|SA-2`
   (the `<!-- line-citations: N -->` markers, measured after the comma form since it adds
   components; the anchor tightening of part 2 does not move the count - it changes resolution,
   not the citation set) and `RP|RP5.2|AT1|AT-1` (exact record-citation counts); full gate
   green; commit. Then, still in part 1, run the tightened anchor of AC-1 / SA-1 as a probe
   (a local, uncommitted change or a unit test that prints instead of asserting) and write the
   red list `(doc, line, citation, symbol it should land on)` to
   `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D07-01.md`.
2. `RP|RP5.1|AC1|AC-1` + `RP|RP5.1|SA1|SA-1` (the anchor, its unit tests) and the digits-only
   re-points of the red list under R9; full gate green; commit. **Stop rule:** if part 1's red
   list exceeds ~15 citations, part 2 does NOT start the re-points: it reports the list to the
   coordinator (state file + record), who decides whether the re-points are routed to the
   doc-owner steps (RF-D05-04, RF-D04-01/02/03/07/09) with the anchor landing behind them, or
   the step gets a third part. Never land the tightened anchor with a weakened check or a
   skip to get green.

Shared-doc clash (§4): a tightened rail reds on citations that are already stale in the two
shared docs, and the gate cannot be green with them. **Ruling R9 (`rulings.md`, 2026-09-20)
applies:** a rail-forcing, digits-only re-point of a `file.rs:N` citation in a
`LINE_CITED_DOCS` document (numbers only, no prose) is made in-step by RF-D07-01, RF-D07-02 and
RF-D07-06, whose **Files** therefore list those documents so the wave scheduler sees the edit;
every such edit is listed one per line in `tmp/retro_fix/notes/testing-md.md` /
`tolerance-notes-md.md` / `golden-rebase-plan-md.md` (and marked there as already applied);
any prose change goes to the notes only. The one `<!-- line-citations: N -->` marker line per
document that the SA-2 bullet introduces is a rail-forced edit of the same kind (one comment
line, no prose; listed in the notes the same way). This step is not a doc-owner (index flag
`inline_shared`); everything beyond the forced lines stays with the owner steps of §4.

**Findings**
- `RP|RP5.1|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`,
  `operational_docs_line_citations_point_at_the_line_they_name`: the anchor set is every
  backticked ident of the citing line plus the line above, matched with `window.contains(id)`
  as a raw substring, so a citation aimed at the wrong item (or greened by the previous table
  row's ident) passes. Fix: anchor each citation on the backticked identifier attached to it
  on the citing line (the nearest one before the citation); fall back to the previous line
  only when the citing line has no ident before the citation, and never across a Markdown
  table row (a line starting with `|`); match as a whole identifier token, not a substring.
  Repair every citation the tightened rail reds (known at triage: `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
  the `tol_for` row citing `mod.rs:1208-1217` / `:1225-1234`, which now land on struct
  fields). Add a unit test with a mis-pointed fixture (right window, wrong ident; and an ident
  inherited from the previous table row) that must be reported.
- `RP|RP5.1|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - same function, the `lines[i-1]` inheritance and the
  `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` `midi` / `micro_wtg3_dynamics` tolerance row: the row was
  re-anchored on `Tolerances`, a token all eight `tol_for` arms spell, so it cannot detect arm
  drift (the doc numbers are correct again at HEAD; the rail weakness is live). Fix: refuse a
  non-discriminating anchor - an ident that occurs in more than one `=> Tolerances {` arm /
  many times in the target file does not count as an anchor - and for rows citing a `tol_for`
  arm require the quoted tier literal (the arm's string pattern) inside the cited range. Add
  a negative unit test that feeds the old row text (`mod.rs:1279-1288` anchored on
  `Tolerances`) and expects red.
- `G|G1.10a|AC4|AC4-4` (note) - `file_citations_in` / `range_end`: only `file.rs:A` and the
  `-B` range are parsed; in the comma form `runner.rs:733,508` the second number is scanned as
  a bare digit run and dropped, so it is never checked. Fix: parse `file.rs:A,B[,C]` and emit
  one citation per component, each anchored like a single-line citation. Unit test:
  `runner.rs:733,508` yields two citations, and a fixture whose second component is stale is
  reported. Re-point any comma-form citation in the two docs that then reds (triage saw
  `corpus_gate/runner.rs:927,930` vs the `harness::assert_complex_close` calls at 928/931).
- `RP|RP5.1|SA1|SA-2` (note) - `LINE_CITED_DOCS`: the anti-vacuity floors are static (`40` /
  `10`) against 176 / 20 resolved citations at triage, so most citations can vanish green.
  Fix: replace the floors by fail-on-stale exact counts of resolved citations per document
  (`assert_eq!`, the `NORM_ROWS` / `ECHO_ROWS` convention) - but the declared count lives IN
  the document, not in the gate: each `LINE_CITED_DOCS` document carries one HTML comment
  `<!-- line-citations: N -->` (first occurrence wins; a missing marker reds), the rail parses
  it and asserts `resolved == N` with a message telling the editor to re-measure and update
  the marker of that document in the same commit and never to drop a citation to keep the
  number. Reason: the later editors of these documents are docs-only steps whose **Files** do
  not hold the cfg gate (RF-D05-04, RF-D04-01/02/03/07/09), and the notes they apply
  (symbol-only conversions) move the count - the marker lets them keep the rail green inside
  their own scope, and the docs gate of §2.2 runs this rail so they see the message. Add the
  marker line to `TESTING.md` and `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` in-step (R9, listed in the notes)
  and measure AFTER the comma-form bullet, since it adds components to the count (the anchor
  bullets change which citations resolve, not how many are checked). Unit test: a fixture
  document whose marker is one off, and one without a marker, are both reported.
- `RP|RP5.2|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - tail of
  `rust_comments_citing_a_record_line_point_at_the_passage_they_name`: the guard ends on
  `checked >= 5 && citing_files.len() >= 2` while the record publishes 7 citations over 2
  files, so one of RP5.2's two code citations can be deleted green. Fix: `assert_eq!(checked,
  7)` and `assert_eq!(citing_files.len(), 2)` (re-measure at landing with a grep for
  `\.md:[0-9]+` over `crates/**/*.rs`, the gate file excluded), with the "a floor lets the
  table shrink" message the RP3 pin tables in `props_r4133_replay.rs` use.

**Acceptance:**
- `operational_docs_line_citations_point_at_the_line_they_name` is green in both lanes with
  the nearest-ident, whole-token, no-table-row-inheritance anchor; new unit tests exist for
  the mis-pointed fixture, the non-discriminating `Tolerances` anchor and the comma form, each
  with a red case - or, under the part-2 stop rule, part 1 is landed (comma form, markers,
  exact record counts) and the measured red list is in the state file and the record with the
  coordinator's routing decision pending; the anchor bullets are then recorded as open, never
  as closed.
- Each `LINE_CITED_DOCS` document carries a `<!-- line-citations: N -->` marker equal to the
  count measured at the step's HEAD and the rail asserts it (`assert_eq!`; a missing or stale
  marker reds); the record-citation guard holds its exact counts in the gate.
- Every digits-only re-point made in `TESTING.md` / `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` (R9) is listed
  in the notes files and `git diff` of those two files shows digit changes plus the one
  marker line per document only; no prose of a shared doc changes; no golden byte, ledger
  entry or tolerance moves.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-02 — Extend the citation rail to the plan, TESTING.md modes/.py cites and retire stale cross-file line cites
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-02", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-01"], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-01.
**Files:** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `TESTING.md`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`

Builds on the RF-D07-01 walker (nearest-ident anchor, comma form, exact counts in
`LINE_CITED_DOCS`). Two halves: (a) widen what the rail covers, (b) turn the cfg gate's own
doc comments from dead cross-file line numbers into symbol cites. Same shared-doc clause as
RF-D07-01 (ruling R9, `rulings.md` 2026-09-20 - see that section): digits-only re-points that
a widened rail forces in `TESTING.md` / `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md` are made in-step (both are in
**Files** for that reason only) and listed in the notes file of that doc; prose changes (plan
note, record mirrors) go to the notes only. At the end re-measure the `LINE_CITED_DOCS` counts
and update the `<!-- line-citations: N -->` markers (RF-D07-01 convention) of `TESTING.md` and,
if it joins the rail, of the plan, in-step.

**Findings**
- `G|G1.6(i)|AC1|AC2` (note) - `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, the G1.6(i) as-executed note, and
  `LINE_CITED_DOCS`: the note's citations for `compare_meter`'s `cmp_members`,
  `compare_reliability` (both `harness/mod.rs`) and `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md:1011` (the
  D11/D18 exactness re-derivation) point at unrelated lines, and the plan is outside the rail.
  Fix: close the class - for the plan either add `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md` to
  `LINE_CITED_DOCS` (then add its `<!-- line-citations: N -->` marker in-step - the plan is in
  **Files** - so its later owners RF-D05-01 / RF-D04-03 / RF-D04-07 keep the rail green by
  updating the marker in-doc; RF-D05-01 G2.4/AC1/AC-4 drops a range and is told so), or,
  if the walker cannot take its prose, add a rail that forbids new bare `harness/mod.rs:N`
  citations in the plan; for the record `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` (NOT in
  **Files**, so no marker can be added here) only the forbid-rail arm is allowed in this step -
  never put the record into `LINE_CITED_DOCS`. Decide by measuring how many citations red; write the chosen
  re-points / symbol-only conversions (incl. the three above, and the TOLERANCE_NOTES D11/D18
  section reference instead of `:1011`) to the plan and record notes. Decision D37(10) parks
  the class for G5.1: if the executor honours it, record that and still ship the forbid-rail.
- `G|G1.0|AT2|AT2-4` (note) - `TESTING.md`, the r4133-bridge section: four
  `crates/dss-epri/src/modes.rs:LINE` citations miss their symbol (`S_SENTINELS`,
  `V_WITHOUT_SENTINEL`, `WP_G1_MODES`, `DO_NOT_CALL`) and pass on a co-mentioned ident. Fix:
  re-point the four by locating each symbol in `modes.rs` (digits only, in-step); confirm the
  RF-D07-01 nearest-ident anchor now reds the old numbers and add a synthetic negative pin for
  exactly this shape (single-line citation, right window, only a co-mentioned ident present).
- `G|G1.6(i)|AT3|AT3-4` (note) - the `.rs`-only filter in the citation walk (`if
  !cited.ends_with(".rs") { continue; }`): `.py` citations are never checked, so
  `TESTING.md`'s `tools/oracle/oracle_server.py:LINE` cites for the capi `RelCalc` drive
  (`d.Text.Command = "RelCalc"`) and `_RELCALC_TOLERATED_ERRNOS` are stale beside exact `.rs`
  siblings. Fix: extend the rail to `.py` paths under `tools/` (same anchor rule), with a
  negative pin; re-point the stale `.py` cites it reds (triage also flagged a third
  `oracle_server.py` cite near TESTING.md:952 - re-check it).
- `G|G2.2c|SA1|SA-1` (note) - `branches_on_lane` doc paragraph: it cites `harness/mod.rs:4738`
  and `harness/lane.rs` line numbers that were stale when written and carries the false
  sentence "the sibling cross-file numbers below are re-measured with each edit". Fix: name
  symbols instead (the `kv_value_eq` compare that holds the file's only `lane::PARITY` read;
  the `PARITY` const assert) and delete the false sentence.
- `G|G2.1f|SA1|SA-2` (note) - same doc block: `lane.rs:795-796 asserts it equals
  dss_core::compat::ORACLE_PARITY` is stale. Fix: cite the test
  `lane_const_tracks_the_engine_build` in `harness/lane.rs` by name; note the same
  replacement for the record (`golden-rebase.md`, G2.1f settlement block).
- `G|G2.2a|SA1|SA-2` (note) - same doc block, the census sentence: "Two such comments exist
  in-tree (`tests/golden_reports.rs:1628`, `tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs:358`)" - one of the

  owns it and builds it on this step's walker shape.

  both numbers (master: previous bullet; verify with `grep -n PARITY` over
  `corpus_gate/scheduler.rs` = no hit). Fix in the same edit; note for the record's settlement

**Acceptance:**

  in `LINE_CITED_DOCS` (with its marker) or a forbid-rail for bare `harness/mod.rs:N` cites

  a negative pin; the `LINE_CITED_DOCS` markers are re-measured.

- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-03 — Give the G2.0 teardown register teeth (attribute stack, empty bodies, lane helpers, rosters, exclusion sites)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-03", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-02"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-02.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/lane.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`

Region: `TornDownRow` / `Evidence` / `TORN_DOWN_ROWS`, `teardown_markers`, `reads_the_lane`,
`test_fn_body`, `every_torn_down_row_keeps_its_pin_and_its_evidence`,
`teardown_markers_and_the_register_agree`. Do NOT make `test_fn_body` comment-aware here and
do not change the pin field to a list - both belong to RF-D07-04. Every new check ships with
a synthetic self-test that reds; a real row that reds is a finding to fix at the row, never a
reason to relax the check.

**Parts:** (seven independent rail mechanisms, each with its own red self-test, in a 6k-line
file - two agents)
1. `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-3` (exclusion-site table, both directions per file), `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-1`
   (attribute stack), `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-2` (assertion token), `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-5` (`count == 1`
   slices, rows added since re-measured); self-tests; full gate green; commit. Hands over the
   table shape it chose for exclusion sites (RF-D07-04 builds on it) and any row it had to
   lengthen.
2. `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-2` (lane-helper roster + the `lane.rs` rail), `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-4` (the two
   start-line rosters from `git show 4f977d9e`), `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-7` (unreadable-file list); the
   TESTING.md note; full gate green; commit; record.

**Findings**
- `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-3` (minor) - row->marker direction of
  `teardown_markers_and_the_register_agree`: only `Evidence::Exclusion(file, _)` is cashed
  and a row has one `Evidence`, so 8 of the 14 `LANE-EXCLUSION(` markers are tied to nothing
  (`harness/lane.rs` x3: the two RELAY rows and `MONITOR_CHANNEL_PADS_THE_UNFLUSHED_STREAM`;
  `golden_reports.rs` x2: `FAULT_DUMP_TAIL_REPRINTS_MINAMPS`, `max_device_name_length`;
  `golden_cim.rs` x1: the g0ch row; the GIC row in `golden_reports.rs` + `props_roundtrip.rs`).
  Fix: let a row carry exclusion sites besides its primary evidence (a parallel `(row, file,
  slice)` table or a slice per row), register the 8, and assert marker<->register both ways
  per file (re-count the markers with `grep -rn 'LANE-EXCLUSION(' crates/`). Note for
  TESTING.md's marker section.
- `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - pin arm of `every_torn_down_row_keeps_its_pin_and_its_evidence`
  / `test_fn_body`: only the pin body is inspected, so `#[cfg(feature = "oracle-parity")]`
  (or `cfg_attr`, or a lane-cfg on the enclosing `mod`) on the pin fn compiles it away in one
  lane while the row stays green. Fix: inspect the attribute stack around the pin's `#[test]`
  and the enclosing `mod` headers for a `#[cfg(` / `#[cfg_attr(` naming the lane feature;
  report "pin exists in one lane only". Self-test: item-gated pin => problem.
- `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - same pin arm: a pin gutted to `{}` passes. Fix: require at least
  one assertion token in the body (`assert!`, `assert_eq!`, `assert_ne!`, `panic!`, `expect`,
  or a harness compare call); report "pin gutted". Self-test with `{}`.
- `G|G2.0|AC1|AC-5` (note) - `Evidence::Site` / `Evidence::Exclusion` arm: `text.contains(slice)`
  does not enforce the plan's "distinctive slice". Fix: assert
  `text.matches(slice).count() == 1`, message naming the duplicate. The auditor measured the
  first 20 rows unique; re-measure rows added since (G2.6). A non-unique slice is lengthened,
  never exempted.
- `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `reads_the_lane`: it matches only `ORACLE_PARITY` / `PARITY` /
  the cfg string, so a pin that branches one call deep through a `harness::lane` helper is
  certified unconditional. Fix: add a roster const of the lane-branching helpers in
  `harness/lane.rs` (`expected_eventlog`, `expected_monitor_channel`, `expected_rerounded`,
  `compare_report`, `exact_value_policy`, `compare_iterations`, `profile_ll_policy`), report a
  pin calling one as "branches one call deep" unless allow-listed with a reason, and tie the
  roster to the file by a rail: every `fn` in `lane.rs` whose body names `PARITY` is in the
  roster and vice versa. `lane.rs` is in **Files** only in case that rail needs a marker or
  helper there; prefer parsing it from the gate and leaving it untouched.
- `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-4` (note) - the ghost (anti-swap) check: it intersects registered SplitAlias
  names with the LIVE split set, so registering a name that was never an alias balances both
  census ties; the WholeCase half has no ghost check. Fix: commit the start-line rosters as
  consts - the 31 split-alias names from `git show
  4f977d9e:crates/{dss-parser,dss-core,dss-sparse}/src/compat.rs` (read-only git) and the 4
  WholeCase `ESCAPE_REGISTER` keys - assert their lengths equal
  `SPLIT_ALIASES_AT_WP_G2_START` / `WHOLE_CASE_MARKERS_AT_WP_G2_START`, every
  `TORN_DOWN_ROWS` name of each kind is a roster member, and roster == live set + torn-down
  set. Correct the register doc sentence that claims the swap is closed.
- `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-7` (note) - `teardown_markers`: `let Ok(text) = fs::read_to_string(&path)
  else { continue; }` drops an unreadable file silently and the `> 300` floor counts paths,
  not reads. Fix: `expect` with the path (as sibling registries do), or return an `unreadable`
  list asserted empty in `teardown_markers_and_the_register_agree`.

**Acceptance:**
- New self-tests exist and red on: item-cfg-gated pin, empty-body pin, a pin calling a roster
  helper, a never-an-alias registered name, a duplicate evidence slice, an untied
  `LANE-EXCLUSION(` marker.
- All 14 (re-measured) exclusion markers are tied both ways; roster consts have the lengths of
  the two `*_AT_WP_G2_START` anchors; the lane-helper roster equals the `PARITY`-naming fns of
  `harness/lane.rs`.
- No engine file, golden, ledger entry or tolerance moves; both lanes green.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-04 — Fix torn-down register rows: pin lists, comment-aware test_fn_body, survivor-table rail, false zero-footprint rationales
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-04", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D03-01", "RF-D07-03"], "n_uids": 10} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D03-01, RF-D07-03.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`

Row-level corrections of `TORN_DOWN_ROWS` on top of the RF-D07-03 mechanisms (exclusion-site
table, `count == 1` slices, attribute-stack check). Mechanism first (first two bullets), rows
after. Every rationale sentence corrected in the gate has a mirror in the record and/or plan:
write the mirror text to the notes, do not edit those docs. Rationale claims about the corpus
are re-measured at the step's HEAD (G1.10b widened the run-file content compare).

**Parts:** (the row bullets cannot start before the mechanisms compile)
1. `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-1` (comment-aware `test_fn_body` + the P1/P1b self-tests) and
   `G|G2.1b|AT1|AT-2` (pin field -> `&[(file, fn)]`, the mechanical edit of every row, the
   per-fn marker check, the CapControl deck pin registered); full gate green; commit. Hands
   over the new row shape and any row the mechanisms redded.
2. The eight row bullets (`G2.1a AC-3`, `G2.2d AC-3`, `G2.4 AT-6`, `G2.0 AT-5` survivor-table
   rail, `G2.6 AC-1`, `G2.1c AT-1`, `G2.1g AC-2`, `G2.1f AT-2`), each with its HEAD
   re-measurement; the notes; full gate green; commit; record.

**Findings**
- `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - `test_fn_body`: any `fn <func>` substring whose nearest
  preceding `#[test]` has no other `fn ` between counts, even inside a comment, and then the
  NEIGHBOURING test's body is returned (`#[test]\n// superseded by fn deleted_pin\nfn
  neighbour_b() {..}` resolves `deleted_pin`). Fix: make it comment-aware - the matched `fn
  <func>` must be code (skip `//` and block comments) and only whitespace, attributes and
  comments may sit between the `#[test]` and that fn. Add the P1 probe above as a self-test
  (and keep P1b, comment above the attribute, => `None`). Correct the closure claim in the doc
  comment of `every_torn_down_row_keeps_its_pin_and_its_evidence`; note for the record (G2.0
  settlement block, "class closed").
- `G|G2.1b|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `TornDownRow` pin field (`Option<(file, fn)>`) and the
  file-scoped `missing` marker check: the CapControl make-like row registers
  `make_like_copies_the_control_signal` but not the deck pin under the second
  `EXPECTED-VALUE-PIN(CAPCONTROL_MAKELIKE...` marker in `cap_control/tests.rs`. Fix: let a row
  register several pins (`&[(file, fn)]`, mechanical edit of all rows), register the deck pin,
  and make the marker check per pin fn (marker directly above that fn), not per file.
- `G|G2.1a|AC1|AC-3` (note) - the G2.1a row (`support/mathutil/mod.rs`): its `Evidence::Site`
  slice matches 1 of the 4 kernels made unconditional. Fix: list all four, spelled as full
  return statements so each is unique under the `count == 1` rule (`return (data[0], 0.0);`,
  and the `(f64::from(data[0]), 0.0)`, `(f64::from(y[0]), 0.0)`, `(y[0], 0.0)` forms - note
  `(data[0], 0.0)` is a substring of the `f64::from` form); needs Evidence to take several
  slices per file if RF-D07-03 did not already provide it.
- `G|G2.2d|AC1|AC-3` (note) - the two G2.2d rows (RELAY event-log labels): their comments do
  not name the check holding the harness half, which `Evidence::Site`'s contract requires.
  Fix: add to both the sentence that the unconditional `expected_eventlog` rewrites (the two
  `LANE-EXCLUSION` markers in `harness/lane.rs`) are held by
  `harness::lane::tests::eventlog_transform_is_the_two_relay_rows` plus the gated corpus cases.
- `G|G2.4|AT1|AT-6` (note) - the `MONITOR_CHANNEL_PADS_THE_UNFLUSHED_STREAM` row: Site-anchored
  on the engine kernel, its `harness/lane.rs` `LANE-EXCLUSION` marker is unenforced because
  the `unmarked` check filters on `Evidence::Exclusion`. Fix: verify the RF-D07-03
  exclusion-site table already enforces this marker and the two RELAY ones (deleting or
  rewording the marker reds); if not, add the `(row, harness file)` entries. Add the negative
  self-test if RF-D07-03 left none for a Site row.
- `G|G2.0|AT1|AT-5` (note) - `TESTING.md` five-row survivor table (rows whose first cell starts
  with `compat::`): the "pinned at" column is checked by nothing. Fix: a rail in the gate that
  parses the table, takes the last path segment of "pinned at", asserts a `fn <name>` exists
  in the workspace test sources and that its file references the row's compat kernel (or a
  lane cfg); negative pin on a synthetic row. No TESTING.md edit is expected (all five names
  exist); if one is needed it goes to the notes.
- `G|G2.6|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - the `max_device_name_length` row comment: it calls the other six
  Show writers "unobservable by construction", false for `report/show/losses.rs` - Pascal
  `Format('%10.5f, ')` (ShowResults.pas:2743-2744) has no separator, so a >= 1000 kW loss glues
  to the quoted name. Fix: say five siblings are unobservable and losses is held by
  `exec::tests::compat_quirks::show_table_layout_is_the_lane_kernel`; add a `losses.rs` needle
  (`let mdnl = super::device_name_width(classes, ckt);`) through the multi-site evidence.
  Notes: same sentence in the plan's G2.6 section and the record's G2.6 block.
- `G|G2.1c|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - the G2.1c row rationale "no golden and no gated corpus deck
  rates an element negatively": false - `tests/corpus/modes/makeposseq/makeposseq_xfmr.dss`
  rates `AutoTrans.at` negatively (r4133 prints NormAmps -152.85 / EmergAmps -208.43). This
  row and its pin are owned by RF-D03-01 (`G|G2.1c|AC1|AC-1`, in **After**): it
  corrects the same row prose ("search `negative` near the G2.1c row") and adds the
  expected-value pin
  `golden_reports.rs::export_seqcurrents_derived_negative_autotrans_rating_prints_zero_pct`.
  Fix here: verify D03-01's rewording at the lane HEAD (the footprint is zero because no
  gating channel compares `Export SeqCurrents` text - re-check against the G1.10b run-file
  content compare that this deck does not export it, and reword only if D03-01's sentence
  disagrees with that re-check); register D03-01's pin name in the G2.1c `TornDownRow` pin
  list (the mechanism AT-2 above adds); mirror any residual wording to the record notes. No
  follow-up for the coordinator - the pin exists.
- `G|G2.1g|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - the G2.1g row rationale: both measurement claims are false -
  gated decks DO hold wye capacitors with explicit `bus2=` (`GICExample/GIC_Example.dss`,
  `asymmetric/capacitor/capacitor_asym.dss`, `NEVTestCase/Capacitors.DSS`) and a gated case
  DOES run `export cim100` (`Examples/CIM/IEEE13_CDPSM.dss`). Fix: state the real reason - the
  run-file compare covers the created-file NAME set, not CIM content (`harness/run_files.rs`),
  and IEEE13_CDPSM's capacitors carry no `bus2`. First confirm at HEAD that CIM XML is still
  outside the G1.10b content compare; if it is inside, stop this finding and report it to the
  coordinator as a ledger question (R8). Mirror to the record notes.
- `G|G2.1f|AT1|AT-2` (note) - the G2.1f row rationale and the record: "no corpus deck exports
  Storage meters at all - the only `/m` caller in the tree is this pin" is false. Fix: no
  corpus deck runs `Export Storage_Meters /m`, but the shared multi-file writer runs under the
  gate via `Test/PVSystemTest.dss` (`Export pvsystem /m`), whose `EXP_PV_PV.CSV` is
  content-compared since G1.10b - so a future gated Storage `/m` deck would be compared by the
  same rail. Drop the "only `/m` caller" clause; mirror to the record notes.

**Acceptance:**
- `test_fn_body` self-tests P1 (=> not resolved) and P1b exist; every row's pins are a list and
  the CapControl row registers both pins with per-fn markers.
- `every_torn_down_row_keeps_its_pin_and_its_evidence` and
  `teardown_markers_and_the_register_agree` are green with four G2.1a slices, the `losses.rs`
  needle, the three harness markers enforced and RF-D03-01's G2.1c pin registered on its row;
  the survivor-table rail exists with a negative pin.
- No rationale in `TORN_DOWN_ROWS` states a corpus measurement that a grep over `tests/corpus`
  disproves; mirrors are in `tmp/retro_fix/notes/`; no golden, ledger entry or tolerance moves.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-05 — Make the lane-split pin walk per-test-fn and register the golden_reports Mask sites
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-05", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-04"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-04.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_reports.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`

The first three findings are one mechanism (the G2.1f deferral that G2.2a never picked up):
implement it once, close all three. It reuses the comment-aware `test_fn_body` /
`balanced_block` of RF-D07-04. If the tightened walk reds a live alias, that is a real
finding: fix the pin, never relax the walk.

**Findings**
- `G|G2.1f|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `every_lane_split_alias_is_pinned_by_an_expected_value_test`:
  it credits an alias when `names_token(region, alias) && branches_on_lane(region, alias)`
  holds over a whole file's test region, so a lane read in a neighbouring test credits the
  pin. Fix: make the walk function-granular - credit only when ONE `#[test] fn` body both
  names the alias token and branches on the lane; keep the non-vacuity const. The auditor
  measured all 11 live aliases already have a same-fn pin. Record note: the deferral is closed
  here.
- `G|G2.1f|AT1|AT-5` (note) - same walk, test side. Fix: add the negative unit test (alias
  named in fn A, lane read in fn B => not credited) and the positive one. Record note: the
  WP-G2 index line "one refuted" must say the deferral was closed by this step.
- `G|G2.1f|AC1|AC-4` (note) - same mechanism plus the WP-G2 summary row of the record. Fix:
  if the per-fn walk needs it, add a per-row register for the LIVE split aliases (alias ->
  (file, pin fn)) beside `TORN_DOWN_ROWS`; otherwise the generic per-fn walk suffices - say
  which in the state file. Notes: record summary row => "5 minor: 4 fixed, 1 mechanism
  deferred (its example refuted), closed by RF-D07-05"; TESTING.md's description of the pin
  walk => per test fn.
- `G|G2.6|SA1|SA-3` (note) - `golden_reports.rs`, the doc comment that spells
  `compat::render_rows`: the prose mention makes the walk credit the file as a pin for
  `render_rows` and falsifies the gate's measurement sentence. Fix: reword so the alias token
  is not spelled ("the report-row renderer split"); optionally make `names_token` skip `//`
  comment lines (then add a unit test for it).
- `G|G2.6|AT1|AT-3` (note) - the `lane::PARITY`-arm measurement paragraph in the
  `branches_on_lane` doc: "`golden_reports.rs` names no split alias at all any more" is false
  (`fmt_g(v, 6)` is called there; the `render_rows` mention until the previous bullet lands).
  Fix: state instead that no split alias named in `golden_reports.rs` is a pin dependency of a
  surviving row through `lane::PARITY` (`render_rows` is pinned in `compat_quirks.rs`, `fmt_g`
  in `fmt_battery.rs` via `ORACLE_PARITY`); no line numbers (RF-D07-02 already removed the
  `harness/mod.rs` cite of this paragraph).
- `G|G2.1f|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - the Storage `/m` pin in `golden_reports.rs`, its doc, and the
  G2.1f `TORN_DOWN_ROWS` rationale ("rows are byte-identical"): `compare_export` with
  `register_policy()` compares trimmed fields, numerics by f64 value at `rel = abs = 0`,
  header verbatim - not bytes. Fix (preferred): add a raw string equality of the data rows,
  multi-file vs single-file, to the pin, which makes the byte claim true - measure first, keep
  only if green in both lanes; otherwise reword the pin doc and the rationale to the
  field/value comparison. Record note for "compare byte-for-byte".
- `G|G2.5|AT1|AT-3` (minor) - `golden_reports.rs` `GateSpec::Mask` sites: the `export_gicmvars`
  mask on both value columns has no existence guard tying it to its replacement pin. Fix: add
  to the gate a closed register of `GateSpec::Mask` code sites (report name -> replacement pin
  fn, or a reasoned "nondeterministic, no pin"), assert the site count equals the register
  (triage counted 7 code sites - re-measure) and that every named `#[test] fn` exists (reuse
  the `TORN_DOWN_ROWS` pin-existence helper); register `export_gicmvars` ->
  `export_gicmvars_matches_the_equivalent_ohms_spec`. TESTING.md note for the new rail.

**Acceptance:**
- The split-alias walk credits per `#[test] fn`; the negative test (A names, B branches) exists
  and all live aliases stay credited in the parity lane.
- The `GateSpec::Mask` register equals the code sites of `golden_reports.rs` and each named
  pin exists; a self-test reds on an unregistered site.
- `golden_reports.rs` spells no split-alias token in prose; the byte claim of the Storage `/m`
  pin is either true (raw row equality, both lanes) or reworded everywhere in **Files**.
- No golden byte moves; no ledger entry or tolerance changes; every uid above is closed or
  recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-06 — Tighten the G1.x pin registries: per-pin citation, missing pins, converse pinned-by rail
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-06", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D06-06", "RF-D07-05"], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-06, RF-D07-05.
**Files:** `TESTING.md`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/oracle_parity_cfg_gate.rs`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`

Registry lengths quoted below (`21 -> 25`, `76 -> 79`, `+4`, `+3`) are triage-time deltas, not
targets: earlier steps add G1.x drives before this step runs (RF-D06-06's through-`compare_bus_at_bus`
S4 drive, left unregistered on purpose and named in its state file / TESTING.md note; RF-D08-04,
RF-D08-05, RF-D10-01 pins). Rule: register the drives named here PLUS every unregistered
at-bus / G1.10 drive present at the lane HEAD (existence-only where no doc names it), and
re-measure every array length at landing - the record states the measured lengths.

Region: the per-sub-step pin registries of the cfg gate - the five threshold-form tests
`every_pin_the_g13a_record_names_exists_and_is_cited`, `...g13d1...`, `...g13d2...`
(`G13D2_PINS`), `...g13b...`, `...g13c...` (each ends on `cited >= N`), the per-pin ones
`every_pin_the_g10_record_names_exists_and_is_cited` (`G10_PINS`),
`the_g1_10_pins_the_docs_cite_exist_exactly_once` (`G1_10_PINS`),
`the_g1_4d_pins_the_docs_cite_exist_exactly_once` (`G1_4D_PINS`).

Shared-doc clash (§4): the per-pin registries assert that a `*_PIN_DOCS` document NAMES each
registered pin, and those documents (record, TESTING.md) are shared. Rule for this step: a pin
that no pin doc names today goes into an explicit existence-only sub-table of its registry
(existence + exact count asserted, citation not), and the sentence that should cite it is
written to the notes; the doc-owner step cites it and moves the row to the cited table. Never
register a row in the cited table that reds.

**Parts:** (runs in the same wave as RF-D05-01 - a mid-way stop costs a wave, so the split is
fixed here)
1. Registry restructuring and additions: `G|G1.3a|SA1|SA-6`, `G|G1.3d(ii)|AT2|AT2-1`,
   `G|G1.4d|AT2|AT2-2`, `G|G1.0|AT2|AT2-3`, `G|G1.10a|AT2|AT2-1`, `G|G1.6b|AT2|AT2-1` (the
   per-row cited / existence-only decision is scripted: for each registered name grep the
   `*_PIN_DOCS` documents once and emit the two tables - never decided by reading the docs
   row by row); full gate green; commit. Hands over the measured table lengths and the list of
   rows to cite, per record block, for the notes.
2. `G|G1.10a|AC1|AC4` (the converse rail, scoped as its bullet says) and `G|G1.9|AT1|AT1-1`
   (count rail + the two R9 digit edits), the `LINE_CITED_DOCS` marker re-measure if a digit
   edit moves a count, all §4 notes; full gate green; commit; record.

**Findings**
- `G|G1.3a|SA1|SA-6` (note) - the five threshold registries (`cited >= 4`, `>= 4`, `>= 5`,
  `>= 8`, `>= 12`): the citation half is an aggregate floor, so most pins can lose their prose
  citation green. Fix: replace the threshold in all five by the per-pin citation assert the
  G1.0/G1.7/G1.8/G1.9 registries use; split each table into "cited" rows (per-pin assert) and
  an existence-only sub-table for rows no document names, and reword each test's doc contract
  so it is true. Notes: the rows to cite, per record block.
