### RF-D07-07 — Pin and narrow the props_norm echo rows exposed on r4133-only cases
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-07", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 5} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/props_norm.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp2.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`
**Parts:**
1. `RP|RP2.3|SA1|SA-1`: the two list rows, the two expected-value pins in `props_r4133_pins.rs`, the `CapiAndPin` witnesses, the count lock, then the manifest-crossing derivation test with its mutation check. Commits with the gate green; hands over the pin names, the derivation test's evidence sources and any list row it exposed beyond the two.
2. `RP|RP2.1|AT1|AT1-1`, `RP|RP2.3|AT2|AT2-2`, `RP|RP2.4|AT1|AT-3`, `RP|RP3.3|AT1|AT-2`: the dormant-norm-row list, the triple-list pin, the empty-band replay test, the `generator.model` narrowing with the corpus-gate run in both lanes (Probes), the §4 notes.
**Findings**
- `RP|RP2.3|SA1|SA-1` (major) - `harness/props_norm.rs`, const `ECHO_ROWS_ON_R4133_ONLY_CASES` and its only guard `every_row_exposed_on_r4133_only_cases_names_a_pin`: the guard iterates the list itself, so two echo rows mask 24 cells on `engines:"r4133"` cases with no pin and no list entry - `gictransformer.pctperm` (22 cells, 4 cases, witness `Capi(4)`; capi gates no GICTransformer deck at all) and `gicsource.spectrum` (2 cells on `asymmetric:gic/gic_midi.dss`, `Capi(2)`). Fix: add both pairs to the list, write one expected-value pin each in `props_r4133_pins.rs` (port's live `pctperm` `'100'` vs r4133's frozen `'0'`, `GICTransformer.pas:708`; `spectrum` `''` per `GICsource.pas:327`/`:332`), move both witnesses to `CapiAndPin`, bump the count lock below the list. Then make the list derivable: a test that crosses the four manifests' `engines` with TRACKED per-pair case evidence (`tests/corpus/props_r4133/` extracts, `population.lock.json` - never the untracked `tmp/props_census.json`), honours `ECHO_NARROWED` and `ECHO_CARVE_OUTS`, and fails when an exposed echo row is missing from the list; mutation-check by deleting one list row.
- `RP|RP2.1|AT1|AT1-1` (minor) - `harness/props_norm.rs` `check_rows_are_live` (called from `assert_norm_rows_are_live`, commented in `corpus_gate.rs` near the norm-row liveness call): a never-visited norm row is silent forever, and eight `PROPS_NORM_R4133` rows (`gendispatcher.element/genlist/enabled`, `sensor.currents/element/kvars/kws/enabled`) sit on classes with zero r4133-gating cases, undeclared. Fix: add `NORM_ROWS_WITH_NO_IN_SCOPE_CELL` (the analogue of `ECHO_ROWS_WITH_NO_IN_SCOPE_CELL`), and a test reading `tests/corpus/props_r4133/bins.tsv` + `shape_in_scope.txt` that asserts "every norm row has `cells_in_scope > 0` or is listed, and every listed row has 0" (both directions); amend the `corpus_gate.rs` comment to name the hole. Gating a sensor/gendispatcher deck on r4133 is a follow-up, not this step - record it.
- `RP|RP2.3|AT2|AT2-2` (minor) - `props_norm::tests::every_live_semantics_row_names_a_pin` (asserts only `named.len()` + the deduped name list), `the_echo_table_is_the_measured_row_set` (no witness column) and replay `every_echo_row_pin_is_a_test_that_exists` (set of names only): the (row -> pin) mapping is pinned nowhere, so re-pointing a row at another existing pin stays green. Fix: in `every_live_semantics_row_names_a_pin` assert the full sorted literal list of `(class, prop, pin)` triples; mutation-check by re-pointing `relay.action` at `fault_bus2_renders_the_live_terminal` and seeing it red, then revert.
- `RP|RP2.4|AT1|AT-3` (note) - `harness/props_norm.rs` docs around `R4133_DISPLAY_FLOOR` and the two worst-cell pins: the empty band (worst claimed `6.431124e-05` .. nearest above `1.374769e-03`) exists only in doc comments and one panic message. Fix: add a replay test over the vendored example spellings asserting that no numeric-comparable spelling has `display_rel` strictly inside the band and that the nearest row above is `storagecontroller.kwneed` at `1.374769e-03`, with its own failure message. The floor constant itself is NOT changed (R8).
- `RP|RP3.3|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `harness/props_norm.rs`, the `echo("generator","model", EchoParse, 2, ..)` row: it excludes the r4133 value compare pair-wide although the measured divergence is one spelling (`'4'` vs `'3'`, 2 cells), leaving ~41 further `generator.model` cells on r4133-only cases compared by no channel. Fix: add `generator.model` to `ECHO_NARROWED` with the single spelling `('4','3')`, bump `ECHO_NARROWED_PAIRS` 20 -> 21 and `ECHO_NARROWED_SPELLINGS` 66 -> 67 plus every doc/lock quoting them, and let the other cells compare live. A cell that then diverges is a port bug: do not mask it - stop that finding, write the case/cell to the state file and the record (product code is outside this step's files).
**Probes:** run the corpus gate in both lanes after the `generator.model` narrowing - the live r4133 channel decides whether the ~41 unmasked cells agree (expected: all agree). For the two new pins confirm the r4133 literals against the vendored census (`tests/corpus/props_r4133/examples_full.txt`); use an epri-worker property read on `asymmetric:gic/gic_midi.dss` only if a literal is absent there.
**Acceptance:**
- Two new pins exist in `props_r4133_pins.rs` (GICTransformer `pctperm`, GICSource `spectrum`), both named by `CapiAndPin` witnesses and listed in `ECHO_ROWS_ON_R4133_ONLY_CASES`; the derivation test reds when a list row is removed.
- `NORM_ROWS_WITH_NO_IN_SCOPE_CELL` holds exactly the 8 rows and is pinned both ways against `bins.tsv`/`shape_in_scope.txt`.
- The triple-list pin and the empty-band test exist; `ECHO_NARROWED_PAIRS == 21`.
- No tolerance moves, no golden byte moves, `ledger.json` unchanged; corpus gate green in both lanes.
- Doc notes left for the rp2/rp3 records (row counts 58 -> 60, narrowed 20 -> 21); every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-08 — Bind the RP3.x witness pins to their cases and census literals
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-08", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D07-07"], "n_uids": 5} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-07.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`
**Parts:** (the two RP3.12 uids are exploratory engine replays with an open outcome; the other
three are rails/pins with count locks)
1. `RP|RP3.12|AC1|AC-3` + `RP|RP3.12|AT1|AT-3`: the compile-edit-replay of the `*_both` /
   `midi_autotrans` decks against `examples_supplement.txt`; ends either with the extended pin
   + bumped `RP312_WITNESSED_ROWS` and quoting lock, or with the measured reason written into
   both docs - either way committed with the gate green. Hands over the outcome per spelling
   and the note text for the record.
2. `RP|RP3.2|AT1|AT-2` (the `Deck::compile`-to-ledger rail + the two deck-specific readings),
   `RP|RP3.8|AC2|AC2-5` (the StorageController pin + `engines` tie), `RP|RP3.9|AT2|AT2-1` (the
   RP3.9 census read-back table); full gate green; commit; record.
**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.2|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - pins `windgen_kvar_renders_the_base_on_the_dynamics_deck` / `..._on_the_fault_ride_through_deck` and replay `the_staged_r4133_property_entries_landed_at_rp41`: nothing binds a per-case witness pin to the case its ledger entry names (`Deck::compile` is never parsed in the replay file), and the two kVA-set pins assert identical literals. Fix: add a replay rail that parses the pin source (as `every_echo_row_pin_is_a_test_that_exists` does via `PINS`) and checks that each `LEDGER_ENTRY_PINS` pin's `Deck::compile("...")` literal equals the case of the ledger entry citing it; give each of the two kVA-set pins one deck-specific reading so they stop being interchangeable.
- `RP|RP3.12|AC1|AC-3` (minor) - replay doc above `RP312_WITNESSED_ROWS` (= 2): the reason for witnessing 2 of 8 spellings ("the other six cannot use this pin's construction because their decks open with a snapshot Solve") is asserted, never measured, and the compile-edit-replay construction extends to a snapshot+daily deck. Fix: try extending the RP3.12 pin to the six unwitnessed census spellings (the `*_both` decks: compile, `edit RegControl.rat enabled=no`, reset the tap, replay the tail including the snapshot `Solve` and the daily run) and compare with `tests/corpus/props_r4133/examples_supplement.txt` rows :127, :129-:133; if reproduced bump `RP312_WITNESSED_ROWS` and the quoting lock in `the_rp312_pin_quotes_the_vendored_census_cells`, otherwise replace the asserted reason with the measured one. Exact string compare only.
- `RP|RP3.12|AT1|AT-3` (minor) - the same sentence in the RP3.12 pin's doc (`props_r4133_pins.rs`) and in the replay doc: it is false for at least two of the six rows, which sit on `controls/autotrans/midi_autotrans.dss` - a deck with no leading snapshot `Solve` that the pin already compiles (cells partition `{3,3,2}` = 8 for midi_autotrans, `{3,3,3}` = 9 for midi_autotrans_both). Fix: re-derive which daily steps those two rows are, witness them in the pin if stepping the daily solve reproduces them, else state the true reason; make the pin doc and the replay doc say the same thing, and leave a note for the record's copy.
- `RP|RP3.8|AC2|AC2-5` (note) - replay `RP38_SUPERSEDED` rows for `StorageController.kWTotal`/`kWActual` on `modes:makeposseq/makeposseq_ctrl.dss`: the one measured port<->r4133 divergence (`'33.3333333333333'` vs r4133 `'100'`, `'-0.333333333333333'` vs `'-1'`; r4133 `MakePosSequence` emits `kWrating=` for the property `kWrated`, `PCElements/Storage.pas:3979-3985` vs `:647`, bug not reproduced) has no expected-value pin and nothing ties the disposition to the manifest key. Fix: add a pin in `props_r4133_pins.rs` that runs the deck and asserts the two port literals, name it in the `RP38_SUPERSEDED` row, and assert in the replay file that the case is `engines=capi_v0145` in `population.lock.json` so a channel flip reds the tie.
- `RP|RP3.9|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - replay `RP39_PINS`: the ten RP3.9 pins check their r4133 literals only against their own chain arithmetic; the census read-back `the_rp312_pin_quotes_the_vendored_census_cells` covers RP3.12 only, and its doc says RP3.9 still carries the unguarded pattern. Fix: add `the_rp39_pins_quote_the_vendored_census_cells` in the same shape - a declared, count-locked table of the RP3.9 witnessed r4133 literals, each required verbatim both in the flattened RP3.9 pin bodies and in `tests/corpus/props_r4133/examples_full.txt`; then correct that doc sentence.
**Probes:** no new oracle capture is expected - the r4133 side is the vendored census (`examples_supplement.txt`, `examples_full.txt`). Only if a needed r4133 literal is absent there, read it with epri-worker on the named deck; the engine replay of the six RP3.12 spellings decides "witness" vs "record the measured reason".
**Acceptance:**
- The `Deck::compile`-to-ledger-case rail exists and reds when a pin is re-pointed at the sibling deck (mutation shown in the record).
- `RP312_WITNESSED_ROWS` equals the number of spellings actually reproduced, and the pin doc, replay doc and record note state one measured reason for any remainder.
- A StorageController aggregates pin on `makeposseq_ctrl.dss` exists, is named in `RP38_SUPERSEDED`, and the `engines=capi_v0145` tie is asserted.
- `the_rp39_pins_quote_the_vendored_census_cells` passes with a count lock; no golden byte, tolerance or ledger entry moves.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-09 — Close the census/deck-reader sweep holes (.txt includes, continuations, abbreviations, Edit lines)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-09", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-08"], "n_uids": 10} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-08.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** -
**Parts:** (split along the dependency: the sweeps of part 2 are rebuilt on the readers of
part 1; a hardened reader can move an existing census count lock, which is a finding to
investigate, never a silent re-pin)
1. The reader helpers and their unit rows: `RP|RP3.2|AT1|AT-1` (`element_scope` /
   `named_token`: `More`/`M`, `object=`/`obj=`, blanks around `=`), `RP|RP3.3|SA1|SA-2`
   (abbreviated redirect/compile verbs), `RP|RP3.3|SA1|SA-3` (the `set a=ncim` message),
   `RP|RP3.2|AC1|AC-6` + `RP|RP3.2|AC1|AC-5` (`windgen_kvar_base` branches + symbol cite);
   full gate green; commit. Hands over the helper signatures and every count lock that moved
   (with the cell that moved it).
2. The five sweeps built on them: `RP|RP3.12|AT1|AT-4` (RegControl x AutoTrans discovery +
   mutated-deck tripwire), `RP|RP3.1|AT1|AT-1` (`.txt`-include sweeps for RP3.1/RP3.2/RP3.4),
   `RP|RP3.2|SA1|SA-4` (Edit-only `kvar=` filter), `RP|RP3.3|AT1|AT-4` (redirect assert),
   `RP|RP3.6|AT1|AT-2` (standalone `switch=` sweep); full gate green; commit; record.
**Findings** (all in `props_r4133_replay.rs`; r4133 paths are under `.inputs/electricdss-code-r4133-trunk/Version8/Source/`)
- `RP|RP3.12|AT1|AT-4` (minor) - `the_autotrans_typecast_cases_pair_their_r4133_channel_with_a_skip_entry` loops over the four hardcoded `RP312_STAGED_SKIPS` cases, so a new RegControl-on-AutoTrans deck fires no guard. Fix: discover every deck declaring both a `RegControl` and an `AutoTrans` object, assert the found case set equals the `RP312_STAGED_SKIPS` cases, and add a synthetic mutated-deck tripwire. Build the scan from this step's own hardened readers, not from `corpus_gate/manifest.rs::collect_gictransformer_decks` (it scans only `*.dss` and only bare `New GICTransformer`, blind to `New object=` and to `.txt`/`.dat` redirect targets - RF-D10-01 `G|G1.4a|AT2|AT2-1` hardens it in RF-D10-01, an independent step that may run before or after this one; cite it only for the manifest-crossing shape): use the extended `element_scope` / `named_token` of `RP|RP3.2|AT1|AT-1` below (`object=`/`obj=`, `More`/`M`, blanks around `=`) and walk `redirected_non_dss_scripts(&decks)` like the `RP|RP3.1|AT1|AT-1` sweeps, so the scan does not reproduce the blind spots this step closes for its other sweeps. The scanner must ignore comments (`midi_autotrans_asym.dss`) and a `Transformer` configured as an autotransformer (`electricdss-tst/Test/TestAuto.dss`).
- `RP|RP3.1|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - `collect_dss` keeps only `*.dss`, while corpus decks redirect `.txt` includes that declare elements; the RP3.1 completeness claim (doc of `RP31_DELAY_CASES`) is therefore extension-scoped. Fix: for every RP3.x census sweep built on `collect_dss` (RP3.1 SwtControl, RP3.2 WindGen, RP3.4 GIC) also run the class scanner over `redirected_non_dss_scripts(&decks)` (as the RP3.3 sweep already does) and assert no hit outside the cited tables; align the doc sentences on `RP31_DELAY_CASES` and the RP3.2 sweep with the real universe.
- `RP|RP3.2|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - `element_scope` / `named_token` miss three legal spellings: `More`/`M` continuations (`Executive/ExecCommands.pas:56-58`), the `object=`/`obj=` form of New/Edit (`Executive/ExecHelper.pas:194,207`), and blanks around `=` (`Parser/ParserDel.pas:431-436`). Fix: teach both helpers the three forms and add one unit case each next to the existing quoted-form case.
- `RP|RP3.2|SA1|SA-4` (minor) - the RP3.2 "no deck types `kvar=`" sweep filters `measured` on `declared > 0`, so an `Edit WindGen.x kvar=` in a non-declaring driver file never reaches the compare. Fix: filter on `declared > 0 ||` any edited token (or assert separately that no non-declaring corpus file types `kvar=` on a WindGen Edit/BatchEdit) and add the mutation witness on a synthetic non-declaring driver text.
- `RP|RP3.3|AT1|AT-4` (note) - the `RP33_NCIM_HELD_OUT` loop asserts the hard-coded sibling path's generator counts but never that the held-out master redirects that file. Fix: assert `redirect_targets(case_deck(master))` contains the sibling path, compared case-insensitively (the corpus spells `Generators.DSS`).
- `RP|RP3.3|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `redirect_targets` / `redirect_args` read only the full verbs `redirect`/`compile`, while r4133 resolves command abbreviations (`Shared/Command.pas:65,88`, `Shared/HashList.pas:335-357` `FindAbbrev`, linear in `ExecCommand` order; `Executive/ExecCommands.pas:67,73`). Fix: accept every prefix that `FindAbbrev` would resolve to Redirect/Compile - mirror the `ExecCommand` order, or conservatively treat any prefix of either verb as a redirect (wider than the engine is fine, say so in the doc); add abbreviated-verb rows to the redirect leg of `the_ncim_sweep_reads_every_spelling_the_engine_accepts`.
- `RP|RP3.3|SA1|SA-3` (note) - the positive-loop assertion message in `the_ncim_sweep_reads_every_spelling_the_engine_accepts` claims `set a=ncim` selects NCIM in the engine; in r4133 `a` resolves to `Addtype` and `al` to `Allowduplicates` (`Executive/ExecOptions.pas:76-84`), only `alg`+ reaches `Algorithm`. Fix: correct the message (the reader is deliberately wider, as `algorithm_values`' doc says); optionally split the loop into engine-true and reader-only rows.
- `RP|RP3.6|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `switch_linecode_line_decls` counts only `linecode=` and `switch=` on the same physical command, although arm 15 also fires from a standalone later `Edit Line.x switch=yes`. Fix: in `the_rp36_census_decomposition_is_read_off_the_corpus` add a sweep asserting the corpus holds zero standalone `Edit Line.<name> ... switch=` commands (no `linecode=`) that target a linecode-declared line (per-deck name join), plus a self-test row for the helper.
- `RP|RP3.2|AC1|AC-6` (note) - `windgen_kvar_base` claims "bit-for-bit" but omits `SyncUpPowerQuantities`' `pf < 0 => kvarBase := -kvarBase` (`PCElements/WindGen.pas:3028`, port `elements/pc/windgen/nominal.rs` `sync_up_power_quantities`) and derives a value where the engine keeps Create's `kvarBase = 60.0` when none of `kW=`/`pf=`/`kVA=` is typed (`WindGen.pas:912`). Fix: add both branches and two unit cases; if either cannot be made faithful, weaken the doc wording instead.
- `RP|RP3.2|AC1|AC-5` (note) - `windgen_kvar_base` doc cites `nominal.rs:280-281` for the kVA-set re-derivation; the code moved. Fix: cite by symbol - the WindGen recalc branch `if !self.kva_not_set` in `elements/pc/windgen/nominal.rs`.
**Acceptance:**
- New self-test rows exist for: `More`/`M`, `object=`/`obj=`, blanks around `=`, abbreviated redirect/compile verbs, the standalone-Edit `switch=` shape, the two `windgen_kvar_base` branches.
- The RegControl-x-AutoTrans corpus scan equals the `RP312_STAGED_SKIPS` case set and its mutated-deck tripwire reds.
- The `.txt`-include sweeps find no SwtControl/WindGen/GIC declaration outside the cited tables; every existing census count lock is unchanged (a count that moves is a real finding - record it, never re-pin silently).
- No golden byte, ledger entry or tolerance moves; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D07-10 — Add read-back and existence guards for the replay constants, dispositions and staged ledger drafts
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D07-10", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-09"], "n_uids": 10} -->

- `RP|RP2.2|AC1|AC-2` (note) - `rp22_settled_every_pair_it_was_handed` accepts a citation by `cite.contains(".pas:")`, which passes `"Foo.pas: read it"`. Fix: one shared helper requiring `.pas:<digit>` (or a `:NNN` ref after the literal `no GetPropertyValue override`), applied to every sibling `contains(".pas:")` guard site in the file (six at triage time); all shipped rows already carry digits.

Fix: replace with one hand-rolled shape scan equivalent to `Version8/Source/\S+\.pas:\d` (no new dependency), sharing the helper above; prove it by the mutation "r4133 cite loses its line, capi cite keeps one" -> red.
- `RP|RP3.9|AT2|AT2-5` (note) - in `the_rp39_pin_list_is_pinned` only the `PIN` disposition has teeth; `LEDGER`/`FIX`/`OPEN` and the `STATE_DIFFERS` verdict carry no enforced obligation (dormant: all 27 rows are `PIN`/`PRECISION_ROUNDTRIP`). Fix, decided together with AC2-3 below: either enforce (a `LEDGER` pair appears in `LEDGER_ENTRY_PINS`, a `FIX`/`OPEN` pair names a witness/route, `STATE_DIFFERS` requires a non-`PIN` disposition) or shrink both vocabularies to the values in use. Prefer enforcing; record the choice.

Fix: add a len-locked `RP36B_PINS` table plus an existence + record-citation guard in the RP3.11/RP3.13 shape (read the two files with `include_str!`, match with `names_identifier`); the pin files themselves are not edited.
- `RP|RP3.9|AC2|AC2-3` (minor) - `RP39_DISPOSITIONS` and `RP39_SETTLED_VERDICTS` are used only in `contains()` membership tests, never pinned literally, contrary to the record. Fix: add literal `assert_eq!` pins in the shape of `the_settled_outcome_taxonomy_is_the_plans_three` (`["PIN","FIX","LEDGER","OPEN"]`, `["PRECISION_ROUNDTRIP","STATE_DIFFERS"]`, or the shrunk sets if AT2-5 shrinks them); leave a note for the record sentence "both pinned literally".

The file still exists in the MAIN checkout (`E:/RustProject/dss-rs/tmp/rp312/staged_ledger.md`, not in a worktree; read it by absolute path, R1-style). ### RF-D08-01 — Drive the ledger envelope/rewrite rails and fix ledger.rs doc drift
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-01", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": [], "n_uids": 13} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`
**Findings** (all in `corpus_gate/ledger.rs` unless said otherwise; line numbers drifted — locate by symbol)
**Reading rule:** never open `ledger.rs` (4.5k lines) or `corpus_gate.rs` (2.9k) whole — `rg -n` the symbol (`envelope_element`, `rewrite_element_selected`, `seq_envelope_fixture`, `bus_array_suppressions`, `corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines`, the named tests) and read a bounded range around it. The AT1 measurement is one unfiltered `cargo test -p dss-core --test corpus_gate -- --nocapture` with stderr redirected to a scratchpad file; extract the D11(2) suppression print with `rg`, never read that file whole (tens of thousands of lines).
- `G|G1.3a|AC1|AC1` (minor) - `rewrite_element_selected`, the three G1.3a polar blocks: five loops (currents, powers, currents_mag_ang, voltages_mag_ang, residuals) are bounded only by the oracle cap's lengths, so a short port snapshot panics on an index before `compare_element_derived` prints its Enabled/length diagnostic. Fix: bound each loop by the snapshot length too (`.min(snap.<arr>.len())`, as the `phase_losses` block already does) and add unit test `a_short_snapshot_survives_the_rewrite_to_the_length_diagnostic` (snapshot shorter than the cap: no panic, tail untouched).
- `G|G1.3a|AC1|AC2` (minor) - `envelope_element`, polar closure: no test pins the `record("{label}.mag", ...)` half (all three polar drives use equal magnitudes), while the file and TOLERANCE_NOTES claim the magnitude is never skipped. Fix: add `an_unmasked_polar_magnitude_still_hits_the_envelope` (cap magnitude != snap magnitude, equal angles; `#[should_panic(expected = "cma[0].mag")]` or an exceeded-floor assert) plus the vma/res analogue, so deleting the `.mag` record reds a test.
- `G|G1.3b|AC3|AC3-1` (minor) - the two G1.3b doc blocks (the `SUBCHANNEL_FIELDS` G1.3b paragraph and the header above `seq_slot_is_banded`) state the pre-D31 guarantee unconditionally ("neither envelope-checked nor rewritten", "same slots by construction"). Fix: qualify both — the guarantee holds for `divergence` scopes; an `exclusion` naming `seq_powers` neutralizes every slot of the PosSeqSinglePhase power array (D31), pinned by `an_exclusion_scope_neutralizes_the_whole_posseq_seq_powers_array`; cross-reference TESTING.md.
- `G|G1.3b|AC3|AC3-2` (note) - the comment bound to `harness::seq_terminal_bands` (in the rewrite's seq block) cites r4133 `:47-49` / capi `:252-254` for the "terminal's first three conductors"; those are the n/a-arm zeroing lines. Fix: cite the real three-conductor reads, r4133 `DDLL/DCktElement.pas:69-70` and capi `CAPI/CAPI_Alt.pas:279-281`; re-verify the positive-sequence pair (`:764-766` / `:559-561`) against the vendored sources while there.
- `G|G1.3b|AT3|AT3-1` (minor) - `seq_envelope_fixture`: `bv == bi` bit-for-bit and `cap.seq_i == cap.seq_v`, so a band or oracle-base swap inside `envelope_element` changes no verdict. Fix: make the fixture asymmetric (distinct V and I magnitudes, e.g. 7200 V vs 150 A as `harness::seq_floors` does), drive at a tier where `v_rel/v_abs != i_rel/i_abs` (`tol_for("feeder")`) with a diff between the two bands; one such test per channel: `seq_voltages`, `seq_currents`, `seq_powers`.
- `G|G1.3c|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - the `cplx_seq_voltages` half of the banded-slot discrete rail is never driven: the `seq_slot_is_banded` filter in the envelope and the `seq_slots` filter in the rewrite can both be deleted with no red. Fix: add voltage-channel twins on non-ThreePhase arms (PositiveSequence and NotAvailable fixtures) — loop `the_cplx_rewrite_and_the_cplx_envelope_cover_the_same_slots` over both channels and add `a_discrete_cplx_voltage_slot_is_never_neutralized_by_a_scope`.
- `G|G1.3c|AT2|AT2-2` (note) - same comment as AC3-2: all four Pascal anchors name the wrong construct and contradict `seq_terminal_bands`' own doc. Fix (one edit with AC3-2): 3-phase slice = r4133 `DCktElement.pas:69-70` / capi `CAPI_Alt.pas:279-281`; posseq arm takes `cBuffer^[1+k]` at r4133 `:54` / capi `:260`; copy the pairs from the `harness::seq_terminal_bands` doc (`harness/mod.rs`) or cite that doc instead.
- `G|G1.3c|AT2|AT2-4` (note) - `envelope_element` gates the `cplx_seq_*` loops on another channel's array (`!ec.seq_i.is_empty()`) while the rewrite is bounded by the complex arrays' own lengths. Fix: gate the cplx loops on the complex arrays' own lengths, or pin the coupling with a unit test (capture with non-empty `cplx_seq_*` and empty `seq_i` must not measure nothing while the rewrite neutralizes).
- `G|G1.3d(ii)|AC2|AC2-4` (note) - the `phase_losses` envelope block comment claims a widened-but-dead channel "trips `Scope::dead_channels` instead of masking". Fix: qualify — `dead_channels` polices `divergence` scopes only; the eight committed `phase_losses` scopes are exclusions backed by `measured.g13d2_phase_losses_first_failure`, not by staleness.
- `G|G1.4a|SA1|SA-1` (note) - doc block of `bus_array_suppressions` still says the runner passes `excluded("voltages", None, step)` as `voltages_excluded`. Fix: rewrite the sentence — the runner passes `LedgerView::bus_arrays_suppressed(step)` (deck-wide `voltages` exclusion, no `name_re`/`node_re`).
- `G|G1.6(i)|AC2|AC2-3` (note) - the rationale for the exclusion-only `reliability` field ("`compare_reliability` compares every value EXACTLY ... no envelope") is false since D17a. Fix: state that `compare_reliability` is exact except the three D17a banded cells (`Meters.Totals` at the energy tier; `calc_current` / `alloc_factors` at the current tier — `harness/mod.rs`, TOLERANCE_NOTES.md) and that the field stays exclusion-only by conservative choice.
- `RP|RP2.1|AC2|AC2-2` (note) - RP2.1 inserted `fn property_scopes` between `property_handled_keys` and its doc, so the orphaned block ("Element property-value pairs handled by a `property` scope ... F1/F2 + RR-1") now documents the wrong function. Fix: move that block onto `pub(crate) fn property_handled_keys`; give `property_scopes` its own short doc.
- `G|G1.4a|AT1|AT1` (minor) - `corpus_gate.rs::corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines` + `ledger.rs::bus_array_suppressions`: the D11(2) bus-array suppression population is `eprintln!`ed, never asserted, so it can widen with any future deck-wide `voltages` exclusion. Fix: add a fail-on-stale-both-ways constant (e.g. `BUS_ARRAY_SUPPRESSIONS`: the sorted (case, channel) pair list, or at least its count) asserted against `run.ledger.bus_array_suppressions()` on an unfiltered run, following the `scheduler.rs::TOPOLOGY_STALE_DECLINES` precedent; keep the eprintln; the literal is measured from one full gate run. Leave §4 notes for TESTING.md (G1.4a section, ~"D11(2)") and a record line for golden-rebase.md.
**Parts:**
1. AT1 first (measure the D11(2) suppression population from one unfiltered run → the constant → both-direction offline drives), then AC1 (bounded loops + `a_short_snapshot_survives_the_rewrite_to_the_length_diagnostic`), AC2 (the `.mag` drives) and the seven doc/comment uids (AC3-1, AC3-2, AT2-2, AC2-4, SA-1, AC2-3, AC2-2). Gate, commit; `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D08-01.md` names the constant, the measured literal and the mutation results.
2. The seq-channel rails: AT3-1 (three asymmetric fixtures / drives), AT2-1 (the `cplx_seq_voltages` twins), AT2-4 (gate the cplx loops on their own lengths or pin the coupling). Gate, commit; record.
**Acceptance:**
- New tests exist and pass in both lanes: `a_short_snapshot_survives_the_rewrite_to_the_length_diagnostic`, `an_unmasked_polar_magnitude_still_hits_the_envelope` (+ vma/res analogue), the three asymmetric seq-channel envelope drives, the `cplx_seq_voltages` rail twins; each is shown to red when the guarded line is removed (state the mutation in the record).
- The bus-array suppression constant is asserted in the live gate, measured not guessed, and reds in both directions (offline drive for each direction).
- `tests/corpus/ledger.json` and every golden are untouched; no tolerance changes.
- Notes left for `TESTING.md` and `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D08-02 — Harden ledger.json load-time guards with negative drives
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-02", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D08-01"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D08-01.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`
**Findings** (all in `corpus_gate/ledger.rs`; the committed `tests/corpus/ledger.json` must load unchanged after every fix)
- `G|G1.0|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - reader structs `RawLedger` / `RawEntry` / `RawScope` accept unknown keys, so a mistyped selector (`name_regex`, `step`, ...) loads cleanly and silently widens an exclusion to deck-wide / all-steps. Fix: add `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` to all three (first take a SCRIPTED key census — a few lines of python over `tests/corpus/ledger.json`: the set of top-level keys, entry keys, scope keys, `measured` keys and every comment-style key, over all entries, written to `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D08-02.md`; the file is 234 KB and is never read into context — and make the committed ledger load under the new structs BEFORE any other edit of this step, since one missed key makes `deny_unknown_fields` refuse the whole file) and add `a_mistyped_scope_selector_is_refused_at_load` (a scope with `name_regex` / `step` must fail to parse). Note the rule for the TESTING.md ledger-schema section.
- `G|G1.0|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `SUBCHANNEL_FIELDS` has no "honoured by the runtime" drive: a name added without a matching `want("...")` arm in `rewrite_element_selected` / `envelope_element` selects nothing yet reports itself applied. Fix: add `every_declared_sub_channel_is_honoured_by_the_runtime` — for each (field, name) drive both functions with a scope selecting only that name over a fixture where every channel differs; assert that channel and only it is rewritten / measured. Note the step for the TESTING.md "add a sub-channel" recipe.
- `G|G1.4b|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - a `distance` scope without `name_re` masks the distance equality on every bus of a (case, channel) and can never go stale; nothing at load refuses it. Fix: refuse a bare `distance` scope at load (consider the other `PER_VALUE_EXCLUSION_FIELDS` too; mirror the `BARE_CHANNELS_ALLOWED` pattern), add loader test `a_bare_distance_scope_is_refused`, and adjust `every_exclusion_field_is_honoured_by_the_runtime`, which currently drives the bare form. Every committed distance scope already has `name_re`.
- `G|G1.9|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `the_aggregate_value_arms_inherit_exactly_the_recorded_element_scopes` keys its recorded set on the `losses` sub-channel only, but `Circuit.TotalPower` slack is driven by `powers`. Fix: key on `selects_losses || selects_powers`; today's 8 powers-selecting pairs are a subset of the recorded 12, so the recorded set must not change (verify). Note for TESTING.md: the claim "keeps the arm running field-by-field on a scoped deck" holds only for a currents-only scope.
- `G|G2.5|AT1|AT-4` (minor) - `assert_structural`'s exclusion-scope refusal whitelists `channel_idx` (and `node_re` outside `voltages`), which no exclusion path consumes; its rationale comment wrongly says `excluded()` reads them. Fix: refuse `channel_idx` on any exclusion scope and `node_re` on an exclusion whose field is not `voltages`; correct the comment to what is really read (`field` / `steps` / `name_re`; `node_re` via `voltage_keep_mask`; `channels` via the element handlers). Drive both refusals in the SA-4 test below.
- `G|G1.0|AT2|AT2-2` (note) - the `channels` rules are documented as enforced "at load", but `LedgerRuntime::load()` applies none; they bite only inside the `ledger_is_structurally_valid` test. Fix (preferred): call `check_scope_channels` per scope from `LedgerRuntime::load()` so the live gate refuses a dead selector (touch `runner.rs` only if the load call site needs it); keep the existing test as the drive. Fallback: reword the doc block above `check_scope_channels`, the test name and TESTING.md to "refused by `ledger_is_structurally_valid`".
- `G|G2.5|SA1|SA-4` (note) - the exclusion-scope envelope/pin refusal in `assert_structural` has no negative drive and no committed entry of that shape. Fix: factor it into `check_exclusion_scope(id, kind, &RawScope) -> Result<(), String>` (shape of `check_scope_channels`) and add `an_exclusion_scope_that_carries_an_envelope_is_refused` driving each refused key (`max_rel`, `max_abs`, `num_rel`, `rust`, `oracle`, `policy`, `line_re`, plus AT-4's `channel_idx` / foreign `node_re`) and one clean selector-only scope that passes.
**Acceptance:**
- The five new tests named above exist and pass in both lanes; `ledger_is_structurally_valid` and the live gate stay green with `tests/corpus/ledger.json` byte-unchanged.
- Each new refusal has a negative drive that fails on the refused shape and a positive control that passes.
- The G1.9 recorded set is unchanged (12 pairs) after the key widening.
- TESTING.md notes left per §4; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D08-03 — Give element exclusions and dead_channels exemptions a fail-on-stale liveness signal
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-03", "effort": "max", "parts": 3, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D06-04", "RF-D06-05", "RF-D08-02"], "n_uids": 2} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-04, RF-D06-05, RF-D08-02.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/ledger.rs`, `tests/corpus/ledger.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G2.5|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `ledger.rs`, `LedgerView::element_rewrites`, its exclusion arm (the `else` of `if e.kind == Kind::Divergence` that only calls `mark_applied`; there is NO `apply_element_scopes` at HEAD - locate by symbol): `element` exclusions have no staleness signal, although the handler already holds both sides (`snap`, `ec`, `tol`) and `envelope_element` already measures them; the G2.5 settlement's "would need a second comparator" ground is false for `element`. Fix: in that exclusion arm run the same measurement `envelope_element` uses in a measure-only mode (record with `measure_note` and `mark_exceeded` exactly as the `voltages` exclusion arm of `voltage_keep_mask` already does) and mark each selected sub-channel "exceeded" when it is over floor; widen `has_measurable_scope` from `voltages` to `voltages | element` so the existing `Kind::Exclusion && has_measurable_scope && !exceeded` STALE check in `assert_all_hit` covers `element` scopes, and extend `dead_channels` to them. The liveness rule is the same hit-on-mask semantics RF-D06-04 (`RP|RP3.10|AC1|AC-2`, in **After**) gave `variables` exclusions: an exclusion is live only when it masked a value that would otherwise have failed the floor. Then re-measure the corpus: any exclusion sub-channel that masks nothing is narrowed in `ledger.json` (never widened), and the "NOT policed" premise in the ledger `source` texts (the two entries that say so), TESTING.md and the phase record is corrected via notes.
- `G|G1.3c|SA1|SA-4` (minor) - `ledger.rs`, the comment census beside `Scope::dead_channels` ("114 / 103"): hand-maintained prose with no fail-on-stale constant, and the "measured provenance" it calls the whole liveness proof is an unenforced optional field. Fix: replace the prose with a constant (e.g. `DEAD_CHANNEL_EXEMPT_CENSUS = (114, 103)`, re-counted at edit time) re-derived from `ledger.json` in a test, and enforce that every non-divergence entry carrying element `channels` has `measured` (+ `source`); backfill the six entries lacking it from real gate output or allowlist them explicitly with a reason.
**Parts:**
1. Measurement + core check only — REPORT-ONLY in the live gate, `tests/corpus/ledger.json` byte-unchanged, gate green by construction. In the `LedgerView::element_rewrites` exclusion arm run the measurement `envelope_element` uses in a measure-only mode (`measure_note`, `mark_channel_exceeded` per selected sub-channel over floor, `mark_exceeded` on the entry — the hit-on-mask rule RF-D06-04 gave `variables`). Put the new STALE verdict for `element` exclusions in an injectable `check_element_exclusion_liveness(...) -> Result<(), String>` (shape of `check_scope_channels`) driven by offline unit tests (a dead exclusion sub-channel reds, a live one passes, a `divergence` entry is unaffected). The two enforcement edges — `has_measurable_scope` widened to `voltages | element` (the entry-level STALE arm) and the sub-channel STALE loop's `if e.kind != Kind::Divergence { continue; }` — are NOT flipped here: `assert_all_hit` only prints the per-sub-channel dead/live verdict under the existing `DSS_LEDGER_MEASURE` stderr aid. (`corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines` runs unconditionally inside `cargo test --workspace` in both lanes, so an enforced check with the committed ledger would red this part's gate on every dead sub-channel the step names as a candidate.) Commits; the state file names the check function, the fixtures/tests and how the report lines are read from the gate's stderr.
2. Run the live corpus gate in BOTH lanes (default and `--features dss-core/oracle-parity`) with `DSS_LEDGER_MEASURE=1`, stderr redirected to one scratchpad file per lane (never read whole — `rg` the report lines). Verdict per excluded `element` sub-channel over both lane runs: over floor on at least one case-step in BOTH lanes → live, stays; never over floor in EITHER lane → dead, narrowed out of the scope; live in one lane and dead in the other → kept and registered by (entry id, sub-channel) in the reasoned allowlist part 3 formalises (create the const here, e.g. `ELEMENT_EXCLUSION_LIVENESS_ALLOWLIST`, each row with a reason) — an exclusion is what a cause gets when its channel cannot be measured reliably (the D12 note above the sub-channel loop), so a one-lane verdict must never become a flaky STALE. Known candidates: the `capi-capcontrol-time-bus...` entry, `r4133-posseq-seqpowers-slot-gic`, the nine widened sets. Apply the narrowing with a small python script over the JSON (load preserving key order, edit only the `channels` arrays of the named entries, dump with the committed indent), then `git diff --stat` must show `tests/corpus/ledger.json` alone and `git diff` only the intended `channels` lines — if the dump reformats anything else, edit those entries textually instead; `ledger.json` (234 KB) is never read into context. In the SAME commit flip the live gate from report-only to enforced (widen `has_measurable_scope`, extend the sub-channel STALE arm past `Kind::Divergence`), so the commit is green by construction. If a narrowed entry is listed in the exactly-asserted register RF-D06-05 (`G|G1.3b|AT2|AT2-1`, in **After**) added to `ledger.rs` (entries rewriting a polar sub-channel with `max_rel > 1e-8` - the capcontrol entry is its named example), update that register in the same commit so its exact assertion stays true. Commits; the state file holds the measured per-entry dead/live list per lane and the new census numbers.
3. Turn the `dead_channels` census into the enforced constant with the mandatory measured-provenance rule (one reasoned allowlist by id covers both the missing-`measured` entries and part 2's lane-split sub-channels), negative drives for both (stale census row, missing `measured`), and the §4 notes for `TESTING.md` and `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`. The constant makes every LATER `ledger.json` edit that adds, narrows or removes an entry carrying element `channels` move it: write that rule into the constant's doc, into the `TESTING.md` note and into `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D08-03.md` ("a ledger.json change to an entry with element `channels` updates `DEAD_CHANNEL_EXEMPT_CENSUS` in `corpus_gate/ledger.rs` in the same commit"), so the later ledger steps (RF-D01-19, RF-D02-04, RF-D02-08, RF-D02-14, RF-D03-08) see it.
**Probes:** the unified live corpus gate itself (both channels: dss-python `capi_v0145` and epri-worker `r4133`) run in measure mode in part 2, once per lane. Per excluded `element` sub-channel it decides: over floor on at least one case-step in both lanes → the exclusion is live and stays; never over floor in either lane → it masks nothing and is narrowed out of the scope; split verdict → kept and allowlisted by id with the reason. Backfilled `measured` values (part 3) must come from that run's output, never typed by hand.
**Acceptance:**
- A dead `element` exclusion sub-channel now fails `assert_all_hit` (offline drive from part 1; enforced in the live gate from part 2's commit on — part 1 commits report-only with `ledger.json` byte-unchanged and the gate green); the census constant reds on drift in both directions; an entry with `channels` and no `measured` is refused unless allowlisted by id with a reason.
- `ledger.json` changes are narrowing or provenance-only: no scope widened, no entry added, no tolerance or golden byte moves (R8).
- Full gate green in both lanes with the narrowed ledger; `lane_diff` not required (no engine code touched).
- Notes left for `TESTING.md` and the golden-rebase record; both uids closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D08-04 — Fix corpus_gate.rs bus/SC capture rails, guards and stale docs
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-04", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D01-14", "RF-D06-04", "RF-D08-01"], "n_uids": 14} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D01-14, RF-D06-04, RF-D08-01.

Fix: leave a TESTING.md note ("five" → "six"); fix any echoed "five" in the comments around `BUS_READ_ORDER` (`rg "five"` in the file).
- `G|G1.4d|AC1|AC2` (note) - `tools/oracle/oracle_server.py`, the capture group-C justification in the at-bus (PCE/PDE) block, and its copies in `corpus_gate.rs` (doc of `AT_BUS_READ_ORDER`) and TESTING.md: "the enumerator moves no global cursor" is wrong for half the surface. Fix in all three copies (TESTING.md by note): capi `getPDEatBus` uses the enumerator, `getPCEatBus` walks `DSSClassList.Get(i)` (moves the class-list ActiveItem), and the enumerator itself writes `lst.ActiveItem` on exhaustion (`DSSPointerList.pas:175-188`); none of it is observable; the real asymmetry with r4133 is that capi never assigns `ActiveCktElement` / `ActiveDSSObject`. Keep the group-C conclusion. Comment-only in the python file (the capture-order source gates scan it — keep every marker line intact). Ordering: RF-D01-14 (`G|G1.4d|AT2|AT2-1`) has already rewritten the "capi at-bus enumerator is pure" claim at the same `corpus_gate.rs` comment (MoveNext writes `lst.ActiveItem` at exhaustion) — start from RF-D01-14's wording, extend it ONCE with the `getPCEatBus` / `ActiveCktElement` facts above, then mirror that final text into `oracle_server.py` and the TESTING.md note; do not write a second version of the claim.
- `G|G1.10a|AT3|AT3-1` (minor) - `the_two_transports_agree_on_the_bus_distances_of_a_metered_both_case` drives both oracle transports on a corpus deck with no `runner::CorpusGuard` (D35(3) rail). - `G|G1.3b|AC1|AC1-1` (minor) - the comment introducing the sequence-arm census guard inside `corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines` ("ZERO ledger rows", r4133 arm reached "never") is false since D31. Fix: rewrite to the post-D31 facts: `SEQ_ARM_CENSUS_MEASURED = (297_867, 78, 4)` (`harness/mod.rs`; re-read the literal), the r4133 arm is reached by exactly 4 cells under ledger entry `r4133-posseq-seqpowers-slot-gic`, the guard reds above OR below; re-count "24 sibling fixtures" against `seq_floors`.
- `G|G1.4a|AC2|AC2-5` (note) - `the_bus_capture_reads_in_one_fixed_order_on_both_transports`: the r4133 "no element-scoped read" forbidden list cannot see the bridge's `ckt_element_*` accessors. Fix: add `ckt_element_` to the r4133 forbidden list there and to the SC-segment list in `the_short_circuit_capture_reads_in_one_fixed_order_on_both_transports`; extend the non-vacuity drive with a `.ckt_element_num_terminals(` insertion.

- `G|G1.4b|AT1|AT-2` (note) - same D9 pin: its "non-vacuity" rail asserts over the compile-time literal `want`. Fix: assert on the MEASURED array (`dss.all_bus_distances().iter().any(|d| *d != 0.0)`), as the harness sibling does.
- `G|G1.4d|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - helper `makeposseq_xfmr_solved` issues `set datapath=<scratch>` BEFORE `compile`, so compile re-points the data path and the helper runs in the live case dir `tests/corpus/modes/makeposseq/` without a guard. Fix: really strip python `#` comments and trailing Rust `//` comments, scan the capi side case-insensitively, add `Text.Command` as the capi twin, correct the comment about the segment (runs to end of body, includes the G1.4d at-bus block). ### RF-D08-05 — Make CorpusGuard survivor, capi clear fault and r4133 props-walk alarms fail the gate
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-05", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D08-02", "RF-D08-04"], "n_uids": 4} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D08-02, RF-D08-04.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/props_norm.rs`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp4.md`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `docs/upgrade/DIVERGENCES.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G1.10a|AT1|AT1-2` (minor) - `runner.rs` (consumer of the capi reply field `teardown_error`, produced in `tools/oracle/oracle_server.py`, read-only here): a failing teardown `clear` is only `eprintln!`ed, which libtest discards on a green run, so D33(1) "recorded, never swallowed" does not hold. Fix: collect the `teardown_error` producers during the gate run and assert in the epilogue of `corpus_gate_all_cases_match_engines` that the set equals a bounded census const (expected: the two `modes:autoadd` decks on `capi_v0145`, `autoadd.dss` and `autoadd_cap.dss` — measure, do not assume), failing on a new producer AND on a stale row, in the style of `SCRATCH_FILE_DECLINES` / `FORCED_RUN_FILES_POPULATION`; keep the eprintln. Notes for TESTING.md (next to D33(1)) and DIVERGENCES.md if the census is a recorded capi behaviour.
- `G|G1.10a|AC4|AC4-2` (minor) - `runner.rs::CorpusGuard` (`impl Drop`): the gate's own outer guard only `eprintln!`s survivors from `Drop`, failing nothing — exactly for `kind=large*`, where it is the sole port-side sweeper; the plan/record claim "a case failure naming its producer ... all three producers". Fix: give `CorpusGuard` an explicit `finish()` (or `assert_swept_clean`) that sweeps while the claim is held and panics on survivors when not already panicking; call it on the outer guard in `scheduler::run_one_case` (at least for `large*` / cases with no `RunFileProbe`); keep Drop's eprintln as the unwind fallback; add a portable fixture (the current one is `#[cfg(windows)]`) or correct the "every platform" doc. Notes to correct the wording in GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md (G1.10a, D32(2)), the golden-rebase record and STATUS.md.
- `RP|RP4.1|AT1|AT1-1` (minor) - `scheduler.rs` (guard doc near `FORCED_PROPS_POPULATION`) + `props_norm.rs::check_r4133_props_compare_ran`: the re-mask alarm is a boolean (`walks > 0 && elements > 0`), so restoring `compare_all_properties = false` on the r4133 arm of `run_one_case` would silently stop property-comparing all 312 `both` cases. Fix: make it a population pin — on an unfiltered run the r4133 gating walk count must equal the r4133 term of `FORCED_PROPS_POPULATION` (312 both + 87 r4133-only) minus ledger channel skips, re-derived from `build_unified_cases()`, fail-on-stale both ways (same for the capi channel); keep the `should_panic` pins and add one for a partial mask; build `seed_one`'s request through the same helper as `run_one_case` so one site owns the flag. Correct the guard doc and, in `r4133-props-rp4.md`, the settlement bullet that claims partial re-masks are caught.
- `RP|RP4.1|AT2|AT2-1` (major) - `props_norm.rs`: unit test `the_global_props_compare_guard_is_silent_when_the_walk_ran` calls the SHIPPED writer `record_r4133_props_walk(3)` on the process-global statics the gate epilogue (`assert_r4133_props_compare_ran`) reads, so the alarm can never fire in the gate binary. Fix: split the writer into a counter-parameterised core (e.g. `record_walk_into(&AtomicUsize, &AtomicUsize, n)`) that the test drives on LOCAL atomics; the shipped statics are written only from the gating call site; add a source rail that no `#[cfg(test)]` code calls the shipped writer. Do this FIRST (AT1-1's population pin is meaningless while the test pollutes the counters), then re-measure and correct the walk/element figures in `r4133-props-rp4.md` (expected −1 walk / −3 elements), the docs above the guard and the `corpus_gate.rs` epilogue comment; TESTING.md re-mask section by note.
**Acceptance:**
- Mutation proof recorded for each alarm: re-adding the `compare_all_properties = false` clear on the r4133 arm, injecting a surviving dropping under a `large*` guard, and adding/removing a teardown-error producer each red the gate or an offline drive.
- The props-walk counters are untouched by any unit test (source rail present and non-vacuous); population pins are fail-on-stale in both directions and skip cleanly on a filtered (`DSS_GATE_*`) run.
- No ledger entry, tolerance or golden byte changes; `oracle_server.py` untouched.
- `r4133-props-rp4.md` corrected in place; notes left for the five §4 docs; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D08-06 — Re-anchor mis-registered golden.lock rows (capi015 payloads, controlsoff decks, schema_divergences, dump3)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-06", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": [], "n_uids": 9} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_lock.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_reports.rs`, `tests/golden/golden.lock.json`, `tools/golden/README.md`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`
**Common rule:** only anchors / reasons / register rows move. The lock is regenerated ONCE at the end with `DSS_UPDATE_GOLDEN_LOCK=1 cargo test -p dss-core --test golden_lock -- --nocapture`; no artifact digest may move and no golden is regenerated (R8). The three groups below are reported by several audits — one code change closes each group, but every uid is recorded.
**Reading rule:** `tests/golden/golden.lock.json` is 4.5k lines / 172 KB — never open it or the regen output in context. Locate the ~14 rows with `rg -n '<path>' tests/golden/golden.lock.json` (the six capi015 payloads, the three controlsoff decks, `schema_divergences.json`, `dump3_commands.txt` + sidecars) and read only those rows; run the regen with stdout/stderr redirected to a scratchpad file and count its `RE-ANCHORED` / `DIGEST MOVED` lines with `rg -c`; review the lock change via `git diff --stat` plus `git diff tests/golden/golden.lock.json | rg '^[-+].*"(anchor|reason|produced_by)"'`, never the whole diff.
**Findings**
- `G|G0.1|AC1|AC1-1` (major) - `golden.lock.json`: six rows are anchored `capi_v0145` although the pinned 0.14.5 oracle cannot produce those bytes — `ncim/pq_Jacobian.csv`, `ncim/pq_PV2PQ.txt`, `ncim/pv_qlimit_Jacobian.csv`, `ncim/pv_qlimit_PV2PQ.txt`, `reports/export_capacity_seasonal.txt`, `reports/export_overloads_seasonal.txt`; each is written by the same generator call as a `.meta.json` sidecar the lock already anchors `capi015`. Fix: register the six payloads in `golden_lock.rs::CAPI015_ARTIFACTS` (first confirm each one's provenance from its sidecar / the `ncim_reports.rs` header) and add a rail: every artifact sharing a stem/generator with a `capi015` `.meta.json` must itself be `capi015`. Notes: re-derive the plan numbers that leaned on the wrong anchors — GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN G3.3a snapshot count (144 → re-count; the two seasonal reports must be refused by the anchor guard) and the G3.6 histogram (capi015 11 → 17 at G0.1).
- `G|G0.1|AC2|AC2-1` (major) - `golden_lock.rs::CAPI015_ARTIFACTS` holds only the sidecars, so the payloads fall through to the pinned-oracle residue and the closed-set tripwire pins the undercount at 11. Fix: same registration; move the closed-set count 11 → 17; expect exactly six `RE-ANCHORED` lines from the regen; notes to sync the TESTING.md anchor histogram (700/11 → 694/17, re-count) and the G0.1 record.
- `G|G0.1|AC3|AC3-1` (major) - same six rows carry `reason: null`, while `provenance_registers_are_well_formed` hard-asserts `CAPI015_ARTIFACTS.len() == 11` as "closed ... never grows"; the G0.1 settlement's converse sweep was JSON-only. Fix: re-anchor with the sidecars' reason; reword the "closed / never grows" assert message to the corrected closed set of 17; the sidecar↔payload sweep of AC1-1 must cover non-JSON payloads; sync any doc quoting `700/11/11/10/4/1`, including `tools/golden/README.md` (direct edit) and TESTING.md / the record (notes).
- `G|G0.1|AC2|AC2-5` (note) - the `capi_v0145` residue also labels four artifacts no engine produced: `feeders_controlsoff/{ieee13,ieee37,ieee123}_controlsoff.dss` (written by `tools/golden/gen_feeders_controlsoff.py`) and hand-authored `json/schema_divergences.json`. Fix: add a small register (e.g. `NON_ENGINE_RESIDUE`) whose truthful reason is written into the row, without minting a new anchor value — GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN §1.2 defines the closed anchor set and a real anchor stays G3.6's call. Note for TESTING.md.
- `G|G0.1|AC1|AC1-2` (minor) - the three `feeders_controlsoff/*.dss` rows claim oracle provenance for generated INPUT decks. Fix: covered by the AC2-5 register; reason text: "input deck written by tools/golden/gen_feeders_controlsoff.py from the vendored corpus master; no oracle bytes — the oracle output is feeders_controlsoff.json". The finding's alternative (a new anchor `derived_input` wired through `seed_metadata` + the anchor guard) is NOT taken: it would break the closed anchor set before G3.2a deletes the family; record that choice.
- `G|G0.1|AT1|AT1-3` (minor) - same three rows from the test-audit side. Fix: same register rows; keep `feeders_controlsoff.json` on `capi_v0145`; update register counts / histogram wherever asserted.
- `G|G0.1|AT1|AT1-2` (minor) - `json/schema_divergences.json` sits in the `capi_v0145` residue and its sibling reason (the born-self reason string in `golden_lock.rs` `DEANCHORED`, mirrored into the lock) still says it "stays capi_v0145 and frozen", although it has been hand-edited six times since. Fix: move it to the port-owned class — a `DEANCHORED` row (anchor `self`) if the `anchor == self <=> produced_by` invariant can be satisfied honestly, otherwise the AC2-5 register row; either way remove the "stays capi_v0145 and frozen" sentence from the reason string in `golden_lock.rs`. Record which option was taken and why.
- `G|G0.1|SA1|SA-1` (major) - `reports/dump3_commands.txt` (+ `.meta.json`) is locked `capi_v0145` with no reason, although its `[Relay]` / `[Recloser]` / `[Fuse]` / `[SwtControl]` blocks are r4133-shaped property surfaces pinned against the port's own render (the evidence that re-anchored `props/relay|recloser|fuse.json`). Fix: add a mixed-provenance register row / reason in `golden_lock.rs` stating those four blocks are r4133-shaped and self-pinned, the rest `capi_v0145`; cross-reference it from the doc of the dump3 test in `golden_reports.rs` (comment only); sync the G0.1 record by note.
- `G|G0.1|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `provenance_registers_are_well_formed` checks register disjointness for every pair except `DEANCHORED` × `CAPI015_ARTIFACTS`, so one edit can re-anchor an unreproducible capi015 artifact to `self`. Fix: add the missing pair — no `DEANCHORED` pattern may cover a `CAPI015_ARTIFACTS` path (`pattern_covers`), mirroring the `R4133_FAMILIES` arm; add a unit drive.
**Acceptance:**
- Regen prints exactly the expected `RE-ANCHORED` lines (six capi015 payloads + the rows whose anchor actually changes) and no `DIGEST MOVED`; `git diff tests/golden` touches `golden.lock.json` only, anchor/reason/count fields only.
- `CAPI015_ARTIFACTS` closed-set assert = 17; the sidecar↔payload rail and the `DEANCHORED` × `CAPI015_ARTIFACTS` disjointness arm each have a drive that reds.
- Every new register is covered by the existing fail-on-stale sweep (an entry matching no artifact reds).
- Notes left for GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md (G3.3a count, G3.6 histogram), TESTING.md and the golden-rebase record; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D08-07 — Close golden_lock.rs rail gaps and stale lock comments/reasons
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-07", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D08-06"], "n_uids": 11} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D08-06.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/golden_lock.rs`, `tests/golden/golden.lock.json`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`
**Common rule:** one lock regen at the end (`DSS_UPDATE_GOLDEN_LOCK=1 ... -- --nocapture`); only the `comment`, `reason` and `produced_by`-derivation bytes may move, no digest.
**Findings** (all in `golden_lock.rs`)
- `G|G0.1|AC2|AC2-4` (minor) - the regen arm announces `SEEDED` / `RE-ANCHORED` rows but is silent when a stored row has no on-disk artifact. Fix: print a `DROPPED` line (e.g. a `Seeded::Dropped` variant) per vanished row and the old→new artifact-count delta in the summary; note for the TESTING.md regen procedure.
- `G|G0.1|AC2|AC2-2` (minor) - `produced_by` is the only provenance field a hand-edit survives (regen carries `prev`, no register derives it), and flipping a `self` row to `lane-invariant` loosens G0.2's producing-lane write guard. Fix: add a `LANE_INVARIANT` register (empty today) next to `DEANCHORED`; the assert arm requires a `self` row's `produced_by` == the register value (Parity unless listed); regen derives it from the register and prints a `RE-ANCHORED`-style line on change; unit pin: a hand-flipped `lane-invariant` row reds. Fix the module-doc paragraphs describing `produced_by`; note for TESTING.md and the record.
- `G|G0.1|AT3|AT3-1` (minor) - the regen's `SEEDED` / `RE-ANCHORED` eprintln lines are discarded by libtest under the exact command the file prints. Fix: add `-- --nocapture` to every regen command the file prints or documents (module doc, the `DIGEST MOVED` remedy, the red-lock panic hint, the `COMMENT` const, which is baked into the lock); note for the TESTING.md env-var row; note to drop the record's deferral.
- `G|G0.1|AC2|AC2-6` (note) - three stale doc claims. Fix: reword the module-doc sentence about the `props_roundtrip` "non-emptiness only" hole to past tense (it pins 51/322/8343 since G0.2 — re-read the literals); in `R3723_REASON` say `tools/opendss/gen_ad_reference.py` was retired in `c060c0b2` (the string is written into lock rows → regen); TESTING.md "One golden-shaped tree" → two (add `tests/corpus/props_r4133`) by note.
- `G|G0.1|AC2|AC2-3` (minor) - the `.gitattributes` binding ignores every declaration not in the repo-root file or not literally prefixed with a locked root, so `-text` drift passes green. Fix: walk `ROOTS` for nested `.gitattributes` and fail if any exists (or parse them relative to their dir); fail on a root pathspec carrying `binary` / `-text` / `eol` that is not `ROOTS`-prefixed yet could match a locked path (bare filename, leading glob); a unit pin for each refusal; soften the "both directions, over every scanned path" doc to what is enforced.
- `G|G0.1|AT3|AT3-3` (note) - nothing binds an artifact's own declared provenance (`engine_spec` / `oracle.engine` / `engine`) to its locked anchor; an unregistered artifact silently takes the `capi_v0145` residue. Fix: add a converse corpus check — every locked JSON artifact that declares one of those keys maps to an anchor class that must contain the locked anchor, with one reasoned allow-entry for `flicker/pst_demo.json` (r4133 anchor, declares r3723 — byte-proven regen). Runs after RF-D08-06, so the capi015 payload rows are already correct.
- `RP|RP0.1|SA1|SA-3` (note) - `EXCLUDED_TREES`, the `tests/corpus/props_r4133` reason: "no row is engine output or an oracle capture the suite compares against" is no longer true (`examples_supplement.txt` is a `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS` rust/r4133 measurement; `props_r4133_replay.rs` replays over `examples_full.txt`). Fix: drop that clause; keep the grounds that hold (not regenerated by `tools/golden`, no anchor fits, bytes locked by `props_r4133_evidence_lock.rs`).
- `G|G0.1|AT2|AT2-3` (note) - the `COMMENT` const (mirrored into `golden.lock.json` line 2) still says the R1–R4 rules "land in TESTING.md ... until then the authority is the module documentation". Fix: point it at TESTING.md "Golden provenance lock" / "Regenerate a self-golden (R1-R4)"; regen; note to strike the record's "deferred to G3.6" sentence.
- `G|G0.1|AC1|AC1-3` (note) - the same stale `comment` seen in the lock file. Fix: closed by the `COMMENT` rewrite + regen above (comment-only byte move; the lock is outside its own artifact set); verify the TESTING.md section title quoted in the comment exists verbatim.
- `G|G0.1|AC1|AC1-4` (note) - `R4133_FAMILIES`, the flicker reason (row `flicker/pst_demo.json`) cites "STATUS 'EPRI bridge parity round'", a record STATUS.md no longer contains. Fix: cite `docs/phase-records/epri-bridge.md` (the byte-parity evidence paragraph; confirm the file and paragraph exist); regen.
- `G|G0.1|AT1|AT1-4` (note) - the ten r3723-witness reasons cite `tools/opendss/gen_ad_reference.py` as if it were available. Triage sketch wrong: it says the script "was never tracked"; `git log --all -- tools/opendss/gen_ad_reference.py` shows it added in `8df9d892` and deleted in `c060c0b2`. Fix: one `R3723_REASON` edit shared with AC2-6 — "retired in c060c0b2 (recoverable from history); the tree is frozen"; leave the digest-locked `PROVENANCE.txt` bytes alone.
**Acceptance:**
- New unit pins exist and pass: `DROPPED` announcement, hand-flipped `produced_by` reds, nested / unprefixed `.gitattributes` refusals, the declared-provenance converse check (with exactly one allow-entry).
- After the regen `git diff tests/golden` shows only `golden.lock.json` with `comment` / `reason` (and register-derived `produced_by`, expected: none) changes; zero digest moves.
- Every printed regen command contains `-- --nocapture`.
- Notes left for TESTING.md and the golden-rebase record; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D08-08 — Strengthen props_r4133_pins.rs discriminating readings and helpers
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D08-08", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-08"], "n_uids": 12} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-08.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_pins.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/props_r4133_replay.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp2.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp3.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Order hint:** harden `Deck::cmd` first (RP3.4 AC-1) — it may expose pins whose `edit` was silently a no-op; repair those before adding readings. Every expected value added below is the PORT's value, measured at HEAD in both lanes; r4133's number is named in the assertion message only. RF-D07-08 (`RP|RP3.9|AT2|AT2-1`, landed before this step) locks the RP3.9 pins' r4133 literals in a count-locked table `the_rp39_pins_quote_the_vendored_census_cells` in `props_r4133_replay.rs` (each literal verbatim in the pin bodies AND in `tests/corpus/props_r4133/examples_full.txt`): every r4133 literal the RP3.9 bullets below add to a pin body (`140.347851871698`, `37.5020834490805`, `50.0027779321073`, `10.2349`) is appended to that table and its count lock in the same commit — `props_r4133_replay.rs` is in `files` for exactly that edit, nothing else there moves.
**Findings** (all in `props_r4133_pins.rs`)
- `RP|RP3.4|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - `Deck::cmd` swallows every non-abort engine error, so a pin's discriminating `edit` can be a silent no-op. Fix: record `errors().len()` before the command and panic on ANY new error; add an explicit `cmd_expecting_error` escape for pins that deliberately provoke a diagnostic; run the binary in both lanes and repair any pin that relied on a swallowed error; self-test: `edit <elem> bogusprop=1` through `cmd` must panic.
- `RP|RP2.3|AC2|AC2-2` (minor) - `DeckDirGuard` claims the CorpusGuard contract "in miniature" but discards removal failures (`let _ = remove_file`, no re-listing), recurses into pre-existing subdirectories, nested guards share no snapshot, created directories are never removed. Fix: bring it to the `corpus_gate/runner.rs::CorpusGuard` contract — no recursion into pre-existing subdirs (or one snapshot shared by nested guards), re-list after removal and panic on a survivor, remove created directories; extend the guard self-test with a nested-dir case and a re-listing case. (The sibling copies in `pd_elements_pins.rs` / `reliability_pins.rs` are out of scope here — they are owned by RF-D01-12, which hoists ONE `DeckDirGuard` for all three files starting from this step's hardened copy; name RF-D01-12 as the owner in the record and in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D08-08.md`, do not record an unowned follow-up.)
- `RP|RP2.3|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - `windgen_dynout_renders_empty_when_unset` — sole witness of `windgen.dynout` — asserts two empty strings; a getter hardwired to `""` passes; `Relay.Action`, `Recloser.DebugTrace`, `RegControl.IdleForward` / `IdleReverse` share the gap. Fix: define a DynamicExp in the pin, `edit WindGen.w1 dynamiceq=... dynout=...`, assert DynOut renders the named list; for the other three either add a discriminating reading or name an explicit no-discriminator exception in the pin doc (the form the Recloser/RegControl exceptions elsewhere in the file already use). Note: sync the rp2 record's "all 20 pins discriminate" claim.
- `RP|RP2.3|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `relay_action_distreverse_and_reset_render_the_live_values`: the `relay.action` assert pins a value the port renders by construction (`PropType::Action` renders `""`, `class_props/value.rs`), yet the doc calls it a live reading. Fix: correct the pin doc — it pins the property type, not a pending action — as an explicit no-discriminator exception; fold into the AC2-1 repair.
- `RP|RP3.1|AC1|AC-3` (note) - the SwtControl `delay` pin doc claims its readings discriminate against a getter that echoes the deck token; all three readings are satisfied by a parse-time echo store. Fix: add `edit SwtControl.sw delay=0.1234567891` and assert the engine's re-formatted render (not the typed token); keep the doc claim only once that reading exists.
- `RP|RP3.1|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `swtcontrol_delay_wires_the_property_on_the_midi_tie`, sole witness of ledger entry `r4133-swtcontrol-delay-ignored-midi`, names only the port's value. Fix: assertion messages and doc name both numbers (port value and r4133's `120`, citing the ledger id); add an unset-default reading if a suitable deck element exists.
- `RP|RP3.9|AT1|AT2` (minor) - `load_kva_after_makeposseq_is_the_exact_typed_conversion` leaves residue cell `makeposseq_shunt` / `Load.ld1.kVA` unasserted while its doc claims full coverage. Fix: add the row `makeposseq_shunt / Load.ld1.kVA == 140.350877192982` (r4133 `140.347851871698` in the message; confirm the port value by running) and correct the doc counts ("remaining ten" → the true row count; 15 census cells in total).
- `RP|RP3.9|AT1|AT3` (minor) - `capacitor_cuf_and_amps_after_makeposseq_are_the_exact_typed_conversion` counts nine census cells over three decks but reads two decks. Fix: compile `makeposseq_report` and assert `Capacitor.cap1` `cuf` / `normamps` / `emergamps` at the port's typed values (normamps `37.5023029706172`; r4133's `37.5020834490805` / `50.0027779321073` / `10.2349` named in the messages) so all 9 cells are read.
- `RP|RP3.9|AC1|AC3` (note) - the rp3 record claims the chain-D/E pins mutate and restore deck state with every restore read back; neither chain-E pin restores. Fix: add the restore + read-back leg to `generator_ratings_after_makeposseq_...` (restore the exact nameplate kW after the `kw=66.667` edit, re-read `? kW`) and `transformer_amps_...` (restore `kvs`, re-assert the cells); if a restore is not meaningful for chain E, leave a note correcting the two record sentences to "only chain D restores".
- `RP|RP3.12|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - the RP3.12 AutoTrans pin leaves the re-derived band-excursion figure as unasserted prose; the settlement's "would need harness machinery" reason is false. Fix: read `Dss::bus_voltages("low")` (`exec/view.rs`), assert `|V(low.1)|/166` equals the measured 118.038 V within a measurement-derived band and sits below the `[119,121]` edge by the documented 0.96 V; note to correct the record sentence.
- `RP|RP3.12|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - same pin: the ampere-turn identity ("discriminating second reading") runs after a byte-exact `assert_eq!` on the same `WdgCurrents` bytes, so it can never be the failing assertion. Fix: run the identity BEFORE the byte-exact assert so a winding-current drift reds with the identity's message first; otherwise restate the doc honestly (it guards only the Tap→active-winding binding and a joint literal re-baseline) and drop the claim that it rules `GetAllWindingCurrents` / `yterminal.rs` out. Note to sync the record sentence.
- `RP|RP3.12|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - same pin: the ampere-turn band `1.0e-5` is a guessed, one-sided round number; its four measured residuals live only in the panic message. Fix: replace it with a named const bracketed on both sides from the four measured residuals (2.2e-6..3.9e-6; cause = the 5–7 significant-digit `WdgCurrents` render), in the `RP312_MIN_GAP_RATIO` style; tightening only (R8); note the derivation for TOLERANCE_NOTES.md.
**Parts:**
1. AC-1 (`Deck::cmd` hardening — 82 call sites at HEAD, `rg -n '\.cmd\('` — plus `cmd_expecting_error` and the self-test; run `cargo test -p dss-core --test props_r4133_pins` in both lanes and repair every pin that reds, each repair named) and AC2-2 (`DeckDirGuard` to the CorpusGuard contract + the extended self-test). Gate, commit; `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D08-08.md` lists the repaired pins, the guard's final API (for RF-D01-12) and any pin whose `edit` turned out to be a silent no-op.
2. The ten reading/doc uids (AC2-1, AT2-1, AC-3, AT-2, AT2, AT3, AC3, AC-2, AT-1, AT-2 of RP3.12) and the `props_r4133_replay.rs` table append with its count lock (that file is 10.9k lines — `rg -n the_rp39_pins_quote_the_vendored_census_cells` and edit the table rows only). Gate, commit; record.
**Acceptance:**
- `Deck::cmd` panics on any new engine error (self-test present); `cargo test -p dss-core --test props_r4133_pins` is green in both lanes with every pin repaired, none deleted or `#[ignore]`d.
- New readings exist: WindGen DynOut named list, SwtControl re-formatted `delay`, `makeposseq_shunt` Load kVA row, `makeposseq_report` Capacitor.cap1 cells, the 118.038 V band-excursion assert, the two-sided ampere-turn const.

Reword the pin doc so it cites that DIVERGENCES row and does not claim the port's spelling is "right"; make sure the assertion messages name r4133's `-1`. Note for the rp2 record: one sentence pointing at the row.
**Ruling:** R6 — keep the port default `0.0` for `%pminnovars` / `%pminkvarmax`; behaviour is proven identical, so this is a DIVERGENCES.md entry plus a pin, not an engine change.
