### RF-D09-01 — Give the props_norm liveness rails teeth (dormant rows, NORM_HITS, SeamTouches, Disposition drift, fault.bus2, N=15 slack)
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-01", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-07"], "n_uids": 6} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-07.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/props_census.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/props_norm.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp2.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Findings**
- `RP|RP2.1|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `props_norm.rs`, `assert_norm_rows_are_live` / `check_rows_are_live`: the live anti-rot guard can never fire on 8 of the 168 `PROPS_NORM_R4133` rows (the gendispatcher/sensor rows have no in-scope cell), yet doc and record claim all 168 are guarded, and nothing lists the 8. Fix: CLOSED BY RF-D07-07 `RP|RP2.1|AT1|AT1-1` (landed before this step), which adds exactly this constant (`NORM_ROWS_WITH_NO_IN_SCOPE_CELL`, the analogue of `ECHO_ROWS_WITH_NO_IN_SCOPE_CELL`) with the both-direction derivation test over `tests/corpus/props_r4133/bins.tsv` + `shape_in_scope.txt` and the `corpus_gate.rs` comment. Here: verify at lane HEAD that the constant, its two-direction test and the wording exist; add ONLY what RF-D07-07 left out — the in-file "all 168 rows" wording ("160 guarded + 8 dormant-by-scope") if it still reads "all 168", and the §4 notes for the same sentence in the RP2.1 record and `TESTING.md`. Never a second list or test; record the uid as a duplicate closed by RF-D07-07 plus whatever residue this step added.
- `RP|RP2.1|AC3|AC3-1` (minor) - `props_census.rs`, `Disposition::all()`: the four normalization rule tags are hardcoded string literals with no anti-drift pin against `NormRule::tag()`, so a fifth rule's cells would vanish from `claims_summary.json` (`per_disposition`, `value_cells`, `value_cells_in_scope`) and the banner. Fix: expose `NormRule::ALL_TAGS` (or an exhaustive-match helper) in `props_norm.rs` and build `Disposition::all()` from it; add pin `disposition_all_covers_every_norm_rule_tag`; assert in `claims_artifacts_carry_the_per_cell_accounting` that the sum of `per_disposition` cells equals the ingested value cells.
- `RP|RP2.1|AC2|AC2-1` (minor) - `props_norm.rs`, `lookup_claim` (NORM hit accounting): a "divergent cell" is raw string inequality, so a row stays alive on cells the property comparator would have passed unnormalized. Fix: count a hit only when the two sides differ in FORM (`numeric_skeleton` differs, or non-numeric text differs), not on raw `!=` with in-band numeric noise; pin that an `ArrayForm` row fed same-skeleton in-band numbers records a visit and no hit. The triage pairs this with "RP2.4 AC-3" (same remedy) - that uid is owned by RF-D06-09 (wave 16, after this step). `assert_norm_rows_are_live` runs inside the live gate, so a row that goes stale under the FORM rule cannot be "stopped" one at a time: if ANY `PROPS_NORM_R4133` row goes stale, revert AC2-1 whole (predicate + pin), list the rows in the record and report the uid to the coordinator (R8); never delete or exempt rows here. Either way write the outcome to `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D09-01.md` — the landed predicate's name, what counts as a FORM difference, the pin name (or: reverted, the stale rows) — because RF-D06-09 builds its ArrayForm half on that predicate and must not re-derive it.
- `RP|RP2.3|AC1|AC1-2` (minor) - `props_norm.rs`, `ECHO_ROWS_WITH_NO_IN_SCOPE_CELL` / `check_echo_rows_are_live`: the `fault.bus2` exemption was justified by the `visits > 0 && hits == 0` arm, which became unreachable when RP4.1 narrowed the row; the early return on `dormant_by_design` now disarms the only applicable arm (`seam_ran && visits == 0`), so the row has no live guard while the pin calls the list correct. Fix: rewrite the justification for the narrowed era (its one covered spelling `b2.0`/`b2.0.0.0` is out of scope, so the narrowed arm would fire - the exemption is needed for THAT reason) and say plainly the row is guarded only by the offline replay; update the message in `the_dormant_row_exemption_list_is_pinned` and add an assertion that every covered cell of an exempt narrowed row is out of scope per the frozen evidence.
- `RP|RP3.3|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - `props_norm.rs`, `normalize_r4133` / `seam_touches_here`: the `norm` arm of the per-thread `SeamTouches` counter has no positive test; deleting `record_touch(|t| &mut t.norm, claimed)` reds nothing, so the three "my query moved no counter" tests compare a permanently `(0,0)` field. Fix: add `the_normalization_seam_counts_visits_and_hits` - call `normalize_r4133` on one claimed and one unclaimed cell of a known row and assert exact `seam_touches_here().norm` deltas, mirroring the echo and floor siblings.
- `RP|RP2.4|SA1|SA-4` (note) - `props_norm.rs`, the second slack of the mechanism clause beside `display_is_render`: the half-unit-of-the-15-digit-grid slack is derived only for N <= 14; at N = 15 it equals the half-step and doubles the admitted window, and 15-digit oracle spellings occur. Fix: bound the slack so it never exceeds a fraction of the printed step (e.g. `min(0.5*10^(e-14), 0.05*step)`) or special-case N = 15; add a boundary pin at N = 15 (a full-grid-unit gap on `50.0027779321073` is refused); confirm every replay count lock is unchanged. Tightening only, never widen (R8); leave a §4 note for the `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` derivation.
**Acceptance:**
- New tests exist and pass in both lanes: `disposition_all_covers_every_norm_rule_tag`, the same-skeleton visit-no-hit pin, `the_normalization_seam_counts_visits_and_hits`, the N = 15 boundary pin; `the_dormant_row_exemption_list_is_pinned` carries the narrowed-row assertion.
- The live corpus gate stays green: `assert_norm_rows_are_live` and the echo guard fire on no row; no replay count lock, ledger entry or golden byte moves.
- Notes left for `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp2.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`; `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D09-01.md` states the landed (or reverted) NORM-hit predicate for RF-D06-09.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-04 — Run RelCalc in the props census walk and repair the props-surface stability test
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-04", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D06-01", "RF-D09-01"], "n_uids": 4} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-01, RF-D09-01.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/props_census.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md`, `tests/corpus/props_r4133/README.md`
**Findings** (three audits report one defect; one code change closes the first three)
- `RP|RP0.2|SA1|SA-1` (major) - `props_census.rs`, doc of `the_props_surface_is_stable_across_the_gates_intervening_comparators` ("fixed by demonstration": every command between solve and the property block is a `?` query): false at HEAD - `runner.rs::compare_capture` now drives the executive `RelCalc` there and `scheduler.rs::census_one` never does, so on the six `compare_reliability` cases the census compares a port that never ran RelCalc against a post-RelCalc oracle capture. Fix: drive `RelCalc` in `census_one` after the last solve when `cc.compare_reliability` (shared helper, see AC-1) before `collect_prop_divergences`; rewrite the "fixed by demonstration" doc to the truth; §4 notes correcting the closure of finding (8) in the RP0.2 record and `TESTING.md`.
- `RP|RP0.2|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `scheduler.rs::census_one` vs `compare_capture` (the "GOLDEN_REBASE G1.6(i)" block: `RelCalc` once, after the LAST solve, `RelCalcOutcome` handling): the census step loop is a private copy issuing only `solve`, so it manufactures false EnergyMeter cells (up to 70 over 7 meters) that disposition UNCLAIMED in `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS=claims`; the test doc's "No export, no set, no second solve" is false. Fix: factor the gate's per-step drive into ONE helper in `runner.rs` called by both `compare_capture` and `census_one`, so the two cannot drift again. `RelCalc` is not idempotent - exactly once per walk. RF-D06-01 (landed before this step) edited that same `compare_capture` block (RelCalc drive liveness, the multi-line RelCalc error check, the denominator gate): the helper carries RF-D06-01's checks unchanged — factor what is at lane HEAD, do not re-derive the block from the triage's reading.
- `RP|RP0.2|AT1|AT-1` (major) - same defect from the artifact side: five false pairs per reliability deck (`energymeter.saifi/saifikw/saidi/caidi/custinterrupts`) on both channels enter `claims_unclaimed_pairs.txt`, which RP4.1's "zero UNCLAIMED" acceptance reads, and the README re-census rule tells a reader to file them as port findings. Fix: the shared helper, plus a pin that a reliability deck (`controls:energymeter/midi_relcalc.dss`) yields zero such census rows (oracle-free form: a unit pin that the census walk issues `RelCalc` exactly when the flag is set); §4 note for the README re-census rule.
- `RP|RP0.2|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `props_census.rs`, the same test: it replays a hardcoded 2026-08-22 traffic list, so it can never red when the gate's step loop gains a state-changing command - it should have caught AT-1. Fix: make it structural - either assert that the census goes through the shared step helper, or add a source-scan pin over `runner.rs` that enumerates every `dss.command(..)` between solve and the property compare and reds on any non-`?` command not on an explicit allowlist (`RelCalc`, listed with its census mirror).
**Probes:** run the claims census (`DSS_PROPS_CENSUS=claims`, procedure in `TESTING.md`) on the six `compare_reliability` cases on both channels (dss-python `capi_v0145` and epri-worker `r4133`) before and after the fix. Before: confirms and names the false rows (expected 5 per EnergyMeter per channel). After: decides closure - zero `energymeter.saifi/saifikw/saidi/caidi/custinterrupts` rows; a row that survives is a real port divergence and is reported to the coordinator, never excluded here (R8).
**Acceptance:**
- One step helper in `runner.rs` is the only place that issues the per-step `solve`/`RelCalc` traffic; `compare_capture` and `census_one` both call it; the corpus gate verdict is unchanged (no ledger entry, tolerance or golden byte moves).
- The reliability-deck census pin and the structural traffic guard exist and pass in both lanes; the guard reds on a locally injected non-query command (not committed).
- Post-fix probe: zero false EnergyMeter reliability rows on both channels, measured figures in the step record.
- Notes left for `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md`, `tests/corpus/props_r4133/README.md`; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-05 — Scheduler rails: wired-flag refusal over force rules, census panic rows, census in_scope predicate, stale scheduler docs
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-05", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D06-08", "RF-D08-05", "RF-D09-04", "RF-D10-01"], "n_uids": 10} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D06-08, RF-D08-05, RF-D09-04, RF-D10-01.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/manifest.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/props_census.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`, `tests/corpus/props_r4133/README.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G1.0|AT3|AT3-1` (minor) - `manifest.rs::assert_no_unwired_g1_flag` / `scheduler.rs::make_case` force rules: the unwired-flag refusal inspects only the RAW manifests, while the scheduler forces nine of ten compare-depth flags ON for whole populations regardless of `G1Flag::wired`. Fix: add scheduler test `no_force_rule_turns_on_an_unwired_g1_flag` that runs `make_case` over every manifest case (or one synthetic case per kind) - `make_case` as RF-D06-08 (`RP|RP2.4|SA1|SA-2`, in **After**) left it, i.e. with the `large*` guard on the family arm of `force_properties` and its no-`large*`-carries-`compare_all_properties` pin - and calls `manifest::assert_no_unwired_g1_flag` on the POST-force case; §4 notes so the plan/`TESTING.md` sentence "can never go live vacuous" names both rails. RF-D10-01 (`G|G1.0|AT3|AT3-2`, landed before this step) split that fn into a thin wrapper over the live `G1Flag` table plus a core over `&[G1Flag]` with a forged-row helper: the scheduler test calls the WRAPPER (live rows) for the post-force check and reuses RF-D10-01's core + forged-row helper for its own non-vacuity drive (a forged `wired: false` row must make the post-force check red) — no second forged-row helper in `manifest.rs`.
- `G|G1.3d(i)|AC2|AC2-3` (note) - `scheduler.rs`, doc of `FORCED_ELEMENT_EXTRAS_POPULATION` + plan as-executed note: both attribute the value to the 2026-09-05 re-derivation `(441, 310, 87, 44)`; the const is `(443, 312, 87, 44)`. Fix: make the const doc stop attributing the current value to that date (the history is already in the `FORCED_PROPS_POPULATION` doc - as RF-D08-05 `RP|RP4.1|AT1|AT1-1` (in **After**) left it: that step rewrites the guard doc near `FORCED_PROPS_POPULATION` and turns the re-mask alarm into a population pin; build on its text, do not restore the pre-D08-05 wording); §4 note for `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`: annotate (441,310,87,44) as the value at the 2026-09-05 merge and point at the const as the live source.
- `G|G1.3d(i)|AT2|AT2-1` (note) - `scheduler.rs`, the "No opt-in list" rationale of `force_element_extras`: claims fastdss `KNOWN_COM_DIFF` has no row for this flag's surface; since G1.3d(ii) the flag also gates `PhaseLosses`, and `KNOWN_COM_DIFF` (`compare_outputs.py:62`) carries a `PhaseLosses` row for ckt24/master_ckt24. Fix: state the real reason (ckt24 is `kind=large_ultra_switch`, outside the forced population - cost guard) and replace "five fields" with the actual surface; §4 note for the `TESTING.md` twin.
- `RP|RP2.1|AT2|AT2-2` (minor) - `scheduler.rs::census_one`, `let in_scope = uc.case.engines != "capi_v0145"`: re-implements the README filter under a comment asserting an equivalence the README denies; RP3.4 fixed the same derivation in the replay only, the drift is now five skipped cases, and the predicate is untested. Fix: extract one named fn using the replay's predicate (`engines != "capi_v0145" && !ctx.ledger.channel_is_skipped(label, r4133)`), unit-test it over the five r4133 `kind=skip` cases (incl. `controls:espvlcontrol/espvlcontrol.dss`), fix the comment; §4 note: README "four" skipped cases -> five.
- `G|G1.10a|AC3|AC3-4` (note) - `scheduler.rs`, run-files doc citing `dss_epri::guard::CorpusGuard::classify`: that method is private; the three producers share the free fn. Fix: cite `dss_epri::guard::classify_created`.
- `G|G1.3d(i)|AT2|AT2-2` (note) - `scheduler.rs`, `FORCED_ELEMENT_EXTRAS_POPULATION` doc: "would stop comparing the five discrete channels". Fix: the ten discrete extras + `PhaseLosses`, matching the test's own failure message.
- `G|G1.8|AC2|AC2-2` (note) - `scheduler.rs`, the `Solution.BusLevels` never-requested doc: cites `crates/dss-epri/src/modes.rs:271-280`, which now points at `classify_s`/`classify_v`. Fix: symbol citation `dss_epri::modes::DO_NOT_CALL` (its Solution/BusLevels entry). Extending the citation walker to Rust doc comments is optional and NOT in scope unless it fits in the same small diff.
- `RP|RP0.2|AC1|AC-5` (note) - `scheduler.rs::census_one`: `rows` and `blind` live inside the `catch_unwind` closure, so a Rust-side panic mid-walk drops every row already measured and the blind-spot counters, contradicting "every failure mode becomes a ROW". Fix: hoist them out of the closure (Mutex/RefCell, or a per-step `catch_unwind`) so the Err arm appends the RustError row to the collected rows and keeps the counters; pin with a unit test over an injected mid-walk panic if the refactor makes that reachable, else correct the doc to say partial rows are dropped and record why.
- `G|G1.10a|AC3|AC3-6` (note) - `scheduler.rs`, run-files doc "of the 44 capi-only live cases exactly three reach a file-writing command": wrong on both halves. Fix: exactly TWO (`HarmonicsTMode/IEEE_519.DSS`, `HarmonicsVariableLoad/IEEE_519.DSS`); `ckt24/Run_Ckt24.dss` is `kind=large_ultra_switch` (outside `force_run_files`) and its `plot` lines open no file on capi. Re-run the writer scan first; §4 note for `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` if it repeats "three".
- `G|G1.4a|AC2|AC2-4` (note) - `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` G1.4a "Measured headroom" block vs `scheduler.rs` `force_bus` doc ("~99.7 k bus-steps"): the table's coverage does not match the gated population (`!kind.starts_with("large")`) and "24 356 bus-step comparisons" contradicts the scheduler figure. Fix: re-measure the bus-step count, correct the `scheduler.rs` figure if it is the wrong one, and leave a §4 note for the notes block: mark each `large*` row measured-but-not-gated, give the gated worst ratio 0.266 (IEEE13Nodeckt) beside the measured worst 0.658, reconcile the count. No floor changes.
**Acceptance:**
- `no_force_rule_turns_on_an_unwired_g1_flag` and the census in-scope predicate unit test exist and pass in both lanes; the predicate fn is the single definition used by `census_one`.
- `census_one` keeps partial rows on a panic (pinned) or its doc states the truth, with the reason recorded.
- No population lock, ledger entry, tolerance or golden byte moves; doc figures are re-measured, not copied from the finding.
- Notes left for the five §4 documents; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-06 — Add oracle-side flake diagnostics (oracle_server.py, dss-epri capture) and fix runner.rs rails, messages and cites
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-06", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D02-05", "RF-D09-03", "RF-D09-05", "RF-D10-03"], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D02-05, RF-D09-03, RF-D09-05, RF-D10-03.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/exec/view.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/capture_order.rs` (pin moves only, see AC2-3), `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/runner.rs`, `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/scheduler.rs`, `crates/dss-epri/src/capture.rs`, `tools/oracle/oracle_server.py`
**Doc notes (§4):** `GOLDEN_REBASE_PLAN.md`, `STATUS.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`, `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md`
**Findings**
- `G|G1.10a|SA1|SA-0` (major) - `runner.rs::run_rust_capture` short-read rail vs the two oracle transports: the only fix for the espvlcontrol flake (D35(3)) is a port-side assertion, while the one channel-attributed record of the flake names the capi transport; neither transport diagnoses it, and D35(3)'s STOP-on-recurrence clause was retired without a ruling. Fix: add the transport-side twin - after compile, `oracle_server.py` (no circuit and no error -> fail reply carrying the deck's `st_size`) and `dss-epri` `capture.rs` (same, via the DLL circuit count) return a named "compiled to no circuit, deck is N bytes" error that the runner surfaces per producer; §4 notes for the G1.10a record / `STATUS.md` / plan on the D35(3) STOP-on-recurrence clause state the fact only — "D35(3) STOP-on-recurrence retired in <commit> without a ruling; ruling requested from the RETRO_FIXES coordinator" — and cite a ruling number only if `tmp/retro_fix/rulings.md` carries a ruling on THAT clause at launch (none of R1–R10 does; R9/R10 are unrelated document rules); never restate the clause as in force and never invent a verdict. `dss-epri` stays `#[cfg(windows)]` with `// SAFETY` docs; no new `unsafe` beyond the existing FFI pattern.
- `G|G1.10a|AC4|AC4-3` (note) - `runner.rs`, six multi-line failure-message literals (the leak, parent-resurrect and short-read rails): lost their `\` line continuations, so messages render with 6-22-space gaps. Re-count the multi-line literals at lane HEAD after RF-D02-05 / RF-D08-05 (both in **After**, both edit `runner.rs`; RF-D02-05 adds `parent_guard_does_not_overwrite_a_running_siblings_file` with its own failure message) - "six" is the ab9f0f76 count, not a target. Fix: restore the continuations; optionally add a source-scan hygiene pin (no string literal under `tests/corpus_gate` holds 6+ consecutive spaces), hosted in `runner.rs`'s test module and added ONLY if at lane HEAD it flags nothing outside this step's `files` (`ledger.rs`, `manifest.rs`, `props_census.rs`, `engines.rs` are out of scope) - it will flag the same defect in `scheduler.rs` population-lock messages, which this step owns too: fix them in the same pass; if it flags an out-of-scope file, drop the pin and record the hits for the coordinator.
- `G|G1.10a|AT4|AT4-1` (minor) - `runner.rs`, `CorpusGuard::new` wait loop (`DIR_CLAIM_DEADLINE`): the assert is `!(timed_out.timed_out() && waiting.elapsed() > DIR_CLAIM_DEADLINE)`, but one global Condvar + `notify_all()` on every Drop means foreign releases restart the window, so a leaked claim never fails while the gate is busy. Fix: drop the `timed_out.timed_out()` conjunct (assert on `waiting.elapsed()` after every wake); factor the loop AS IT STANDS at lane HEAD after RF-D02-05 / RF-D08-05 (in **After**: `finish()` / `assert_swept_clean` and the narrowed snapshot/restore live on the same struct - keep them intact) so the deadline is injectable and add a unit test - one leaked key plus a second thread claiming/releasing another key must trip the rail within the scaled deadline.
- `G|G1.10a|AT4|AT4-4` (note) - `runner.rs::run_rust_capture`: asserts only `dss.circuit().is_some()` while the message claims "no active circuit AND reported no error". Fix: emit the "read back with no command" message only when the circuit is None AND no error beyond the declared `expect_warnings` was reported; with no circuit but errors, fail with a different message listing them.
- `G|G1.3c|SA1|SA-5` (note) - `runner.rs`, comment "only its six named value channels overwritten" with a `:1702` anchor: `rewrite_element_selected` has thirteen `want()` arms. Fix: say thirteen (count the arms at HEAD) and cite `ledger.rs::rewrite_element_selected` by symbol (the triage's "do together with SA-1" sibling is not in this step).
- `G|G1.3d(ii)|AT2|AT2-4` (note) - `runner.rs` comment "the ten measured widenings" + `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` "Ten committed `element` ledger scopes": eight at HEAD. Fix: re-count in `tests/corpus/ledger.json`, write eight-at-HEAD with the lane's ten parenthetical (mirror the corrected `TESTING.md` paragraph) in `runner.rs`; §4 note for the notes twin.
- `G|G1.8|AC2|AC2-3` (note) - `scheduler.rs` inc-matrix doc + `capture.rs`: three off-by-N Pascal cites. Fix: `DSolution.pas:545-546` -> `:544-545` (the `[0]` sentinel; `:546` is the `IncMat <> Nil` test) in `scheduler.rs`; the `capture.rs` half (`CAPI_Solution.pas:959` -> `:961`, the `DefaultResult` call) is CLOSED BY RF-D10-03 `G|G1.8|AT2|AT2-3` (landed before this step) - verify it at HEAD and record the duplicate, do not re-edit. Re-check against `.inputs` first. General rule for this step's `capture.rs` / `oracle_server.py` edits (SA-0, AC3): RF-D10-03 rewrote docstrings there and `crates/dss-core/tests/capture_order.rs` pins both files by source text - `rg` the old text under `crates/dss-core/tests` before touching a line, and move a pinned literal in the same commit - `crates/dss-core/tests/capture_order.rs` is in **Files** for exactly that: edit only a pin whose literal this step's own docstring/comment edit moves, never its rules or marker lists (those belong to RF-D01-13/-14/-17); a pinned MARKER line (`# capture-order: ...`) is never edited - re-word around it.
- `G|G1.6b|AC1|AC3` (note) - four off-by-one cites around the "leaves ActiveCktElement as is" contract. Fix: `capture.rs` `DPDELements.pas:92` -> `:93`; `oracle_server.py` `CAPI/CAPI_PDElements.pas:251` -> `:252`; `exec/view.rs` capi ranges for Name / IsShunt / pctPermanent start at `:184` / `:146` / `:156` (re-read each function's end line before writing the range).
**Parts:**
1. The transport-side twin and the cross-file citations: SA-0 (`oracle_server.py` + `dss-epri` `capture.rs` no-circuit error, the runner surfacing it per producer, the `capture_order.rs` pin moves, both transport probes), AC2-3 (verify the `capture.rs` half at HEAD, fix the `scheduler.rs` cite) and AC3 (`capture.rs` / `oracle_server.py` / `exec/view.rs` cites). Gate, commit; `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D09-06.md` names the runner error path, every `capture_order.rs` literal moved and the probe transcripts' location (scratchpad).
2. The `runner.rs` / `scheduler.rs` rails: AT4-1 (injectable `DIR_CLAIM_DEADLINE` + two-thread unit test), AT4-4 (the two distinct `run_rust_capture` messages), AC4-3 (line continuations + the hygiene-pin decision, incl. the `scheduler.rs` population-lock messages), SA-5, AT2-4. Gate, commit; record. Reading rule for both parts: every file here is 2–5k lines — `rg -n` the symbol, read a bounded range, never a file whole.
**Probes:** both transports must be exercised live: run the corpus gate subset for `controls:espvlcontrol/espvlcontrol.dss` on dss-python and on epri-worker to show the new check is silent on a healthy compile, and feed each transport a deliberately truncated/empty copy of a deck (scratchpad, never the vendored corpus) to show the named "compiled to no circuit, deck is N bytes" error reaches the runner per producer. If the DLL circuit count cannot distinguish "no circuit" after a failed compile, record what r4133 reports instead and use that.
**Acceptance:**
- All three producers name a no-circuit compile with the deck size; the two transport paths are demonstrated by the probe and covered by a test where one is reachable offline (Python-side/unit level), both lanes green.
- The `DIR_CLAIM_DEADLINE` rail has a unit test that reds on the old `timed_out` conjunct; `run_rust_capture` has the two distinct messages.
- No ledger entry, tolerance or golden byte moves; `dss-epri` keeps `#![deny(unsafe_op_in_unsafe_fn)]` clean under both clippy runs.
- Notes left for the four §4 documents (incl. the D35(3) clause); every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-07 — lane.rs: complete the ElemChannels guard, inject cores for monitor-pad and reround liveness, pin the relay trace filter, fix docs
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-07", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D00-03"], "n_uids": 9} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-03.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/lane.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp2.md`
**Findings** (two pairs are duplicates: one change closes each pair, both uids are recorded)
- `G|G1.3a|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `lane.rs`, test `newton_powers_are_the_only_element_channel_exclusion`: the anti-tautology guard asserts 10 of the 13 `ElemChannels` fields; `cplx_seq_currents`, `cplx_seq_voltages`, `total_powers` appear in no assertion, so flipping one in `ElemChannels::ALL` drops that channel everywhere with a green suite. Fix: on the newton labels assert `ch.cplx_seq_currents && ch.cplx_seq_voltages && !ch.total_powers`, add the three to the `ALL` conjunction; preferably destructure `let ElemChannels { .. }` exhaustively (no `..` rest) so a 14th field fails to compile until guarded. Ordering: RF-D00-01 P3 and RF-D00-03 (`G|G2.3|AT1|AT-4`; both in **After**) edit the `LANE_SKIP_ELEM_POWERS` doc/exclusions and the `ElemChannels` region of `lane.rs` first - destructure `ElemChannels` as it stands at lane HEAD (a field they added is guarded too) and keep their wording of the exclusion doc.
- `G|G1.3c|AT3|AT3-2` (minor) - same test, same gap, seen for `CURRENTS_ONLY`: add `cplx_seq_currents`/`cplx_seq_voltages` to its positive list and `!total_powers` to its excluded list. Fix: closed by the SA-1 change (one exhaustive destructure serves both `ALL` and `CURRENTS_ONLY`); keep the `ALL` assert message truthful.
- `G|G2.2a|AC1|AC-5` (note) - `lane.rs`, `profile_ll_policy` doc: says the exclusion uses "the mechanism the `Iresidual` row already uses ([`GateSpec::Mask`])". Fix: the `Mask` mechanism is the one the `Bus_Int_Duration` row uses; the `Iresidual` row uses `GateSpec::ColAbove`.
- `G|G2.2d|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - `lane.rs`, the `RELAY_SAMPLE_TRACE_IGNORES_DEBUGTRACE` filter (`!l.contains(", Element=Debug Sample: Relay.")`): it drops EVERY relay Debug Sample line from the oracle capture, including the nine DebugTrace-guarded sibling traces that are no divergence, so on a gated deck with `debugtrace=yes` a missing guarded line in the port passes silently. Fix: drop only lines whose Action starts with `FPRESENTSTATE:` (r4133 `Relay.pas:1325`, the unguarded call); extend the existing unit test (the one with `Debug Sample: Relay.r1` / `Recloser.rc1`) with a DebugTrace-guarded sibling action (`Relay.pas:1822..2007` shapes) asserting it SURVIVES. Confirm the ten call sites' action prefixes against r4133 `Relay.pas` first; if an unguarded call uses another prefix, list it explicitly rather than widening to a substring.
- `G|G2.2d|AT1|AT-3` (note) - `lane.rs`, the same exclusion's doc "no other gated deck names the property at all": four gated cases spell `debugtrace=no` on a relay (`Test/DistanceRelayTest.DSS`, `Test/ReverseDistanceRelayTest.DSS`, the two `Examples/DistanceRelays` copies, evlog=0). Fix: re-grep the vendored corpus, then state the operative claim - no gated deck turns it ON - and name the four.
- `G|G2.4|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - `lane.rs`, `assert_monitor_pad_is_live` / `expected_monitor_channel`: `MONITOR_PAD_MISSES` is incremented by no test and there is no injectable rule fn, so a mis-wired `else` arm leaves the only fail-on-stale guard of a corpus-wide un-ledgered exclusion dead. Fix: extract `check_monitor_pad(hits, misses) -> Result<(), String>` and test both verdicts (`misses > 0` errs; `hits > 0 && misses == 0` passes), mirroring `check_seq_arm_population` / `check_control_census`.
- `G|G2.4|SA1|SA-3` (minor) - same rail, settlement view: the miss branch and the wrapper's unflushed-non-placeholder path are unproven. Fix: closed together with AT-1 - route the transform through a core over injected counters (`expected_monitor_channel_with(&hits, &misses, ..)`, the `check_bus_reliability_compare_ran` precedent); unit test: an unflushed non-placeholder capture charges a MISS (not a hit) and returns the capture unchanged. The global statics stay untouched by tests.
- `G|G2.4|SA1|SA-4` (note) - `lane.rs`, `expected_monitor_channel` doc "`flushed_records` ... is what makes this transform safe rather than circular": inverted. Fix: `flushed_records` is the engine-under-test's own state and only scopes WHEN the rewrite may fire; what bounds the rewrite is the literal one-sample `[0.0]` shape check (`is_monitor_pad`). Document the residual silent-pass window (port lost flush + the oracle's single genuine 0.0 sample) and why it is narrow (any other channel of that monitor reds).
- `RP|RP2.1|SA1|SA-1` (minor) - `lane.rs`, `assert_reround_cells_are_live`: same untestable-guard weakness, recorded only in an R4133_PROPS phase record. Fix: give it an injectable core (`check_reround_cells(&[(visits, hits)]) -> Result`, as `props_norm.rs::check_rows_are_live`) plus a canary test pinning both verdicts (visited-but-never-hit errs; unvisited and hit pass); §4 note closing the "recorded, not fixed" item in `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp2.md`.
**Acceptance:**
- `newton_powers_are_the_only_element_channel_exclusion` covers all 13 fields by exhaustive destructure; the monitor-pad core tests (both verdicts + miss-charging), the reround canary and the extended relay-trace test exist and pass in both lanes.
- The narrowed relay filter leaves the corpus gate green in both lanes with no new ledger entry; if a gated deck reds, that is a real finding - record and report it, do not re-widen (R8).
- `#[cfg(feature = "oracle-parity")]` placement rules stay satisfied (`oracle_parity_cfg_gate`); no golden byte or tolerance moves.
- Note left for `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp2.md`; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-08 — Make the 52902-abort reliability pin discriminate wrote-zeros from wrote-nothing
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-08", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 1} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/reliability_pins.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** -
**Findings**
- `G|G1.6(ii)|AT1|AT1-1` (minor) - `reliability_pins.rs`, test `bus_reliability_survives_the_52902_abort` (its doc block and the headline assert message "the aborted calc must write nothing at all"). The stated mechanism is not the port's: `solution/meters/reliability.rs` writes the zone head bus (`bus_num_interrupt`, `bus_cust_interrupts`, `bus_int_duration`) and stamps `bus_section_id` on every zone bus BEFORE it returns the 52902 `Err`, loop-for-loop as r4133 `Meters/EnergyMeter.pas:2484-2496`; the cells read 0 only because `source_num_interruptions` / `source_int_duration` default to 0.0 (`elements/meter/energymeter/mod.rs`; r4133 `EnergyMeter.pas:950-951`, props #18/#19 at `:651-652`) and no flagged deck sets them - so hoisting the abort above the writes stays green. Fix: add a variant (same test file, inline deck or the existing 52902-aborting deck plus an `edit energymeter.<m> SourceNumInterruptions=.. SourceIntDuration=..`) with both source values non-zero, and assert the head bus carries the pre-abort writes (Num_Interrupt, Cust_Interrupts = source x total customers, Int_Duration, and the SectionID stamps) with literals measured on r4133; rewrite the doc and the assert message from "writes nothing" to "writes its source values before the abort; they read zero here only because the sources default to 0". Do not change the engine.
**Parts:**
1. Probe only, no commit. Locate the 52902-aborting deck the pin uses; on the r4133 DLL (epri-worker) and on the port run it with non-zero EnergyMeter props #18/#19, RelCalc, and read the bus reliability columns of the zone head bus and at least one other zone bus. Record deck text, commands, the measured rows of both engines and the chosen assertion design in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D09-08.md`.
2. Implement the discriminating pin plus the doc/message rewrite from the state file, mutation-check it (temporarily hoist the `return Err` above the head-bus writes in a scratch build: the new pin must red, then restore), run the gate, commit.
**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 - the bus reliability row (Num_Interrupt, Cust_Interrupts, Int_Duration, SectionID, plus Cust_Duration as a control) of the head bus after an aborted RelCalc with non-zero source values. Decides the literal expected row. If r4133 shows the head bus still at zero (i.e. the abort precedes the writes there), stop: that contradicts the cited source lines - record the transcript and report to the coordinator instead of pinning the port's row. dss-python may be read as a second witness where the deck compiles on capi 0.14.5; it is not the authority.
**Acceptance:**
- A pin in `reliability_pins.rs` fails when the 52902 abort is hoisted above the head-bus writes (mutation result stated in the record).
- Expected literals are r4133-measured; the probe recipe is in the test doc (R5-style one-liner), no transcript committed.
- `bus_reliability_survives_the_52902_abort` doc and assert message no longer claim "writes nothing".
- No engine file, golden byte or ledger entry moves; the uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-10 — Neutralise the registry READ leg (DataPath/LastFile) in the epri bridge and restate RegistryUpdate=No on clear
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-10", "effort": "max", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": [], "n_uids": 2} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/max; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** -.
**Files:** `crates/dss-epri/src/dss.rs`, `crates/dss-epri/tests/protocol.rs`, `tools/opendss/README.md`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`
**Findings**
- `G|bridge D13|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - `dss.rs`, `Engine::new` init sequence (the `Set RegistryUpdate=No` / `Set DefaultBaseFrequency=60` block and its D13 doc). Only `BaseFrequency` of the machine-wide `HKCU\Software\OpenDSS\MainSect` channel is neutralised; r4133 `ReadDSS_Registry` (V8 `Common/DSSGlobals.pas:1006-1009`) also loads `LastFileCompiled` and `TestDataDirectory` -> `SetDataPath` -> `ChDir` (`:934-972`, `ChDir` at `:951`), so a fresh worker's data directory is whatever another worktree's worker last wrote (measured: lane-e's vendored `13Bus/` dir, which `CorpusGuard` does not sweep), and because the write is frozen the stale value stays. Fix: right after the strict `Set RegistryUpdate=No`, issue a strict `Set DataPath=<worker-owned dir>` (choice of dir and the LastFile handling come from part 1); stop blindly swallowing the errno at the init site the finding names (poll it, tolerate only 907, record it); add a `protocol.rs` pin that a fresh worker's `Get DataPath` is the worker's own dir, not the registry's; align the README bullet "No process-global state escapes the worker" and leave the matching `TESTING.md` D13 rewording ("neutralizes") as a §4 note. The test must never write the user's real HKCU key.
- `G|bridge D13|AC1|AC-5` (note) - `dss.rs`, `Engine::clear`. It re-asserts `Set ShowReports=No` / `Set ShowExport=No` per case but not `Set RegistryUpdate=No`, although option 102 is served with a circuit active (r4133 V8 `Executive/ExecOptions.pas:864`, besides `:574` in `DoSetCmd_NoCircuit`); a deck saying `Set RegistryUpdate=Yes` reopens the registry write for the rest of the process (probed). Fix: add a strict `Set RegistryUpdate=No` to `Engine::clear` beside the report switches, update `clear`'s doc, and extend `clear_re_asserts_the_report_switches` (or a sibling) to cover it. `Get RegistryUpdate` cannot observe the flag (the `dss.rs` D13 doc says so), so the pin must be structural - e.g. assert on the command sequence `clear` issues (a command log, or a const list of the per-case re-assertions that both `clear` and the test consume) - not a registry write.
**Parts:**
1. Probe and design, no commit. Trace the r4133 registry reads at DLL load (`DSSGlobals.pas` `initialization` `:2092`, `ReadDSS_Registry`, `SetDataPath`); on a fresh epri-worker measure `Get DataPath` before any command, after `Set DataPath=<dir>`, after `clear`, and after a `Compile` (which re-points DataPath); check what `LastFile` steers, if anything observable. Choose the worker-owned directory (must exist, be writable, be per-worker, leave no trace in the vendored corpus) and whether it must be restated per `clear`. Write measurements and the decision to `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D09-10.md`.
2. Implement in `dss.rs` (init + `Engine::clear` incl. `RegistryUpdate=No`), add the `protocol.rs` pins, README wording, the `TESTING.md` note, gate, commit. Hand over to RF-D09-11 through the state file: the final shape of `Engine::clear` and any command-log / const-list mechanism introduced.
**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 only. (a) fresh worker `Get DataPath` vs the HKCU `DataPath` value (read-only registry query) - confirms the inbound leg; (b) after `Set DataPath=<dir>`: `Get DataPath`, and whether `clear` / `new circuit` / `Compile` move it - decides init-only vs per-clear restatement; (c) whether `Set DataPath` on a circuit-less engine raises an errno - decides placement relative to the throwaway circuit. Never write HKCU; if a measurement would need it, skip it and record why.
**Acceptance:**
- `protocol.rs`: a pin that a fresh worker's DataPath is the worker-owned dir, and a structural pin that `Engine::clear` restates `RegistryUpdate=No`; both fail when the respective command is removed.
- The init errno is polled, not swallowed; only 907 is tolerated and that is documented at the site.
- No test or probe writes `HKCU\Software\OpenDSS`; the corpus tree stays clean after the `dss-epri` tests (`git status` clean).
- Corpus gate green in both lanes with no r4133 capture moved (a moved capture is a stop-and-report, R8); every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-11 — dss.rs: reset Editor/AllowForms on clear, route pre-table accessors through check_callable, strict counts, fix read_mode docs and a vacuous protocol test
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-11", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D01-15", "RF-D09-10", "RF-D10-04"], "n_uids": 6} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D01-15, RF-D09-10, RF-D10-04.
**Files:** `crates/dss-epri/src/dss.rs`, `crates/dss-epri/tests/protocol.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`

Every count and every "at HEAD" claim below is re-measured at lane HEAD after those landings.
**Findings**
- `G|G1.10a F0-prime|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - `dss.rs`, `Engine::clear` and the D39 layer docs on `Engine::new`. Only 2 of the 3 D39 layers are re-asserted per case: `Set Editor=...` (option 15, r4133 `ExecOptions.pas:570/:722`) and `Set AllowForms=No` (option 149, `:639/:1118`) are deck-settable, survive `clear` (`Executive.pas:234-276`), and a re-armed AllowForms hangs the headless worker on the first modal `DoSimpleMsg` (`DSSGlobals.pas:615/:651/:676`). Fix: re-assert in `Engine::clear` the same no-op editor `Engine::new` installs plus `Set AllowForms=No` (confirm from `ExecOptions.pas` whether each is served without a circuit, else place after the throwaway circuit); move the layer-1/3 docs to the per-case lifetime wording layer 2 already has; add a `protocol.rs` pin: set a different Editor, clear, `Get Editor` shows the reset value. For AllowForms do NOT set `Yes` in a test unless the probe shows no modal can fire before the clear - otherwise pin it structurally through the mechanism RF-D09-10 introduced. Leave the `TESTING.md` D39 wording change as a §4 note.
- `G|G1.0|AC3|AC3-1` (minor) - `dss.rs`, `read_mode` doc (soundness paragraph "every table row is a getter (`modes::EXCLUDED_WRITE_MODES` records the two arms that are not)"). False: `EXCLUDED_WRITE_MODES` holds five arms (pinned in `modes.rs`) and the table contains one state-writing row, `METERS_SET_ACTIVE_SECTION` (`MetersI(22)`, r4133 `DDLL/DMeters.pas:254-263`), which the acceptance walk in `tests/modes.rs` drives with the neutral argument. Rewrite the paragraph: the exclusion arms re-counted at lane HEAD (five at `ab9f0f76`; RF-D01-15 registered `Circuit.Capacity` on `DO_NOT_CALL`, so check whether `EXCLUDED_WRITE_MODES` also moved), the one named exception (point at its `modes.rs` doc), and reconcile `probe_mode`'s "Point this at getter modes only" contract with it. Doc only.
- `G|G1.0|SA1|SA-2` (minor) - `dss.rs`, `ffi_dispatch` doc ("This is the crate's **single chokepoint** ... the DLL is never touched, whatever the caller"). False: the pre-table private helpers `call_v` / `v_f64s` / `v_i32s` / `v_strings` / `call_s` / `call_f` (27 call sites at `ab9f0f76`, e.g. `element_currents`) reach the DLL without `modes::check_callable`. This is the RESIDUE after RF-D01-15, which already moved `bus_kvbase` (`call_f`) onto the mode-table rail: re-count the bypass sites at lane HEAD first and work on that list. Preferred fix: thread a family tag (plus mode) into those helpers and consult `check_callable` there, with a unit test that a DO_NOT_CALL triple (Solution V:2 or Bus V:17) is refused through a helper without touching the DLL. Minimum, if the threading does not fit the budget: correct the doc to name the bypass and why it is latent (no direct call reaches either triple), and record the remainder.
- `G|G1.3c|SA1|SA-2` (note) - `dss.rs`, the sequence-currents accessor doc ("`CplxSeqCurrents` runs `GetCurrents` into a scratch buffer ... via `CalcSeqCurrents` `DDLL/DCktElement.pas:56`"). `:56` is an `End;`; the `GetCurrents` calls are `:47` (1-phase positive-sequence arm) and `:66` (three-phase arm). Re-point the citation; verify against `.inputs/electricdss-code-r4133-trunk/Version8/Source/DDLL/DCktElement.pas`.
- `G|G1.3d(i)|AC2|AC2-1` (note) - `dss.rs`, `ckt_element_num_terminals` / `ckt_element_num_conductors` / `ckt_element_num_phases`. They decode with the loose `read_mode_i`, so the `CktElementI` unknown-mode sentinel `-1` enters the capture as data (it first falsifies the NodeOrder predicate `n_terms > 0` in `capture.rs`). Switch all three to `Engine::read_count`, which refuses a negative reply, as `ckt_element_num_controls` already does (if RF-D01-15's strict-reply work already converted any of them, that one is "closed by RF-D01-15 - verified"). Run the corpus gate: a negative reply surfacing anywhere is a finding in itself - record it, do not loosen.
- `G|G1.10a F0-prime|AT1|AT-1` (minor) - `protocol.rs`, `report_switches_survive_a_compile_and_gag_every_guarded_editor_site` (the `after_init == []` assert and its "measured, not assumed" comment) and helper `WorkerProc::spawn_in`; matching claim in the `dss.rs` init doc. The assert cannot fail: the r4133 DLL `ChDir`s to its DataDirectory at load (`DSSGlobals.pas:2092` -> `SetDataPath` `:934-972`, `ChDir` `:951`), so nothing init writes lands in the spawn CWD. Fix: the assertion relocation is CLOSED BY RF-D10-04 `G|bridge D13|AC1|AC-1` (landed before this step, after RF-D09-10): it snapshots the worker-owned `DataPath` (read via `Get DataPath`) around `Engine::new` and an `Engine::clear` replay, proves non-vacuity once and decides the `spawn_in` policy. Here: verify at lane HEAD that the assertion sweeps that directory and reds when a report switch is un-gagged (probe (c) below); record the uid as closed by RF-D10-04; the only edit left to this step is the matching "measured, not assumed" wording in the `dss.rs` init doc. Do not re-site the assertion or re-decide `spawn_in`.
**Probes:** epri-worker r4133. (a) `Set Editor=` / `Set AllowForms=` with and without an active circuit: errno and `Get` read-back - decides placement in `clear` and whether the pin can be value-based; (b) `Get DataPath` on a fresh worker after RF-D09-10 - decides which directory the no-trace assertion snapshots; (c) one guarded-editor command with the gag removed in a scratch build, to show the relocated assertion sees the write (mutation evidence, not committed).
**Acceptance:**
- `protocol.rs`: Editor reset pin; AllowForms reset pin (value-based or structural, reason stated); the no-trace assertion RF-D10-04 relocated is verified (not rewritten) to fail when a report switch is un-gagged, and the `dss.rs` init doc says so truthfully.
- The three count accessors use `read_count`; corpus gate green in both lanes with no capture moved and no ledger change.
- Either a helper-level `check_callable` refusal test exists, or the chokepoint doc is corrected and the remainder is recorded.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D09-13 — golden_reports.rs: assert the Iresidual excluded-cell count, non-zero I1, EXP_GEN_ prefix, a pf guard that can fail; fix comments
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-13", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D00-14", "RF-D03-01"], "n_uids": 8} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D00-14, RF-D03-01.

**Ordering:** RF-D03-01 (`G|G2.1c|AC1|AC-1`, the negative-rating SeqCurrents divergence: edits `report/export/seq_currents.rs`, `golden_reports.rs`, `ledger.json`) and RF-D00-14 (`RP|RP3.8|AT2|AT2-1`, the IndMach012 PF render pin and its `golden_reports.rs` twin) both landed before this step. For the three G2.1c bullets below re-derive EVERY number from the landed kernel and deck (the `6.104` / `4.069`, `24.4153 A` and `+/-2441.5 %` figures are the triage's readings at `ab9f0f76`); if RF-D03-01 changed the `rating > 0.0` guard or the test deck, rewrite the mutant sentence for the landed form.
**Findings** — reading rule: `golden_reports.rs` is 6.9k lines (~90k tokens); never read it whole. `rg -n '<test name>'` for each of the eight anchors (`export_seqcurrents_prints_zero_for_an_undefined_rating`, `export_seqcurrents_iresidual_sums_the_rows_own_terminal`, `export_storage_multifile_uses_the_storage_prefix`, `export_busreliability_multimeter`, `query_indmach012_pf_renders_the_live_power_factor`, `with_register_fixture`) and read the test body only; for the three G2.1c re-derivations read only the landed kernel function in `report/export/seq_currents.rs` and the test deck.
- `G|G2.1c|AC1|AC-3` (note) - `export_seqcurrents_prints_zero_for_an_undefined_rating`, the `Line.bad` half. Its claimed kill of a sign-blind rating rewrite needs `Line.bad`'s terminal-1 I1 to be non-zero, which nothing asserts (with I1 = 0 the mutant renders 0 and `(0.0, 0.0)` still passes); `Line.good`'s loading is asserted, `Line.zero` is robust (NaN / +Inf). Fix: read `Line.bad`'s I1 column from the same export row and assert it is > 1 A (about 24.4 A today).
- `G|G2.1c|AT1|AT-2` (note) - same test, the comment deriving the `|good_n/good_e - 1.5| < 1e-3` band. It cites `6.1` / `4.067`, the pre-G2.1c two-line deck's renders; the landed three-line deck renders `6.104` / `4.069` (I1 = 24.4153 A, row format `%8.4g`, `.inputs/dss_capi/src/Common/ExportResults.pas:428`). Update the comment to the landed numbers, keeping the 1e-3 band and its ~1.5e-4 four-significant-digit rounding bound. No tolerance change.
- `G|G2.1f|AT1|AT-4` (note) - finding filed against `exec/report.rs` (the `EXP_GEN_` / `EXP_PV_` multi-file prefixes), fixed here. The one-prefix-per-class rule the G2.1f teardown argues from is pinned only for `EXP_MTR_` and `EXP_STORAGE_`; `EXP_PV_` became gated by G1.10b (`run_file_contents` compares `exp_pv_pv.csv`), `EXP_GEN_` is asserted nowhere. Fix: beside `export_storage_multifile_uses_the_storage_prefix` add a pin via `with_register_fixture` that `export generators /m` writes `EXP_GEN_<NAME>.csv` (and, if the fixture has or cheaply gains a PVSystem, `export pvsystem /m` -> `EXP_PV_<NAME>.csv`), including the negative half (no sibling-prefixed file appears).
- `G|G2.2a|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - `export_seqcurrents_iresidual_sums_the_rows_own_terminal`. Rows are dropped by silent `continue`s and the only totals are `residual.len() > 50` and `checked > 50`, five rows of slack below the measured 55, so excluded cells can become un-pinned while green. Fix: turn each `continue` into a panic naming the row (every seq row must resolve in the `Export Currents` map and parse), assert `checked` equals the number of seq rows, and count the `Terminal >= 2` rows actually pinned and assert it equals 20 - the cells the golden compare's `ColAbove` exclusion removes (audit replay: 55 rows / 20 excluded / 20 pinned). Derive 55 from the parsed golden, keep 20 as the literal.
- `G|G2.2a|AC1|AC-3` (note) - same pin's doc ("oracle-anchored by that report's own byte golden"). Overstated: `export_currents_matches_oracle` compares `Iresid_j` at the policy default abs 1e-8 (its `ang_tol` covers angle columns only), sound for terminal-1 magnitudes (~2.8e-5) but ~1000x looser than the replaced `Terminal >= 2` cells (~1e-11). Reword: for those cells the pin is a port-internal cross-path identity (`seq_currents.rs` vs `report/export/currents.rs`) plus a < 1e-8 magnitude bound. No tolerance change.
- `G|G2.2a|AT1|AT-1` (note) - the G2.2a LANE-EXCLUSION comment on `export_busreliability_multimeter` ("the oracle capture, which both gating engines produce"). Report-text goldens come from the pinned dss-python alone (`tools/golden/gen_reports.py`); no r4133 channel observes this report text. Reword like the sibling seqcurrents comment: the capture is dss-python's and carries the bug both oracles share in source (r4133 `Meters/EnergyMeter.pas:2567-2574`).
- `RP|RP3.8|AC2|AC2-2` (minor) - `query_indmach012_pf_renders_the_live_power_factor`. Both legs (`fmt_g(v, 6) == "0.908343"`, `rendered == float_to_str_ex(v)`) are round-trip identities on the parsed render, so an engine emitting r4133's `%.6g` passes. Fix: CLOSED BY RF-D00-14 `RP|RP3.8|AT2|AT2-1` (landed before this step), whose bullet covers "the PF render pin and its twin in `golden_reports.rs` ... compare against `float_to_str_ex` of the live f64 power factor (state variable #21) in both files". Here: verify the `golden_reports.rs` leg exists at lane HEAD and reds under a scratch `fmt_g(pf, 6)` render (mutation result in the record); add the live-f64 leg ONLY if RF-D00-14 fixed the `ind_mach012/tests.rs` twin alone, and record the uid as a duplicate closed by RF-D00-14 otherwise.
- `G|G2.1c|AC1|AC-2` (note) - same undefined-rating test, the mutant-attribution sentence ("a sign-blind rewrite such as `rating.abs()`, which would report its -1 A rating as a loading of +1 % ..."). Arithmetically impossible and conflates two mutants of `report/export/seq_currents.rs` (`if rating > 0.0 { i1 / rating * 100.0 } else { 0.0 }`): a guard-level `rating.abs() > 0.0` renders `Line.bad` at about -2441.5 % (I1 = 24.4153 A over -1 A), a rebinding `rating.abs()` at about +2441.5 %; either reds the `(0, 0)` assert, and `Line.zero` still passes because `abs(0)` is not `> 0`. Rewrite the sentence so; the identical wording in `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` goes out as a §4 note with the exact replacement text; any `investigations/` copy is updated locally per R1.
**Acceptance:**
- The Iresidual pin asserts exact coverage (`checked` == seq rows, 20 `Terminal >= 2` cells pinned) and has no silent `continue`.
- `query_indmach012_pf_renders_the_live_power_factor` fails under a `%.6g` render (verified on RF-D00-14's leg, mutation result in the record); a new `EXP_GEN_` prefix pin exists; `Line.bad` I1 > 1 A is asserted; every G2.1c number in the comments matches the landed kernel and deck.

### RF-D09-14 — SwtControl tests: re-pin micro-deck currents on DLL f64, add New-path quoted-state pin, fix vacuous recalc test and stale docs
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D09-14", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D01-01", "RF-D01-02", "RF-D01-09", "RF-D03-04"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D01-01, RF-D01-02, RF-D01-09, RF-D03-04.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/src/elements/control/swt_control/tests.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** -
**Ordering:** four earlier steps edit this same test file and landed before this one - RF-D01-01 (`RP|RP3.7|AC3|AC3-1`, R3: the per-phase parse keeps the 6th token in both lanes and every "dropped 6th token" pin became an expected-value pin that it lands), RF-D01-02 (pin provenance re-pointing / renames), RF-D01-09 (JSON-import per-phase pin) and RF-D03-04 (real JSON-import seam pin). Re-locate every pin named below by its landed name at lane HEAD (AT2 / AT5 / AT7 name pins that RF-D01-01 / RF-D01-02 may have renamed or reshaped).
**Findings**
- `RP|RP3.7|AC1|AC1-5` (note) - the locked-action test containing `sw.side_effects(prop::ACTION, 0); // early-return on locked`. `side_effects` (`swt_control/accessors.rs`) has had no lock branch since RP3.7 (a2); the asserts hold because the interpreter refused the write, leaving `present_state` unchanged. Replace the comment with that real reason.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT1|AT1` (minor) - `per_phase_open_zeros_only_its_phase_currents_on_a_micro_deck` and `per_phase_state_write_through_the_executive_opens_only_its_phase`. The expected magnitudes `13.900155478555806` / `13.886231627361722` are |x| of a 6-decimal PRINT, not the DLL's f64, although the doc says "at full precision"; the whole recorded 3.66e-7 / 2.75e-7 "gap" is that print error and eats 37 % / 27 % of the abs 1e-6 band. Fix: re-probe (below), replace both constants with the DLL f64 magnitudes (audit measured 13.900155112381297 / 13.886231352883541 - re-measure, do not copy), re-measure the port's delta, tighten the band towards the tight tier of `tests/TOLERANCE_NOTES.md` (never loosen; keep a stated headroom over the new measured delta), and rewrite the "MEASURED on this tree" and "at full precision" comments with the probe recipe.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT1|AT2` (note) - `interpreter_lock_guard_follows_the_property_name` comment ("the observable property seam keeps the scalar-era all-three refusal until A2 re-points it (the `locked_ignores_*` pins below)") and the module doc ("RP3.7 A1 pin set"). Stale since landing: A1 and A2 landed together, a locked `normal=` applies (`locked_normal_applies_locked_state_and_action_do_not`), and only `locked_ignores_action_write` remains. Rewrite the comment to the shipped state and retitle the module doc to cover A1 + A2.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT1|AT4` (note) - `edit_prop` doc cites `exec/command.rs:1428` for the executive's WasQuoted capture; the line has drifted. Replace with a symbol citation: the `param_was_quoted = parser.is_quoted()` capture in the `exec/command.rs` edit loop (two sites), no line number.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT1|AT5` (note) - coverage gap: the quoted per-phase write is exercised only through `Edit`, never through `New`, the spelling plan §RP3.7(a) names; safe today only because `do_new_cmd` -> `add_object` -> `edit_active` shares the loop that captures `param_was_quoted`. Fix: add an executive-level pin that `new swtcontrol.sw1 switchedobj=line.l1 switchedterm=1 state=(open, closed, closed)` renders `[open, closed, closed, ]`, and the same for `normal=(...)`, on the micro-deck the neighbouring executive pins use; confirm both renders on r4133 (below).
- `RP|RP3.7|AT1|AT7` (note) - `the_property_seam_caps_the_per_phase_parse_at_five_tokens` ("slot 6 still rendered"). The literal `[open, open, open, open, open, closed, ]` reads as an r4133 byte but r4133's slot 6 there is uninitialised heap (`Create` allocates `FNPhases` = 3 entries, `Controls/SwtControl.pas:299-305`; measured flipping between runs). Since RF-D01-01 (R3) the port no longer drops the 6th token and no test asserts a dropped one, so this pin exists at HEAD only in the form RF-D01-01 left for the 6-token parse. Fix on THAT pin: make its doc say that r4133 drops the 6th token (`i < SWTCONTROLMAXDIM`, `SwtControl.pas:461`) and renders uninitialised heap in slot 6 (`Create` allocates `FNPhases` = 3 entries, `SwtControl.pas:299-305`; measured flipping between runs), while the port keeps all NPhases tokens per R3 with the divergence recorded by RF-D01-01 (DIVERGENCES note) — never re-assert a dropped or heap-valued 6th slot, and never restore the `[open, open, open, open, open, closed, ]` literal as an r4133 byte.
- `RP|RP3.7|AT1|AT8` (note) - `recalc_redrives_after_a_phase_count_change`. It ends with `apply_ref_actions(&mut sw, &mut ms)` after `sw.take_ref_actions()` already drained the queue, so the `MockSwitch` is never written nor asserted. Fix: apply the already-taken `actions` to `ms` (or use `apply_ref_actions` instead of the manual take and assert on what it applied), assert the one-conductor open landed on `ms` and the other conductors did not move, drop the dead trailing call.
**Probes:** epri-worker r4133 on the pins' own micro-decks. (a) `read('element_currents')` on `Line.swk` after `edit swtcontrol.sw1 state=(open, closed, closed)` + solve, and the same for the second pin's executive spelling - yields the f64 components whose magnitudes become the constants, and decides how far the band can tighten; (b) `new swtcontrol.sw1 ... state=(open, closed, closed)` and `normal=(open, closed, closed)` property read-back - confirms the two literals of the New-path pin. If the port's delta against the f64 values exceeds the tight-tier floor, that is a port finding: stop that bullet, record, report (R8) - do not widen.
**Acceptance:**
- Both micro-deck pins carry DLL-f64 constants with the probe recipe in the doc, and a band no looser than before (tighter, with the measured delta stated).
- A New-path pin for quoted `state=(...)` and `normal=(...)` exists and fails if the New path stops capturing `param_was_quoted`.
- `recalc_redrives_after_a_phase_count_change` asserts on the `MockSwitch`.
- Only `swt_control/tests.rs` changes; no golden byte or ledger entry moves; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D10-01 — Harden the manifest.rs census guards and fix its stale docs
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D10-01", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": false, "after": ["RF-D07-09"], "n_uids": 7} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D07-09.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/manifest.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` (leave notes per §2.1; do not edit them here).
**Findings**
- `G|G1.0|AT3|AT3-2` (minor) - `manifest.rs`, `an_unwired_g1_surface_flag_on_a_case_is_refused` + `assert_no_unwired_g1_flag`: the test loops over the LIVE `G1Flag` table and branches on each row's `wired`, so once Step 0 (§1) lands G1.10c and flips `compare_di` no row reaches `expect_err` and the non-vacuity proof asserts nothing. Fix: split `assert_no_unwired_g1_flag` into a thin wrapper plus a core taking the row slice (`&[G1Flag]`), and drive the test with a FORGED row set - every real row cloned with `wired: false` must `expect_err` naming the flag and its sub-step, the same row with `wired: true` must be `Ok` (precedent: the forged-row test in `crates/dss-epri/tests/modes.rs`). Note for `TESTING.md`: its sentence naming this test as the non-vacuity driver now describes a forged table.
- `G|G1.3c|AC1|AC3` (note) - `manifest.rs`, field doc of `compare_derived`: says the flag carries "all **thirteen** fields" while it enumerates ten (`Enabled` + 3 polar + 3 seq + 2 `CplxSeq*` + `TotalPowers`); thirteen is the `ElemChannels` bool count (harness), four of which belong to the always-on capture / `compare_element_extras`. Fix: reword to "all ten derived fields" and say what thirteen counts. Notes: `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` ("closing the flag at thirteen fields") and the two `TESTING.md` places that repeat "thirteen" for this flag; the `TESTING.md` uses that mean the `ElemChannels` count are correct and stay.
- `G|G1.2|AC1|AC-1` (minor) - `manifest.rs`, `CONTROLS_REQUIRED`: `espvlcontrol/espvlcontrol.dss` (the only live ESPVLControl coverage) is absent from the pinned deck floor; a scripted diff of `tests/corpus/controls/manifest.json` (107 paths) against the constant (98) finds 9 unpinned decks. Fix: add `espvlcontrol/espvlcontrol.dss`; re-run the diff at HEAD and pin the other eight too (`fuse/fuse_curvemult_blow`, `fuse/fuse_legacy_noblow`, `indmach_r4133/indmach_snap`, `indmach_r4133/indmach_dyn`, `energymeter/midi_relcalc`, `capcontrol/capcontrol_follow_noshape`, `regcontrol/regcontrol_idle`, `upfc/upfc_dual_daily` - take the exact paths from the manifest) unless a reason not to is recorded in the constant's doc. Add a test asserting the controls manifest path set equals `CONTROLS_REQUIRED` (or equals it plus a named, documented exemption list) so the drift cannot recur. Write into the constant's doc and into `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D10-01.md` that from now on every step registering a new deck in `tests/corpus/controls/manifest.json` appends it to `CONTROLS_REQUIRED` in the same commit (the later RF-D01-18 `controls/relay/relay_debugtrace.dss` is the first such case).
- `G|G1.4a|AT2|AT2-1` (minor) - `manifest.rs`, `deck_instantiates_a_gictransformer` + `collect_gictransformer_decks`: the closed-set census accepts only the bare `New GICTransformer...` spelling and walks only `*.dss`, while r4133 accepts `New object=GICTransformer.x` (`Executive/ExecHelper.pas:184-238`, `GetObjClassAndName` / `DoNewCmd`) and 160 `.txt`/`.DSV` corpus files are Redirect targets. Fix: strip an optional `object` `=` prefix (with or without spaces, quoted or not) before the class test; make the collector walk every file, as `props_r4133_replay::collect_files` does; extend `the_gictransformer_channel_guard_refuses_a_capi_gated_deck` with `New object=GICTransformer.x` and `new object = "gictransformer.x"` rows and add a tempdir drive of the collector with a `.txt` redirect target. `GICTRANSFORMER_DECKS` must not change (the census is accurate today); if the wider walk finds a new member, stop and record it.
- `G|G1.4b|AT1|AT-3` (note) - `manifest.rs`, `no_r4133_gated_case_reduces_by_merging` (and its precedent `no_capi_gated_case_instantiates_a_gictransformer`): both test only cases whose OWN path is a set member, so a member reached through `Redirect`/`Compile` from a wrong-channel master is invisible, while the doc presents the census as covering that class. Fix: add a reachability rail - for every case on the forbidden channel follow `Redirect`/`Compile` targets transitively (cycle-safe, paths resolved relative to the including file) and assert none resolves to a `REDUCE_MERGE_DECKS` / `GICTRANSFORMER_DECKS` member; add an offline tempdir fixture that reds on a one-hop `Redirect`. The walker accepts every spelling r4133 `FindAbbrev` resolves to Redirect or Compile (shortest unambiguous prefix from `ExecCommands.pas`, the same derivation SA-3 below makes for `reduce` - a `redir foo.txt` must not escape it; add an abbreviated row to the fixture); RF-D07-09 (`RP|RP3.3|SA1|SA-2`, in **After**) fixed the identical hole in `props_r4133_replay.rs::redirect_targets` - mirror that function (or share it if it is reachable from the corpus_gate test crate), do not write a third verb matcher. Doc updated to say exactly what is covered.
- `G|G1.4b|SA1|SA-3` (minor) - `manifest.rs`, `deck_reduces_by_merging` + the `REDUCE_MERGE_DECKS` doc: only the full `reduce` token and `reduceoption=` are matched although r4133 dispatches abbreviations (`Executive/Command.pas:53` `AbbrevAllowed := True`, `:88` `CommandList.FindAbbrev`); and the constant's doc describes a per-case deck scan the test does not perform. ### RF-D10-02 — Make the props census run-id/short-run guard real and repair its citations
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D10-02", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 2, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D09-04", "RF-D09-05"], "n_uids": 6} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D09-04, RF-D09-05.
**Files:** `crates/dss-core/tests/corpus_gate/props_census.rs`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp4.md`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md`.
**Findings**
- `RP|RP0.2|AC1|AC-4` (note) - `props_census.rs`, the artifact emit path (the writer of `tmp/props_census/run.json` and its siblings `props_census.json`, `claims_summary.json`, ...): provenance is stamped per file with no run identity, so two runs in one worktree leave a directory whose stamps contradict each other. Fix: mint one run id (timestamp + pid) per census run, stamp it into `run.json` and every sibling artifact, and add a unit pin that all artifacts written by one emit call carry the same id. Note for `TESTING.md` (census section): mismatched ids mean a mixed directory.
- `RP|RP1.4|AC1|AC-2` (minor) - `props_census.rs`, the `oracle_errors` / `rust_errors` counts (JSON twins + the `eprintln!` summary): the short-run guard is print-only and RP1.4's hand-off ("RP4.1's single-census acceptance must read that count") was never discharged - the RP4 record carries no error counts and the `TESTING.md` baseline (5 r4133 / 22 capi) is the 2026-08-23 measurement on 439 cases while the census walks 440. Fix: add an opt-in env knob whose PAYLOAD is the baseline — `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS_MAX_ERRORS=r4133:N,capi_v0145:M` (a sibling of the existing `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS`, documented in the module doc; a channel not named in the payload is not checked) — that makes the census FAIL when a channel's `oracle_errors` exceeds its declared value; no constant in code carries the dated numbers (they live in the rp4 record and the TESTING.md note, so nothing rots with the population), and a unit pin covers the payload parser plus the comparison (above → fails, at → passes, unset → today's behaviour); run one full census on the current population, record the per-channel counts in `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp4.md` as the discharged RP1.4 hand-off, and leave notes with the new dated baseline for `TESTING.md` and a one-line "discharged by RF-D10-02" for `r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md`. The full census is long and drives both oracles - it is part 2 of this step (below), handed over through the state file (§3).
- `RP|RP0.2|SA1|SA-3` (note) - `props_census.rs`, the doc block of finding (8)'s "fixed by demonstration" disposition (search `runner.rs:554`): four file:LINE citations (`runner.rs:554`, `ledger.rs:863`, `ledger.rs:1017`, `runner.rs:641`) are dead at HEAD and two omit the `corpus_gate/` segment. Ordering: RF-D09-04 (in **After**) rewrites this very doc block "to the truth" (RelCalc IS driven there) and turns the test into a structural pin, and RF-D09-05 (in **After**) also edits `props_census.rs` - verify at lane HEAD which file:LINE citations survived; if none did, record this uid as closed-by RF-D09-04. Fix for any survivor: replace it by a symbol, no line numbers - `harness::compare_probe` (the DEFINITION lives in `crates/dss-core/tests/harness/mod.rs`; `runner.rs:554` was only its call site inside `compare_capture`), the `compare_capture` call site and the ledger property rewrite (`dss.command("? <element>.<prop>")`) in `corpus_gate/runner.rs`, and the ledger probe/element handlers by name in `corpus_gate/ledger.rs`; never write `runner.rs::compare_probe`.
- `RP|RP2.1|SA1|SA-4` (note) - `props_census.rs`, two places (search `makeposseq-cuf-applied-capi-props` and "11 such hits"): the justification for keeping the ledger link live on the capi channel names one entry and an RP2.1-era hit count; at HEAD `tests/corpus/ledger.json` has 11 capi ids with `property` scopes. Fix: name the family (several property-scoped capi entries), and either drop "11 hits" or date it explicitly as the RP2.1 measurement.
- `RP|RP2.1|SA1|SA-5` (note) - `props_census.rs`, comment above the aggregation pin (search `gic-pct-r2-honoured-`): cites the two `gic-pct-r2-honoured-*-capi-props` ids deleted by coordinator decision D12. Fix: cite the live r4133 twins `gic-pct-r2-honoured-gictransformer-r4133-props` / `gic-pct-r2-honoured-midi-r4133-props`, or describe the shape without ids.
- `RP|RP2.3|AC1|AC1-4` (note) - `props_census.rs`, doc of `Disposition::Echo` and the chain test's comment ("the exclusion is per (class, prop) and not per case"): says "81 rows" (`props_norm::ECHO_ROWS` is 82) and omits `ECHO_NARROWED`. Fix: drop the literal count (refer to `props_norm::ECHO_ROWS`), name `ECHO_NARROWED` beside `ECHO_CARVE_OUTS`; soften the test comment to "per (class, prop), narrowed per cell by the two valves". Layer this on the `Disposition` shape RF-D09-01 `RP|RP2.1|AC3|AC3-1` (transitively in **After** via RF-D09-04) left - it rebuilds `Disposition::all()` from `NormRule::ALL_TAGS`; edit the doc text only.
**Parts:**
1. Code and citations, no census run: the run-id stamping + its unit pin (AC-4), the `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS_MAX_ERRORS=<channel>:<N>,...` knob + its parser/comparison unit pin (AC-2, the whole code half — part 2 edits no code), the four citation/doc fixes (SA-3, SA-4, SA-5, AC1-4). Gate, commit; hand over the knob's exact name/payload syntax and the exact census command in `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D10-02.md`.
2. The census run and its record (docs only): one full `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS` walk over the current population on BOTH channels (dss-python `capi_v0145` and epri-worker `r4133`), the per-channel `oracle_errors` / `rust_errors` counts written to `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp4.md` (dated, with the population size, and the ready-to-paste `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS_MAX_ERRORS=r4133:N,capi_v0145:M` line) as the discharged RP1.4 hand-off, the §4 notes for `TESTING.md` (new dated baseline + the same knob line in the census procedure) and `r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md` ("discharged by RF-D10-02"); re-run the census once with that payload set to show it passes at the baseline. Commit.
**Probes:** the props census itself (`DSS_PROPS_CENSUS=...`, procedure in `TESTING.md`) run once over all ~440 in-scope cases on both oracle channels in part 2; its per-channel error counts are the values recorded and used as the baseline - never typed from the 2026-08-23 figures (5 r4133 / 22 capi on 439 cases), which are the stale reading this uid corrects.
**Acceptance:**
- A unit pin proves one emit call stamps one run id into every artifact; a unit pin proves the `DSS_PROPS_CENSUS_MAX_ERRORS` payload parses and the census fails above / passes at the declared value; unset, the census behaves as today; no code constant carries the measured counts.
- `docs/phase-records/r4133-props-rp4.md` records the measured `oracle_errors` / `rust_errors` per channel with date and population size; notes left for `TESTING.md` and `r4133-props-rp0-rp1.md`.
- No ledger, golden or lock byte moves; `rg "runner.rs:554|ledger.rs:863|ledger.rs:1017|runner.rs:641" props_census.rs` is empty.
- Every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.

### RF-D10-04 — Make the bridge D13 registry/init-trace tests in protocol.rs able to red
<!-- RF-STEP {"step": "RF-D10-04", "effort": "xhigh", "parts": 1, "gate": "full", "oracle": true, "after": ["RF-D09-10", "RF-D10-03"], "n_uids": 4} -->
**Tier:** executor opus/xhigh; audits, settler per §3. **Gate:** full gate (§2.2). **After:** RF-D09-10, RF-D10-03.
**Files:** `crates/dss-epri/tests/protocol.rs`
**Doc notes (§4):** `TESTING.md`, `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`.
**Findings**
- `G|bridge D13|AT1|AT-2` (minor) - `protocol.rs`, `init_resets_the_default_base_frequency_to_sixty`, `the_worker_never_writes_the_opendss_registry_key`, `init_overrides_the_os_editor_and_never_writes_it_back`, `reg_restore_removes_a_value_the_machine_did_not_have`: unsynchronized read-modify-restore of the machine-global `HKCU\Software\OpenDSS\MainSect` values; libtest runs them concurrently and parallel lanes run the same binary, so an interleaving can leave `BaseFrequency` at 50 for good or green the init pin on a broken tree. Fix: one guard taken by every test that touches the key - an in-file `static Mutex` (poison-tolerant) PLUS a cross-process lock (a `create_new` lock file under `%TEMP%` with retry and a stale timeout, or a named mutex; std only unless unavoidable), released on drop after the restore. Note for `TESTING.md`: the "brief exposure" paragraph of the bridge section must describe the lock.
- `G|bridge D13|AC1|AC-1` (major) - `protocol.rs`, `report_switches_survive_a_compile_and_gag_every_guarded_editor_site`, the F0' assertion `assert_eq!(after_init, Vec::<String>::new(), ...)` fed by `dir_entries(&scratch)` after `spawn_in(scratch)`: it sweeps the spawn directory, but r4133 sets its data directory and `ChDir`s at DLL load (`Common/DSSGlobals.pas:1007-1009` `DataPath` registry read -> `SetDataPath`, `ChDir` at `:950`, default `GetDefaultDataDirectory + '\OpenDSS\'` at `:2085-2093`; run from `TExecutive.Create`, `Executive/Executive.pas:124`), so the claim "the throwaway `new circuit.dssrs_bridge_init` leaves no trace" is measured on a directory the engine never uses - the pin cannot red. Fix (this step runs AFTER RF-D09-10, which makes the engine's `DataPath` a worker-owned directory right after init - read `tmp/retro_fix/state/RF-D09-10.md` for the dir choice): measure the directory the engine really uses - read it via `Get DataPath` after `Engine::new` (the worker-owned dir; the registry `DataPath` / r4133 default `GetDefaultDataDirectory + '\OpenDSS\'` is only the pre-RF-D09-10 load-time value and is checked once, as a negative control, to still hold no `dssrs_bridge_init*` entry), snapshot it around `Engine::new` and around an `Engine::clear` replay, and assert no NEW `dssrs_bridge_init*` entry appeared (compare before/after sets; if the dir is shared, unrelated files are tolerated). Decide the `spawn_in` policy HERE and once: drop `spawn_in(scratch)` or keep it as a secondary sweep with a reworded message - state the reason in the test doc. Prove non-vacuity once (a throwaway init that does write, e.g. a `Save`, makes the assertion red), record the proof in the test doc and clean up whatever the proof wrote. This closes RF-D09-11 `G|G1.10a F0-prime|AT1|AT-1` (that step only verifies and fixes the `dss.rs` init-doc wording); say so in the record. Notes for `TESTING.md` and `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md` where they describe this pin.
- `G|bridge D13|AT1|AT-3` (minor) - `protocol.rs`, `reg_read` / `reg_restore`: `reg query` output is parsed with `split_whitespace` and the third token returned, so a `REG_SZ` with spaces (`Editor` can be a path with spaces) is truncated and the truncation is written back machine-wide; an empty value reads as `None`, so the restore deletes it. Fix: split the parser into a pure fn that takes everything after the `REG_SZ` token (trim leading whitespace only), returns `Some("")` for an empty value, and unit-test it on sample lines (`MS Sans Serif`, a path with spaces, an empty value, a missing value); `reg_restore` must rewrite an empty value rather than delete it. Do this first, so the restore under the new lock is already lossless.
- `G|bridge D13|AT1|AT-6` (note) - `protocol.rs`, doc of `init_resets_the_default_base_frequency_to_sixty` ("a bare probe session that only issues exec never does" / "without any clear"): stale since F0' - `Engine::new` itself issues a raw `clear` (`dss.rs`, `eng.command_strict("clear", "init")`), which does NOT reset `DefaultBaseFreq`. Fix: rewrite the argument - the pin isolates the `Set DefaultBaseFrequency=60` reset because the raw clear leaves 50 in place, not because no clear ran; keep the measured 50-vs-60 evidence.
**Probes:** epri-worker r4133: (1) `Get DataPath` on a fresh worker spawned in a scratch cwd, after RF-D09-10's init - confirms the worker-owned dir is what the no-trace assertion sweeps and that it is not the spawn dir; (2) the non-vacuity drive (an init variant that writes) - decides that the rewritten assertion can red; (3) raw `exec clear` -> `Get DefaultBaseFrequency` = 50 vs protocol `clear` = 60, re-measured for the AT-6 doc only if no existing test already shows it.
**Acceptance:**
- All registry-touching tests take the shared guard; `cargo test -p dss-epri --test protocol` is green under default parallelism and the `MainSect` values are identical before and after the run (executor checks `reg query` before/after, including a value with spaces).
- The pure `reg query` parser has unit tests for spaces, empty and missing values.
- The init-trace assertion sweeps the engine's real (worker-owned, post-RF-D09-10) data directory around init AND around a `clear` replay, the `spawn_in` decision is stated once, its non-vacuity proof is recorded in the test doc, and nothing is left behind in that directory.
- Notes left for `TESTING.md` and `docs/phase-records/golden-rebase.md`; every uid above is closed or recorded with a reason.
